# 六爻 · 疾病 · 医药

*Liu Yao (six-line coin divination) · Illness · Doctors and medicine: which doctor, which remedy, when to treat*

82 条。原文逐字核对过 `functions/_lib/doctrine/books/` 下的文本,行号是那份整理本的行号,不是原刻本的页码。「规则(整理)」是本库的归纳,引用时引原文。

## LY.ILL.BSZZ.12 子孙发动当勿药而自痊、父母交重纵延医而难治：子孙发动而不遇日月动变伤克，用药立效，喻之不药也能好

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙发动，应当不用吃药自己就好；父母爻交重（发动），即使请医生也难以治好。具体说，子孙发动、没有遇到日、月、动爻、变爻的伤克，如果占用药，应当许他立刻见效，所以用「不吃药也能好」来比喻。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Output moves, one recovers of oneself without medicine; if Resource moves, then even calling a doctor it is hard to cure. Specifically, if Output moves and meets no harm or control from the Day, Month, moving or changed lines, then if one divines about taking medicine one may promise that it will take effect at once, so it is put as 'it can be cured without medicine'.

**《卜筮正宗·新增痘疹》原文** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2251 行 · *Bushi Zhengzong (The Orthodox Line of Divination), "Added chapter on smallpox", base text; Bushi Zhengzong, line 2251*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙发动，当勿药而自痊； | 子孙发动，应当不用吃药自己就好； | If Output moves, one recovers of oneself without medicine; |
| 父母交重，纵延医而难治 | 父母爻交重，即使请医生也难以治好 | if Resource moves, then even calling a doctor it is hard to cure |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2251 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2251*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如子孙发动，不遇日月动变伤克者，倘卜用药，当许立效，故喻之不药而能愈也。 | 如果子孙发动，没有遇到日、月、动爻、变爻的伤克，如果占用药，应当许他立刻见效，所以用「不吃药也能好」来比喻。 | If Output moves and meets no harm or control from the Day, Month, moving or changed lines, then if one divines about taking medicine one may promise that it will take effect at once, so it is put as 'it can be cured without medicine'. |

> **按**:这一条对痘疹讲的「子孙发动」和增删卜易讲的「子孙持世发动服之立可解忧」（LY.ILL.ZS.37）、「子孙制鬼最喜旺相生扶」（LY.ILL.ZS.30）是同一个看法；「父母交重」难治，和增删卜易的「父爻持世妙药难调」（LY.ILL.ZS.32）也一致。「交重」就是发动。
>
> **Note:** What is said here of a moving Output line agrees with the Zengshan Buyi's 'Output on the Self position or moving, taking it gives immediate relief' (LY.ILL.ZS.37) and 'Output controlling Pressure most wants to be strong and supported' (LY.ILL.ZS.30); that a moving Resource line is hard to cure also agrees with the Zengshan Buyi's 'Resource holding Self: even wonderful medicine is hard to mix' (LY.ILL.ZS.32). 'Jiaozhong' simply means moving.

相关:LY.ILL.BSZZ.10、LY.ILL.BSZZ.13、LY.ILL.ZS.30、LY.ILL.ZS.32、LY.ILL.ZS.37

## LY.ILL.CA.08 医药章(一)：子孙出现是医人、生世生身药必灵；两个子孙齐发动换医困厄即时平；子囚医拙、子旺医明；无子病人难服药、子空患者便归程；子孙值土宜丸散、火艾火蒸、木咬咀、金针刺、水汤药；青龙独发最为良、持世生身便离床；子孙同位发不须服药；鬼财安贴无忧虑、身世比和大吉祥；何日瘥：鬼衰身旺世生方

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙出现就是医生，生世、生身，药一定灵验。两个子孙一起发动，换医生，困厄立刻平息。子孙被囚，医生笨拙，没有功效；子孙旺，医生高明，有准绳。没有子孙，病人难服药；子孙空亡，患者就要归去了。子孙值土，宜丸散；值火，要用艾火熏蒸；遇木，用咬咀才对症；逢金，用针刺才保平安；子孙值水，宜汤药；有天喜扶持，妙不可言。青龙独发，最好；持世生身，便可离床；再有子孙同位发动，不必服药，保安康。鬼、财安贴，没有忧虑；身、世比和，大吉祥。想问病人哪一天痊愈？鬼衰、身旺、世生的时候。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Output appearing is the doctor; generating the Self and the Body, the medicine is surely effective. Two Output lines moving together: change the doctor and the distress is settled at once. Output trapped: the doctor is clumsy and without effect; Output strong: the doctor is wise and has a standard. With no Output the patient can hardly take medicine; Output Void: the sufferer starts on his homeward road. Output on Earth: suit pills and powders; on Fire: it must be moxa fire steaming; meeting Wood: crushed herbs answer the illness; meeting Metal: the needle is guaranteed to bring health; Output on Water: suit decoctions; with Heavenly Joy helping, the wonder cannot be praised enough. The Azure Dragon moving alone is best; holding the Self and generating the Body, he leaves his bed at once; and if Output moves in the same place, no medicine need be taken and health is assured. With the ghost and Wealth quiet and settled there is no worry; the Body and Self harmonious is greatly auspicious. To ask on what day the sick person recovers: when the ghost is weak, the Body strong and the Self generated.

**《阐奥歌章·阐奥歌章·二十三、医药章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 4025 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Chan'ao Gezhang, chapter 23: medicine", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4025*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙出现是医人， | 子孙出现就是医生， | Output appearing is the doctor; |
| 生世生身药必灵。 | 生世、生身，药一定灵验。 | generating the Self and the Body, the medicine is surely effective. |
| 两个子孙齐发动， | 两个子孙一起发动， | Two Output lines moving together: |
| 换医困厄即时平。 | 换医生，困厄立刻平息。 | change the doctor and the distress is settled at once. |
| 子囚医拙无功效， | 子孙被囚，医生笨拙，没有功效； | Output trapped: the doctor is clumsy and without effect; |
| 子旺医明有准绳。 | 子孙旺，医生高明，有准绳。 | Output strong: the doctor is wise and has a standard. |
| 无子病人难服药， | 没有子孙，病人难服药； | With no Output the patient can hardly take medicine; |
| 子空患者便归程。 | 子孙空亡，患者就要归去了。 | Output Void: the sufferer starts on his homeward road. |
| 子孙值土宜丸散， | 子孙值土，宜丸散； | Output on Earth: suit pills and powders; |
| 值火须当艾火蒸。 | 值火，要用艾火熏蒸； | on Fire: it must be moxa fire steaming; |
| 遇木咬咀方应病， | 遇木，用咬咀才对症； | meeting Wood: crushed herbs answer the illness; |
| 逢金针刺保亨贞。 | 逢金，用针刺保平安。 | meeting Metal: the needle is guaranteed to bring health. |
| 子孙值水宜汤药， | 子孙值水，宜汤药； | Output on Water: suit decoctions; |
| 天喜扶持妙莫评。 | 有天喜扶持，妙不可言。 | with Heavenly Joy helping, the wonder cannot be praised enough. |
| 青龙独发最为良， | 青龙独发，最好； | The Azure Dragon moving alone is best; |
| 持世生身便离床。 | 持世生身，便可离床。 | holding the Self and generating the Body, he leaves his bed at once. |
| 那更子孙同位发， | 再有子孙同位发动， | and if Output moves in the same place, |
| 不须服药保安康。 | 不必服药，保安康。 | no medicine need be taken and health is assured. |
| 鬼财安贴无忧虑， | 鬼、财安贴，没有忧虑； | With the ghost and Wealth quiet and settled there is no worry; |
| 身世比和大吉祥。 | 身、世比和，大吉祥。 | the Body and Self harmonious is greatly auspicious. |
| 欲问病人何日瘥？ | 想问病人哪一天痊愈？ | To ask on what day the sick person recovers: |
| 鬼衰身旺世生方。 | 鬼衰、身旺、世生的时候。 | when the ghost is weak, the Body strong and the Self generated. |

> **按**:这是阐奥歌章「医药章」的第一首，和天玄赋的「医药」（LY.ILL.TXF.12、TXF.13）、易林补遗的「求医疗病」（LY.ILL.EL.26）、易隐的「医药」（LY.ILL.YY.21、YY.25）一样，都是「子孙为医药」的一套：子孙旺相、生世生身，药灵；两个子孙齐发，换医；无子孙、子孙空，难服药或不治。五行用药：土宜丸散、火宜艾灸、木宜咬咀、金宜针刺、水宜汤药——和天玄赋的「火鬼宜凉剂、水鬼宜温剂、木鬼宜针、金鬼宜灸、土鬼宜剉散」（LY.ILL.TXF.13）不同：那里按鬼的五行、这里按子孙的五行；这两个标准给出的用药并不一样（如木：天玄赋鬼属木宜针、这里子孙属木宜咬咀；金：天玄赋鬼属金宜灸、这里子孙属金宜针刺），书里没有说明二者的关系。「咬咀」是把药物切碎、入口嚼碎之类的做法（下面注释：「咀」音 ju，嚼）。「子空患者便归程」的「归程」是回去的路，指死。「鬼财安贴」的「贴」是安稳。
>
> **Note:** This is the first poem of the Chan'ao Gezhang's chapter on medicine, and like the Tianxuan Fu's 'medicine' (LY.ILL.TXF.12, TXF.13), the Yilin Buyi's 'seeking a doctor to treat illness' (LY.ILL.EL.26) and the Yi Yin's 'medicine' (LY.ILL.YY.21, YY.25) it is one set of 'Output is the medicine and doctor': Output strong and generating the Self and Body, the medicine is effective; two Output lines moving together, change the doctor; with no Output or Output Void, medicine can hardly be taken or there is no cure. The medicine by phase: Earth suits pills and powders, Fire moxa, Wood crushed herbs, Metal the needle, Water decoctions — which differs from the Tianxuan Fu's 'a Fire ghost suits cooling remedies, a Water ghost warming remedies, a Wood ghost the needle, a Metal ghost moxa, an Earth ghost cut-and-ground powders' (LY.ILL.TXF.13): there it goes by the phase of the ghost, here by the phase of Output, and the two criteria do not give the same medicine (for Wood: in the Tianxuan Fu a Wood ghost suits the needle, here Wood Output suits crushed herbs; for Metal: a Metal ghost suits moxa there, Metal Output the needle here), and the book does not say how the two relate. 'Yao ju' (crushed herbs) is cutting medicines fine and crushing them by chewing and the like (the note below has 'ju' as to chew). The 'gui cheng' of 'Output Void: the sufferer starts on his homeward road' is the road home, meaning death. The 'tie' of 'the ghost and Wealth quiet and settled' is settled and steady. 

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 794 行:子孙出现是医人;同文 第 794 行:生世生身药必灵;同文 第 794 行:两个子孙齐发动;同文 第 794 行:换医困厄即时平;同文 第 795 行:子囚医拙无功效;同文 第 795 行:子旺医明有准绳;同文 第 795 行:无子病人难服药;同文 第 795 行:子空患者便归程;同文 第 796 行:子孙值土宜丸散;异文(相似 0.86) 第 796 行:值金针刺保亨贞;同文 第 797 行:子孙值水宜汤药;同文 第 797 行:天喜扶持妙莫评;同文 第 797 行:青龙独发最为良;同文 第 797 行:持世生身便离床;同文 第 798 行:那更子孙同位发;同文 第 798 行:不须服药保安康;同文 第 798 行:鬼财安贴无忧虑;异文(相似 0.86) 第 798 行:身世比和大吉;同文 第 799 行:欲问病人何日瘥;异文(相似 0.86) 第 799 行:鬼衰生旺世生方
- 《易隐》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 2993 行:鬼衰身旺世生
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1365 行:子孙出现是医人;同文 第 1365 行:两个子孙齐发动;同文 第 1365 行:换医困厄即时平;同文 第 1365 行:子囚医拙无功效;同文 第 1365 行:子旺医明有准绳;同文 第 1365 行:无子病人难服药;同文 第 1365 行:子空患者便归程;同文 第 1365 行:子孙值土宜丸散;同文 第 1365 行:值火须当艾火蒸;同文 第 1365 行:逢金针刺保亨贞;同文 第 1365 行:子孙值水宜汤药;同文 第 1365 行:天喜扶持妙莫评;同文 第 1365 行:青龙独发最为良;同文 第 1365 行:持世生身便离床;同文 第 1365 行:那更子孙同位发;同文 第 1365 行:不须服药保安康;同文 第 1365 行:鬼财安贴无忧虑;同文 第 1365 行:身世比和大吉祥;同文 第 1367 行:欲问病人何日瘥;同文 第 1367 行:鬼衰身旺世生方

## LY.ILL.CA.09 医药章(二)：夫病欲医须卦静、爻须旺相子扶身；应爻及外为医者、内卦身爻是病人；医来克病人方愈、病若克医药不灵；鬼爻持世人难保、吉曜加临大利贞

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:丈夫的病要医，要卦静，爻旺相，子孙扶身。应爻和外卦是医生，内卦和身爻是病人。医克病，病人才好；病克医，药不灵。鬼爻持世，人难保；吉曜加临，大吉大利。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a man's illness is to be treated the hexagram must be still, the lines strong and Output supporting the body. The Other line and the outer trigram are the doctor; the inner trigram and the Body line are the sick person. If the doctor controls the illness the person is cured; if the illness controls the doctor the medicine is not effective. The Pressure line holding the Self, the person can hardly be kept; a lucky star added, greatly favourable.

**《阐奥歌章·阐奥歌章·二十三、医药章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 4028 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Chan'ao Gezhang, chapter 23: medicine", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4028*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫病欲医须卦静， | 病人的病要医，卦要安静， | When a man's illness is to be treated the hexagram must be still, |
| 爻须旺相子扶身。 | 爻要旺相，子孙扶身； | the lines strong and Output supporting the body; |
| 应爻及外为医者， | 应爻和外卦是医生， | the Other line and the outer trigram are the doctor; |
| 内卦身爻是病人。 | 内卦和身爻是病人。 | the inner trigram and the Body line are the sick person. |
| 医来克病人方愈， | 医生克病，病人才好； | If the doctor controls the illness the person is cured; |
| 病若克医药不灵。 | 病克医生，药不灵验。 | if the illness controls the doctor the medicine is not effective. |
| 鬼爻持世人难保， | 鬼爻持世，人难保； | The Pressure line holding the Self, the person can hardly be kept; |
| 吉曜加临大利贞。 | 吉曜加临，大吉大利。 | a lucky star added, greatly favourable. |

> **按**:这是医药章的第二首。「应爻及外为医者，内卦身爻是病人」的划分（应和外卦是医、内卦和身爻是病人）和天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.12「世为病人，应及外卦为医药」）、易隐（LY.ILL.YY.21「内卦世爻用爻是病人、外卦应爻子孙是医药」）一致，只是这里说「身爻」而不说「世爻」。「医来克病人方愈」的「病人」原文如此，意思是「医克病」。「吉曜」是吉星，书里没有列名。
>
> **Note:** This is the second poem of the chapter on medicine. Its division 'the Other line and the outer trigram are the doctor, the inner trigram and the Body line are the sick person' agrees with the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.12, 'the Self is the sick person, the Other and the outer trigram are the doctor and the medicine') and the Yi Yin (LY.ILL.YY.21, 'the inner trigram, the Self line and the Subject are the sick person, the outer trigram, the Other line and Output the doctor and medicine'), except that here it says 'the Body line' and not 'the Self line'. The 'sick person' of 'if the doctor controls the sick person he is cured' is as the original reads, and means 'the doctor controls the illness'. 'Lucky star' is a lucky star, of which the book gives no name. 

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1173 行:夫病欲医须卦静;同文 第 1173 行:爻须旺相子扶身;同文 第 1173 行:应爻及外为医者;同文 第 1173 行:内卦身爻是病人;同文 第 1173 行:病若克医药不灵;同文 第 1173 行:鬼爻持世人难保
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1367 行:夫病欲医须卦静;同文 第 1367 行:爻须旺相子扶身;同文 第 1368 行:应爻及外为医者;同文 第 1368 行:内卦身爻是病人;同文 第 1368 行:医来克病人方愈;同文 第 1368 行:病若克医药不灵;同文 第 1369 行:鬼爻持世人难保;同文 第 1369 行:吉曜加临大利贞

相关:LY.ILL.CA.08、LY.ILL.TXF.12、LY.ILL.YY.21

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.16 洞林秘诀·占疾病：鬼杀刑克主灾疾、游魂入墓死亡推；占亲人爻要有；鬼爻持世须求鬼、服药进灾殃；卦有子孙鬼不犯便须服药；我克他先呕逆、他克我泻为伤；忌食鬼爻生肖肉、子孙能解

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼、杀、刑克，主有灾病；游魂卦、入墓，按死亡推。占亲人，那个爻要有，没有就主倾危。虎刑这一个杀最可怕，其余别的凶恶都可以禳解。鬼爻持世，要向鬼求（祈祷），这时若服药反而增加灾殃；卦里有子孙，鬼不来侵犯，就该服药，得安康。我克他，先呕逆；他克我，泻而成伤。忌吃鬼爻生肖的肉，子孙的能解，就无妨。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A ghost, a killing, punishment and control mean calamity and illness; a Wandering Soul hexagram or entering a tomb is inferred as death. Divining for a relative, the relative's line must be there, and if it is not it means peril. The killing of Tiger and punishment is the one to fear deeply, and all the other evils can be warded off. With the Pressure line holding the Self one must seek the ghost, and if one then takes medicine it only advances calamity; if the hexagram has Output and the ghost does not offend, then one should take medicine and gain health and peace. If I control him there is retching first; if he controls me purging is the harm. Avoid eating the meat of the animal of the ghost line; that of Output can dissolve it and does no harm.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·洞林秘诀》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1708–1710 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Dongling Mijue", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 1708–1710*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 洞林秘诀云 | 洞林秘诀说： | The Dongling Mijue says: |
| 鬼杀刑克主灾疾， | 鬼、杀、刑克，主有灾病； | A ghost, a killing, punishment and control mean calamity and illness; |
| 游魂入墓死亡推， | 游魂卦、入墓，按死亡推； | a Wandering Soul hexagram or entering a tomb is inferred as death; |
| 若占亲人爻要有， | 如果占的是亲人，那个亲人的爻要有； | if one divines for a relative, the relative's line must be there; |
| 若还无有主倾危。 | 若是没有，主倾危。 | if it is not there it means peril. |
| 虎刑一杀深为惧， | 虎刑这一个杀，最可怕； | the killing of Tiger and punishment is the one to fear deeply; |
| 其余别恶尽堪禳， | 其余别的凶恶，都可以禳解。 | all the other evils can be warded off. |
| 鬼爻持世须求鬼， | 鬼爻持世，要向鬼求； | the Pressure line holding the Self, one must seek the ghost; |
| 若逢服药进灾殃。 | 若这时服药，反而增加灾殃。 | if one then takes medicine it only advances calamity. |
| 卦有子孙鬼不犯， | 卦里有子孙，鬼不来侵犯， | if the hexagram has Output the ghost does not offend, |
| 便须服药得安康， | 就该服药，可得安康。 | then one should take medicine and gain health and peace. |
| 我若克他先呕逆， | 我若克他，先呕逆； | if I control him, there is retching first; |
| 他还克我泻为伤。 | 他若克我，泻泄成伤。 | if he controls me, purging is the harm. |
| 忌食鬼爻生肖肉， | 忌吃鬼爻所属生肖的肉， | Avoid eating the meat of the animal of the Pressure line's branch; |
| 子孙能解却无妨（1）。 | 子孙的生肖能解，反而无妨。 | the animal of Output can dissolve it and does no harm. |

> **按**:「我」「他」这里按世和应读（世克应先呕逆、应克世泻），孙膑断疾病歌的「外克内应克世有药频频泻、内克外世克应多呕吐」是同一对（LY.ILL.DYTJ.04）。「鬼爻持世须求鬼」的「须求鬼」按字面译作「要向鬼求」：上下文是接着说服药会增加灾殃，所以这里大概是说该向鬼神祈求，而不是服药，这是读法，原文没有明说。「虎刑一杀」是白虎加刑的杀，原文没有展开。「（1）」是今人加的注号，那条注不收；忌食生肖肉的做法，火珠林一段（LY.ILL.HZL.04 一带）也有：丑鬼不可食牛肉，未子孙当食羊。
>
> **Note:** 'I' and 'he' are read here as the Self and the Other (the Self controlling the Other means retching first, the Other controlling the Self purging), the same pair as in Sun Bin's song, 'the outer controlling the inner, the Other controlling the Self: purging again and again; the inner controlling the outer, the Self controlling the Other: mostly vomiting' (LY.ILL.DYTJ.04). The 'xu qiu gui' of 'the Pressure line holding the Self, one must seek the ghost' is rendered literally as 'must seek the ghost': the context goes on to say that taking medicine will add calamity, so it probably says one should pray to the spirits rather than take medicine, which is a reading and the original does not say so plainly. 'The killing of Tiger and punishment' is the killing of the White Tiger with punishment, and the original does not expand on it. The '(1)' is a modern annotation mark and that annotation is not quoted; the practice of avoiding the meat of the branch animal is also in the Huozhu Lin (around LY.ILL.HZL.04): a Chou ghost must not eat beef, and Output at Wei should eat mutton.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.04、LY.ILL.TXF.12、LY.ILL.TXF.13、LY.ILL.HZL.04

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.30 月解、日解、天医：病人有月解神天医上卦即病愈；日解戊癸鸡啼官事散、病人无药不须忧；天医正卯二亥…病人有此爻上卦必主名医下药有效

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月解：正月二月起在申，三月四月在酉，五月六月在戌，七月八月在亥，九月十月在午，子丑两月（十一月、十二月）在未。病人有这个解神，天医又上了卦，就病愈了。日解：甲己日逢巳（蛇），乙庚日逢申（猴），丙辛日逢寅（虎），丁壬日逢丑（牛），戊癸日逢酉（鸡），官事就散，病人没有药也不用忧。天医：正月在卯，二月亥，三月丑，四月未，五月巳，六月卯，七月亥，八月丑，九月未，十月巳，十一月卯，十二月亥。病人有这一爻上卦，必定有名医下药有效。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Month Resolution: in the first and second months it is at Shen, the third and fourth at You, the fifth and sixth at Xu, the seventh and eighth at Hai, the ninth and tenth at Wu, and in the two months of Zi and Chou (the eleventh and twelfth) at Wei. A sick person who has this resolving spirit, with the Heavenly Physician also in the hexagram, is cured at once. The Day Resolution: on Jia and Ji days it is Si (the snake), on Yi and Geng days Shen (the monkey), on Bing and Xin days Yin (the tiger), on Ding and Ren days Chou (the ox), on Wu and Gui days You (the rooster), and official business scatters, and a sick person without medicine need not worry. The Heavenly Physician: first month Mao, second Hai, third Chou, fourth Wei, fifth Si, sixth Mao, seventh Hai, eighth Chou, ninth Wei, tenth Si, eleventh Mao, twelfth Hai. A sick person who has this line in the hexagram will surely have a famous doctor whose medicine is effective.

**《断易天机·论神煞·吉神(十四月解、十五日解、十六天医)》原文** — 《断易天机》第 211 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "On spirits and killings: the lucky spirits (14 Month Resolution, 15 Day Resolution, 16 Heavenly Physician)", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 211*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 月解正二起于申， | 月解：正月、二月起在申， | The Month Resolution: in the first and second months it starts at Shen, |
| 三四还从酉上临， | 三月、四月仍在酉上； | in the third and fourth it falls again on You; |
| 五六之月居戌上， | 五月、六月在戌上； | in the fifth and sixth months it sits on Xu; |
| 七八能行亥上存， | 七月、八月行到亥上； | in the seventh and eighth it goes on to stay at Hai; |
| 九十之月临午位， | 九月、十月到午位； | in the ninth and tenth months it comes to the Wu place; |
| 子丑两月未宫停。 | 子丑两月（十一月、十二月）停在未宫。 | in the two months of Zi and Chou (the eleventh and twelfth) it stops at the Wei palace. |
| 病人有此解神， | 病人有这个解神， | A sick person who has this resolving spirit, |
| 天医上卦即病愈也。 | 天医又上了卦，就病愈了。 | with the Heavenly Physician also in the hexagram, is cured at once. |

**《断易天机·论神煞·吉神(十四月解、十五日解、十六天医)》原文** — 《断易天机》第 214 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "On spirits and killings: the lucky spirits (14 Month Resolution, 15 Day Resolution, 16 Heavenly Physician)", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 214*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 甲己逢蛇乙庚猴， | 日解：甲、己日逢巳（蛇），乙、庚日逢申（猴）， | The Day Resolution: on Jia and Ji days it is Si (the snake), on Yi and Geng days Shen (the monkey), |
| 丙辛逢虎丁壬牛， | 丙、辛日逢寅（虎），丁、壬日逢丑（牛）， | on Bing and Xin days Yin (the tiger), on Ding and Ren days Chou (the ox), |
| 戊癸鸡啼官事散， | 戊、癸日逢酉（鸡），官事就散； | on Wu and Gui days You (the rooster), and official business scatters; |
| 病人无药不须忧。 | 病人没有药也不用忧。 | a sick person without medicine need not worry. |
| 官事若得旺发即散也。 | 官事如果得旺相发动，就散了。 | Official business, if it is strong and moves, scatters at once. |

**《断易天机·论神煞·吉神(十四月解、十五日解、十六天医)》原文** — 《断易天机》第 217–218 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "On spirits and killings: the lucky spirits (14 Month Resolution, 15 Day Resolution, 16 Heavenly Physician)", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 217–218*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 天医正卯二猪临， | 天医：正月在卯，二月在亥（猪）， | The Heavenly Physician: in the first month at Mao, in the second at Hai (the pig), |
| 三月随丑四未寻， | 三月随到丑，四月在未， | in the third month it follows to Chou, in the fourth it is found at Wei, |
| 五蛇六兔七居亥， | 五月是巳（蛇），六月是卯（兔），七月居亥， | the fifth Si (the snake), the sixth Mao (the rabbit), the seventh it stays at Hai, |
| 八丑九羊十巳存， | 八月丑，九月未（羊），十月巳， | the eighth Chou, the ninth Wei (the sheep), the tenth it stays at Si, |
| 十一再来寻卯上， | 十一月再回到卯上， | the eleventh it comes again to Mao, |
| 十二亥上作医人。 | 十二月亥上作医人。 | and in the twelfth month Hai acts as the physician. |
| 病人有此爻上卦， | 病人有这一爻上卦， | A sick person who has this line in the hexagram |
| 必主名医下药有效也。 | 必定有名医下药有效。 | will surely have a famous doctor whose medicine is effective. |

> **按**:月解的排法（正二申、三四酉、五六戌、七八亥、九十午、子丑未）和神煞月令表里的「地解」一行完全一样（LY.ILL.DYTJ.34），天医的排法和表里的「天医」一行也一样。两处的断语略有不同：这里说「病人有此解神，天医上卦即病愈」，表里说地解是「病者有此发动即安」。日解「甲己逢蛇乙庚猴……」是按日干取解神所在的地支（甲己取巳、乙庚取申、丙辛取寅、丁壬取丑、戊癸取酉），「官事散」是它对官事的作用，「病人无药不须忧」是对病人的作用。「天医上卦」是天医这个地支落在卦里的爻上。「病人有此爻上卦，必主名医下药有效也」和天玄赋的「月解交重灾渐退，天医发动便回生」（LY.ILL.TXF.05）是同一类说法。
>
> **Note:** The way the Month Resolution is laid out (first and second months Shen, third and fourth You, fifth and sixth Xu, seventh and eighth Hai, ninth and tenth Wu, Zi and Chou Wei) is exactly the same as the row 'Earth Resolution' in the table of spirits and killings by month (LY.ILL.DYTJ.34), and the way the Heavenly Physician is laid out is also the same as the row 'Heavenly Physician' in the table. The judgments differ slightly: here it says 'a sick person who has this resolving spirit, with the Heavenly Physician in the hexagram, is cured at once', and the table says of the Earth Resolution 'a sick person who has this moving is at once at ease'. The Day Resolution 'Jia and Ji meet the snake, Yi and Geng the monkey ...' takes the branch where the resolving spirit lies by the day stem (Jia and Ji take Si, Yi and Geng Shen, Bing and Xin Yin, Ding and Ren Chou, Wu and Gui You), and 'official business scatters' is its effect on official business and 'a sick person without medicine need not worry' its effect on a sick person. 'The Heavenly Physician in the hexagram' is the branch of the Heavenly Physician falling on a line of the hexagram. 'A sick person who has this line in the hexagram will surely have a famous doctor whose medicine is effective' is the same kind of saying as the Tianxuan Fu's 'when the Month resolves and the Day moves heavily calamity fades, when the Heavenly Physician moves he recovers at once' (LY.ILL.TXF.05).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 9997 行:月解正二起于申;异文(相似 0.86) 第 9997 行:三四还从酉上;异文(相似 0.86) 第 9997 行:五六之月从戌上;同文 第 9997 行:七八能行亥上存;同文 第 9997 行:九十之月临午位;异文(相似 0.86) 第 9997 行:子丑两月未宫
- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 10001 行:甲己逢蛇乙庚猴;同文 第 10001 行:丙辛逢虎丁壬牛;同文 第 10001 行:戊癸鸡啼官事散;同文 第 10001 行:病人无药不须忧
- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 9947 行:天医正卯二猪临;同文 第 9947 行:三月随丑四未寻;同文 第 9947 行:五蛇六兔七居亥;同文 第 9947 行:八丑九羊十巳存;同文 第 9947 行:十一再来寻卯上;同文 第 9947 行:十二亥上作医人
- 《易冒》:同文 第 696 行:天医正卯二猪临;异文(相似 0.86) 第 696 行:三月随牛四未寻;同文 第 696 行:五蛇六兔七居亥;同文 第 696 行:八丑九羊十巳存;同文 第 696 行:十一再来寻卯上;同文 第 696 行:十二亥上作医人

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.34、LY.ILL.TXF.05、LY.ILL.DYTJ.16

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.34 神煞月令表·吉神(天医、地解)：天医有此爻者疾病易愈；地解病者有此发动即安

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:神煞月令表的吉神两行：天医（正月起卯、亥、丑、未、巳、卯、亥、丑、未、巳、卯、亥），卦里有这一爻的，疾病容易痊愈；地解（申、申、酉、酉、戌、戌、亥、亥、午、午、未、未），病人有这一爻发动，就安稳了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Two lucky rows of the table of spirits and killings by month: the Heavenly Physician (from the first month Mao, Hai, Chou, Wei, Si, Mao, Hai, Chou, Wei, Si, Mao, Hai): if this line is in the hexagram, an illness is easily cured; the Earth Resolution (Shen, Shen, You, You, Xu, Xu, Hai, Hai, Wu, Wu, Wei, Wei): for a sick person, if this moves, he is at once at ease.

**《断易天机·神煞月令表》原文** — 《断易天机》第 360 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "The table of spirits and killings by month", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 360*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 神煞 | 神煞 | the spirit or killing |
| 月令 | 月令（哪个月） | the month |
| 一二三四五六七八九十十一十二 | 一、二、三、四、五、六、七、八、九、十、十一、十二（月） | first, second, third, fourth, fifth, sixth, seventh, eighth, ninth, tenth, eleventh, twelfth (month) |
| 说明 | 说明 | explanation |

**《断易天机·神煞月令表》原文** — 《断易天机》第 368 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "The table of spirits and killings by month", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 368*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 天医 | 天医 | Heavenly Physician |
| 卯亥丑未巳卯亥丑未巳卯亥 | 卯、亥、丑、未、巳、卯、亥、丑、未、巳、卯、亥（正月到十二月依次） | Mao, Hai, Chou, Wei, Si, Mao, Hai, Chou, Wei, Si, Mao, Hai (from the first month to the twelfth in turn) |
| 有此爻者，疾病易愈。 | 卦里有这一爻的，疾病容易痊愈。 | if this line is in the hexagram, an illness is easily cured. |

**《断易天机·神煞月令表》原文** — 《断易天机》第 381 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "The table of spirits and killings by month", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 381*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 地解 | 地解 | Earth Resolution |
| 申申酉酉戌戌亥亥午午未未 | 申、申、酉、酉、戌、戌、亥、亥、午、午、未、未（正月到十二月依次） | Shen, Shen, You, You, Xu, Xu, Hai, Hai, Wu, Wu, Wei, Wei (from the first month to the twelfth in turn) |
| 病者有此发动即安。 | 病人有这一爻发动，就安稳了。 | for a sick person, if this moves, he is at once at ease. |

> **按**:这是《断易天机》「神煞月令表」（表头：神煞、月令一到十二、说明）里和病有关的两行吉神。每一行：第一格是名字，接着十二格是它在正月到十二月各落的地支，最后一格是断语。原表分吉神（天德、月德……）和凶神（岁杀、三丘……）两半，病的几行分散在其中。天医这一行的排法和前面「十六、天医」那首歌（LY.ILL.DYTJ.30）一样；地解这一行的排法和「十四、月解」的排法一样（正二申、三四酉、五六戌……），但断语不同：月解说「病人有此解神，天医上卦即病愈」，地解说「病者有此发动即安」。表里别的解神（天解「卦遇此爻恶事解散」、外解「遇此爻灾患消除」、天喜「官事消散」）没有专说病，不收。
>
> **Note:** These are the two lucky rows about illness in the 'table of spirits and killings by month' of the Duanyi Tianji (the head of the table: spirit or killing, month one to twelve, explanation). Each row has as its first cell the name, then twelve cells for the branch it falls on from the first to the twelfth month, and last the judgment. The original table is in two halves, lucky spirits (Heavenly Virtue, Month Virtue ...) and fierce spirits (Year Killing, Three Mounds ...), and the rows about illness are scattered through it. The layout of the Heavenly Physician row is the same as the song '16 Heavenly Physician' before it (LY.ILL.DYTJ.30); the layout of the Earth Resolution row is the same as that of '14 Month Resolution' (first and second Shen, third and fourth You, fifth and sixth Xu ...), but the judgment differs: the Month Resolution says 'a sick person who has this resolving spirit, with the Heavenly Physician in the hexagram, is cured at once', and the Earth Resolution says 'a sick person who has this moving is at once at ease'. The other resolving spirits in the table (Heavenly Resolution 'a hexagram meeting this line, ill matters are resolved and scatter', Outer Resolution 'meeting this line calamity is removed', Heavenly Joy 'official business dissolves') do not speak specially of illness and are not taken.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《易隐》:异文(相似 0.93) 第 823 行:正二三四五六七八九十十一十二
- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 362 行:卯亥丑未巳卯亥丑未巳卯亥
- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 361 行:申申酉酉戌戌亥亥午午未未
- 《易隐》:同文 第 884 行:申申酉酉戌戌亥亥午午未未

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.30、LY.ILL.DYTJ.35、LY.ILL.TXF.05

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.37 神煞月令表·血忌：不家针灸凶

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:血忌（正月起丑、未、寅、申、卯、酉、辰、戌、巳、亥、午、子）：不可在家里针灸，凶。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Blood Taboo (from the first month Chou, Wei, Yin, Shen, Mao, You, Chen, Xu, Si, Hai, Wu, Zi): no needling or moxibustion at home, ominous.

**《断易天机·神煞月令表》原文** — 《断易天机》第 388 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "The table of spirits and killings by month", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 388*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 血忌 | 血忌 | Blood Taboo |
| 丑未寅申卯酉辰戌巳亥午子 | 丑、未、寅、申、卯、酉、辰、戌、巳、亥、午、子（正月到十二月依次） | Chou, Wei, Yin, Shen, Mao, You, Chen, Xu, Si, Hai, Wu, Zi (from the first month to the twelfth in turn) |
| 不家针灸凶。 | 不可在家里针灸，凶。 | no needling or moxibustion at home; ominous. |

> **按**:血忌按月排：正月丑、二月未、三月寅、四月申、五月卯、六月酉、七月辰、八月戌、九月巳、十月亥、十一月午、十二月子。「不家针灸凶」的「不家」读不确定，这里按「不可在家里（用针灸）」译；意思大概是血忌落在的月份、日子里，不要在家里做针灸放血这类会见血的治疗。这一行是这一章里唯一说到治疗方式的神煞。针灸的宜忌按鬼爻五行定的说法，见天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.13）和火珠林（LY.ILL.HZL.09）。
>
> **Note:** Blood Taboo is laid out by month: Chou in the first month, Wei in the second, Yin in the third, Shen in the fourth, Mao in the fifth, You in the sixth, Chen in the seventh, Xu in the eighth, Si in the ninth, Hai in the tenth, Wu in the eleventh, Zi in the twelfth. The 'bu jia' of 'bu jia zhen jiu xiong' is not certain, and here it is rendered 'no needling or moxibustion at home'; the sense is probably that in the months and days on which the Blood Taboo falls, one should not do at home the treatments that draw blood, such as needling and moxibustion. This row is the only spirit or killing in this chapter that speaks of a way of treatment. For the rules on what to use and avoid in needling and moxibustion by the phase of the ghost line see the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.13) and the Huozhu Lin (LY.ILL.HZL.09).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 364 行:丑未寅申卯酉辰戌巳亥午子
- 《易林补遗》:异文(相似 0.83) 第 1481 行:丑未、寅申、卯酉、辰戌、巳亥
- 《易隐》:同文 第 1007 行:丑未寅申卯酉辰戌巳亥午子

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.13、LY.ILL.HZL.09

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.44 鬼谷辨爻法·占医药：初爻针药、二爻酸药、三爻甘药、四爻苦药、五爻辛药、六爻医师

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼谷子辨爻法在占医药一章的表：初爻是针药，二爻是酸药，三爻是甘药，四爻是苦药，五爻是辛药，六爻是医师。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Guigu's table of the lines for the chapter on doctors and medicine: the first line is needle medicine, the second sour medicine, the third sweet medicine, the fourth bitter medicine, the fifth pungent medicine and the sixth the physician.

**《断易天机·占医药第三十五·鬼谷辨爻法》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1166–1169 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 35: Guigu's method of telling the lines apart", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 1166–1169*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼谷辨爻法 | 鬼谷子辨别各爻的办法 | Guigu's method of telling the lines apart |
| 初爻二爻三爻四爻五爻六爻 | 初爻、二爻、三爻、四爻、五爻、六爻 | the first, second, third, fourth, fifth and sixth lines |
| 针药酸药甘药苦药辛药医师 | 针药、酸药、甘药、苦药、辛药、医师 | needle medicine, sour medicine, sweet medicine, bitter medicine, pungent medicine, the physician |

> **按**:这是占医药一章开头的一张三行的表，原书排成六列：第一行是「鬼谷辨爻法」这个标题，第二行是初爻到六爻，第三行是六个名目。按位置一格对一爻：初爻针药、二爻酸药、三爻甘药、四爻苦药、五爻辛药、六爻医师。「针药」是用针（针灸）的治法，「酸药」「甘药」「苦药」「辛药」是酸、甘、苦、辛四味的药，表里没有咸味；书里没有解释这六格为什么这样配。这张表的性质和占疾病一章开头的鬼谷辨爻法表（LY.ILL.DYTJ.01）一样，是把六个爻位配给这一章要看的东西。
>
> **Note:** This is a three-row table at the head of the chapter on doctors and medicine, laid out in the original as six columns: the first row is the heading 'Guigu's method of telling the lines apart', the second the first to sixth lines, and the third six names. Cell by cell by position: the first line needle medicine, the second sour medicine, the third sweet medicine, the fourth bitter medicine, the fifth pungent medicine, the sixth the physician. 'Needle medicine' is treatment with the needle (acupuncture), and 'sour', 'sweet', 'bitter' and 'pungent medicine' are medicines of the four tastes sour, sweet, bitter and pungent, with no salty taste in the table; the book does not explain why the six cells are matched so. This table is of the same kind as the table under Guigu's name at the head of the chapter on illness (LY.ILL.DYTJ.01), matching the six line positions to what the chapter is about.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 443 行:初爻二爻三爻四爻五爻六爻

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.01、LY.ILL.DYTJ.45

---

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.45 孙真人论医歌：夫病欲医须卦静、爻须旺相子扶身；应爻及外为医者、内卦身爻是病人；医能克病人方治、病若克医药不灵；鬼爻持世人难保、吉杀加临获万全

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:病人的病要医治，卦要安静，爻要旺相，子孙扶身；应爻和外卦是医生，内卦和身爻是病人；医生克病，病人才能治；病克医生，药不灵；鬼爻持世，人难保；吉杀加临，可获万全。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a man's illness is to be treated the hexagram must be still, the lines strong and Output supporting the Body; the Other line and the outer trigram are the doctor, the inner trigram and the Body line are the sick person; if the doctor controls the illness the person can be treated; if the illness controls the doctor the medicine is not effective; the Pressure line holding the Self, the person can hardly be kept; a lucky killing added, complete safety is gained.

**《断易天机·占医药第三十五·孙真人论医歌》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1173 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 35: Master Sun's song on doctors", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1173*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫病欲医须卦静， | 病人的病要医，卦要安静， | When a man's illness is to be treated the hexagram must be still, |
| 爻须旺相子扶身， | 爻要旺相，子孙扶身； | the line must be strong and Output support the Body; |
| 应爻及外为医者， | 应爻和外卦是医生， | the Other line and the outer trigram are the doctor; |
| 内卦身爻是病人。 | 内卦和身爻是病人。 | the inner trigram and the Body line are the sick person. |
| 医能克病人方治， | 医生能克病，病人才能治； | if the doctor is able to control the illness the person can be treated; |
| 病若克医药不灵， | 病如果克医生，药不灵； | if the illness controls the doctor the medicine is not effective; |
| 鬼爻持世人难保， | 鬼爻持世，人难保； | The Pressure line holding the Self, the person can hardly be kept; |
| 吉杀加临获万全。 | 吉杀加临，可获万全。 | a lucky killing added, complete safety is gained. |

> **按**:孙真人是唐代的孙思邈。这首医药歌和阐奥歌章医药章的第二首（LY.ILL.CA.09）几乎一样，前四句（夫病欲医须卦静……内卦身爻是病人）和「鬼爻持世人难保」一字不差，只有三处不同：阐奥歌章是「医来克病人方愈」，这里是「医能克病人方治」；阐奥歌章是「吉曜加临大吉大利」，这里是「吉杀加临获万全」；「病若克医药不灵」两处相同。「吉杀」是带吉的神煞（指吉神吉煞），「获万全」是可得万全。原文孙真人这一首后面，断易天机没有再引注（今人有译文，不收）。这一首的医、病分法（应和外卦是医、内卦和世身是病人）和天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.12）是同一套。
>
> **Note:** Master Sun is Sun Simiao of the Tang. This song on medicine is almost the same as the second poem in the chapter on medicine in the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.09): the first four lines ('when a man's illness is to be treated the hexagram must be still ... the inner trigram and the Body line are the sick person') and 'the Pressure line holding the Self, the person can hardly be kept' are word for word the same, and there are only differences in a few lines: the Chan'ao Gezhang has 'if the doctor controls the illness the person is cured' where this has 'if the doctor is able to control the illness the person can be treated', and 'a lucky star added, greatly favourable' where this has 'a lucky killing added, complete safety is gained'; 'if the illness controls the doctor the medicine is not effective' is the same in both. 'A lucky killing' is a spirit or killing that brings luck, and 'complete safety is gained' is that complete safety can be had. After this poem of Master Sun the Duanyi Tianji cites no commentary (modern hands have added a translation, which is not quoted). The division of doctor and illness in this poem (the Other line and the outer trigram are the doctor, the inner trigram and the Self and Body are the sick person) is the same set as the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.12).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 4028 行:夫病欲医须卦静;同文 第 4028 行:爻须旺相子扶身;同文 第 4028 行:应爻及外为医者;同文 第 4028 行:内卦身爻是病人;同文 第 4028 行:病若克医药不灵;同文 第 4028 行:鬼爻持世人难保
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1367 行:夫病欲医须卦静;同文 第 1367 行:爻须旺相子扶身;同文 第 1368 行:应爻及外为医者;同文 第 1368 行:内卦身爻是病人;同文 第 1368 行:病若克医药不灵;同文 第 1369 行:鬼爻持世人难保

相关:LY.ILL.CA.09、LY.ILL.TXF.12、LY.ILL.DYTJ.46

---

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.46 卜筮元龟·占医人善恶：欲占医人善恶详无过贞悔可斟量；子爻在土宜丸药、水宜服汤、木青丸药、金火宜灸灼；子爻旺相好医人、无气杀冲还恶；卦无子爻何以遂、旺相相生药无崇、内外不和服药凶、更遇杀冲须早惧

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:要占医生的好坏，要看得详细，不过是斟酌贞（内卦）和悔（外卦）。子爻在土，宜用丸药；若是水爻，宜服汤；子爻是木，宜青丸药；金、火两爻，宜灸灼。子爻旺相，是好医生；无气、遭杀冲，还是坏的。卦里没有子爻，怎么能遂心？旺相相生，药就没有祟；内外不和，服药凶；再遇到杀、冲，要早早警惕。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To divine whether a doctor is good or bad, look in detail; it is no more than weighing the inner (zhen) and outer (hui) trigrams. If the Output line is on Earth, pills are fit; if it is Water, a decoction should be taken; if the Output line is Wood, green pills; if Metal or Fire, moxibustion and cautery are fit. If the Output line is strong the doctor is good; if it has no qi or meets a killing or clash he is still a bad one. With no Output line in the hexagram how can it be accomplished? Strong and generating each other, the medicine has no curse; with the inner and outer not in harmony taking medicine is ominous; and if meeting a killing or clash as well, one must be wary early.

**《断易天机·占医药第三十五·卜筮元龟》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1180 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 35: Bushi Yuangui", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1180*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 欲占医人善恶详， | 要占医生的好坏，要看得详细， | To divine whether a doctor is good or bad, look in detail, |
| 无过贞悔可斟量（1）， | 不过是斟酌贞（内卦）和悔（外卦）； | it is no more than weighing the inner (zhen) and outer (hui) trigrams; |
| 子爻在土宜丸药， | 子爻在土，宜用丸药； | if the Output line is on Earth, pills are fit; |
| 若是水爻宜服汤。 | 若是水爻，宜服汤药。 | if it is Water, a decoction should be taken. |
| 子爻是木青丸药， | 子爻是木，宜青色的丸药； | if the Output line is Wood, green pills; |
| 金火两爻宜灸灼， | 金、火两种爻，宜用灸灼； | if Metal or Fire, moxibustion and cautery are fit; |
| 子爻旺相好医人， | 子爻旺相，是好医生； | if the Output line is strong the doctor is good; |
| 无气杀冲还恶矣（2）。 | 无气、遭杀冲，还是坏的。 | if it has no qi or meets a killing or clash he is still a bad one. |
| 卦无子爻何以遂， | 卦里没有子爻，怎么能遂心？ | with no Output line in the hexagram how can it be accomplished? |
| 旺相相生药无崇， | 旺相相生，药就没有祟； | strong and generating each other, the medicine has no curse; |
| 内外不和服药凶， | 内外不和，服药凶； | with the inner and outer not in harmony taking medicine is ominous; |
| 更遇杀冲须早惧（3）。 | 再遇到杀、冲，要早早警惕。 | and if meeting a killing or clash as well, one must be wary early. |

> **打架**:用药按谁的五行选，几本书的做法不同。断易天机卜筮元龟这一首按子爻（药）的五行：子爻属土宜丸药、属水宜汤、属木宜青丸药、属金火宜灸灼；洞林秘诀（LY.ILL.DYTJ.47）说「药色五行自斟酌」，注也是按子孙爻的五行取药色；天玄赋注（LY.ILL.TXF.13）、火珠林（LY.ILL.HZL.09）、易隐（LY.ILL.YY.25）按鬼爻的五行：火鬼宜凉剂、水鬼宜温剂或丸药、木鬼宜针、金鬼宜灸、土鬼宜锉散。两个体系都在：一个看药本身（子孙）的五行，一个看病（鬼）的五行，并且结论不同（比如金爻：子爻属金宜灸灼，金鬼也宜灸，一致；水：子爻是水宜服汤，水鬼宜温剂或丸药，不一致）。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whose phase to use in choosing medicine is done differently in different books. The Bushi Yuangui song of the Duanyi Tianji takes the phase of the Output line (the medicine): Output on Earth pills, Water a decoction, Wood green pills, Metal and Fire moxibustion and cautery; the Dongling Mijue (LY.ILL.DYTJ.47) says 'the colour of the medicine is for oneself to weigh by the five phases', taking the colour of the medicine also from the phase of the Output line; the commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.13), the Huozhu Lin (LY.ILL.HZL.09) and the Yi Yin (LY.ILL.YY.25) take the phase of the ghost line: a Fire ghost a cooling remedy, a Water ghost a warming remedy or pills, a Wood ghost the needle, a Metal ghost moxa, an Earth ghost cut-and-ground powders. Both systems are there: one looks at the phase of the medicine itself (Output), the other at the phase of the illness (the ghost), and their conclusions differ (for Metal, Output on Metal is fit for moxibustion and a Metal ghost is also fit for moxa, which agree; for Water, Output on Water is fit for a decoction and a Water ghost for a warming remedy or pills, which do not agree). The books do not settle it.

> **按**:「贞悔」是内卦（贞）和外卦（悔），内卦是病人、外卦是医药，拿这两个来比。「子爻」是子孙爻，是药；这里按子爻所属的五行选药：子爻属土宜丸药、属水宜汤药、属木宜青色的丸药、属金或火宜灸灼。「药无崇」的「崇」按「祟」译（药里没有作祟的东西），字面译作 curse。「（1）」「（2）」「（3）」是今人加的注号，那几条注不收。这里的用药是按子孙爻的五行选，天玄赋、火珠林和易隐是按鬼爻的五行选（见「打架」）。
>
> **Note:** The 'zhen and hui' are the inner trigram (zhen) and the outer trigram (hui); the inner trigram is the sick person and the outer the medicine, and the two are compared. The 'Output line' is the Output line, the medicine; here the medicine is chosen by the phase the Output line belongs to: Output on Earth is fit for pills, on Water for a decoction, on Wood for green pills, on Metal or Fire for moxibustion and cautery. The 'chong' of 'the medicine has no chong' is rendered 'curse' as a miswriting of 'sui' (a curse), that there is nothing in the medicine that brings a curse. The '(1)', '(2)' and '(3)' are modern annotation marks and those annotations are not quoted. The use of medicine here is chosen by the phase of the Output line, and the Tianxuan Fu, the Huozhu Lin and the Yi Yin choose it by the phase of the ghost line (see the dispute).

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.47、LY.ILL.TXF.13、LY.ILL.HZL.09、LY.ILL.YY.25、LY.ILL.DYTJ.45

---

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.47 洞林秘诀·占医药：卦中子孙爻为药、天医相助如扁鹊；应爻是医生、世爻是病人、病不克药总堪医；鬼爻持世不肯食先宜祀神后服药；病伏为鬼不除根

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里的子孙爻是药，有天医相助，像扁鹊一样；卦爻若旺相、伏神没有鬼，病得以除根，治好、安乐。要看医生，应爻就是；病人求医药，世爻来当；病不克药，总是可以医治；药的颜色按五行自己斟酌。鬼爻持世，不肯吃东西，或者过不了喉咙就吐，即使吃下去，病更加重，应当先祭祀神灵，后服药。病伏着成了鬼，不能除根，一时暂且收功，得个大概；再加上生克、杀神来推；病人所适宜的，全在医生。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In the hexagram the Output line is the medicine, and with the Heavenly Physician helping it is like Bian Que; if the lines are strong and the hidden spirit has no ghost, the illness is rooted out, cured, and one is at ease. If one looks at the doctor, it is the Other line; the sick person seeking medicine, the Self line stands for him; if the illness does not control the medicine it can always be treated; the colour of the medicine is for oneself to weigh by the five phases. With the Pressure line holding the Self he will not take food, or it does not pass the throat and is vomited, and even if it is eaten the illness grows worse; one should first sacrifice to the spirits and then take medicine. With the illness hidden and turned into a ghost, it is not rooted out, for a time it gains merit briefly, roughly so; further work it out by generation, control and the killing spirits; what suits the illness is chosen entirely by the doctor.

**《断易天机·占医药第三十五·洞林秘诀》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1192 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 35: Dongling Mijue", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1192*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦中子孙爻为药， | 卦里的子孙爻是药， | In the hexagram the Output line is the medicine, |
| 天医相助如扁鹊， | 有天医相助，像扁鹊一样； | and with the Heavenly Physician helping it is like Bian Que; |
| 卦爻若旺伏无鬼， | 卦爻如果旺相、伏神没有鬼， | if the lines are strong and the hidden spirit has no ghost, |
| 疾得除根治安乐。 | 病得以除根，治好、安乐。 | the illness is rooted out, cured, and one is at ease. |
| 若见医生应爻是， | 如果要看医生，应爻就是； | If one looks at the doctor, it is the Other line; |
| 病求医药世爻作， | 病人求医药，世爻来当； | the sick person seeking medicine, the Self line stands for him; |
| 病不克药总堪医， | 病不克药，总是可以医治； | if the illness does not control the medicine it can always be treated; |
| 药色五行自斟酌（1）。 | 药的颜色按五行自己斟酌。 | the colour of the medicine is for oneself to weigh by the five phases. |
| 鬼爻持世不肯食， | 鬼爻持世，病人不肯吃东西； | the Pressure line holding the Self, he will not take food; |
| 或不过喉便吐却， | 或者过不了喉咙就吐了； | or it does not pass the throat and is vomited; |
| 纵然吃了病尤加， | 即使吃下去，病更加重； | even if it is eaten the illness grows worse; |
| 先宜祀神后服药。 | 应当先祭祀神灵，后服药。 | one should first sacrifice to the spirits and then take medicine. |
| 病伏为鬼不除根， | 病伏着成了鬼，不能除根； | with the illness hidden and turned into a ghost, it is not rooted out; |
| 时暂收功得大略（2）， | 一时暂且收功，得个大概； | for a time it gains merit briefly, roughly so; |
| 更加生克杀神推， | 再加上生克、杀神来推； | further work it out by generation, control and the killing spirits; |
| 病撰所宜尽医者（3）。 | 病人所适宜的，全在医生。 | what suits the illness is chosen entirely by the doctor. |

> **按**:这首洞林秘诀在占医药一章里，和占疾病一章里那一段洞林秘诀（LY.ILL.DYTJ.16）是同一部书的两首：那一首讲鬼、杀、刑克主灾疾，这一首讲医和药。「鬼爻持世不肯食……先宜祀神后服药」和那一首「鬼爻持世须求鬼，若逢服药进灾殃」是同一个意思：鬼爻持世，先要向神灵祈求，然后才服药。「病伏为鬼不除根」按「病爻下伏着鬼」读（病的爻下面伏着鬼爻，所以断不了根），字面译。最后一句「病撰所宜尽医者」读不确定，「撰」有选的意思，这里按「病人所适宜的（药），全在医生（去选）」字面译。「扁鹊」是战国时的名医。「（1）（2）（3）」是今人注号，那几条注不收。天医（天医相助如扁鹊）见 LY.ILL.DYTJ.30、DYTJ.34。药色按五行取，见 LY.ILL.DYTJ.46 的「打架」。
>
> **Note:** This Dongling Mijue in the chapter on doctors and medicine is a second poem from the same book as the passage of the Dongling Mijue in the chapter on illness (LY.ILL.DYTJ.16): that one speaks of the ghost, the killings, punishment and control bringing calamity and illness, and this one of the doctor and the medicine. 'The Pressure line holding the Self, he will not take food ... one should first sacrifice to the spirits and then take medicine' and the other one's 'the Pressure line holding the Self, one must seek the ghost, and if one then takes medicine it only advances calamity' are the same sense: with the Pressure line holding the Self one must first pray to the spirits, and only then take medicine. 'With the illness hidden and turned into a ghost, it is not rooted out' is read as a ghost line hidden under the line of the illness (so that the root cannot be cut), and is rendered literally. The last line, 'what suits the illness is chosen entirely by the doctor', is not certain, and 'zhuan' has the sense of choosing; it is rendered literally as 'what suits the illness (medicine) is chosen entirely by the doctor'. Bian Que was a famous physician of the Warring States. The '(1)', '(2)' and '(3)' are modern annotation marks and those annotations are not quoted. For the Heavenly Physician (the Heavenly Physician helping) see LY.ILL.DYTJ.30 and DYTJ.34. For the colour of the medicine taken by the five phases see the dispute in LY.ILL.DYTJ.46.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.16、LY.ILL.DYTJ.46、LY.ILL.DYTJ.30、LY.ILL.DYTJ.34

---

## LY.ILL.EL.26 求医疗病：子为药剂应为医，福德交重病必驱，鬼旺福灾病不治，官衰子旺患能除，应空只恐人难遇，子陷还愁药不宜；子发动药奏神功；官鬼太旺子休囚不愈；父发动药力全空，父与兄弟同发子赖兄生药有效

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:以子孙为主，应象为凭。子孙是药剂，应爻是医生；福德发动，病必被驱走；鬼旺、福衰，灾病治不好；官鬼衰、子孙旺，病患能除；应爻空，只怕遇不到医生；子孙陷落，还愁药不对症。注说：凡是求医，子孙是用象，子孙如果发动，药就奏神功；其中官鬼太旺，子孙休囚，这个灾不会好；官鬼如果衰弱、福神兴隆，再得到应克世爻、或外卦伤内卦，一定遇到良医，没有灾不好的；如果内克外、以及世克应、子孙旺相，还行；子孙再休囚，药就没有效验，月将伤官铲有余；鬼如果遇上生财、或动，子孙纵然发动也成空虚。父爻如果发动，药力全空；父爻与兄弟同时发动，子孙靠兄弟生，那药有效；如果得到月建、或日辰克制官爻，就像遇到岐伯；倘或鬼临日月、或鬼持世卦，虽然有子孙药也无功；又如子孙、财爻并发，官鬼伏着、财爻扶它，怎么能治病？财爻、子孙纵然发动，卦里没有鬼或鬼空，仍然有效；凡是论医药，若得子孙上卦，官鬼父母两方都安，应爻不空亡，才有效。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Output is the medicine and the Other is the doctor. The fortune spirit moving, the illness is surely driven out; the ghost strong and the fortune spirit weak, the calamity cannot be treated; the Pressure line weak and Output strong, the affliction can be removed; the Other Void, one fears only that the man is hard to meet; Output fallen, one worries still that the medicine is unsuitable. The commentary says: in seeking a doctor Output is the Subject image, and if Output moves the medicine works wonders; where the Pressure line is too strong and Output resting or trapped, the calamity is not cured; if the Pressure line is weak and the fortune spirit thriving, and further the Other controls the Self line or the outer trigram wounds the inner trigram, one is sure to meet a Lu Bian-like physician and no calamity is not cured; if the inner controls the outer, or the Self controls the Other, and Output is strong, it is still fine; if Output is again resting or trapped the medicine has no effect, the Month general wounding the official and planing off what is in excess; if the ghost meets generation of Wealth, or moves, even if Output moves it is hollow. If the Resource line moves, the power of the medicine is wholly empty; if the Resource and Peer lines move together, Output depends on Peer to generate it and the medicine is effective; if the Month or the Day controls the Pressure line, it is as if one met Qi Bo; if the ghost sits on the Day or Month, or the ghost holds the Self, then even with Output as medicine there is no effect; again, if Output and Wealth both move, the Pressure line hidden and supported by Wealth, how can the illness be treated? If Wealth and Output move but the hexagram has no ghost or the ghost is Void, it is still effective; in all discussion of medicine, it works only if Output is in the hexagram, the Pressure line and the Resource line are both at peace, and the Other is not Void.

**《易林补遗·求医疗病章第七十二》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1348 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 72: Seeking a doctor to treat illness", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1348*

| 如内克外、及世克应、子孙旺相，还可。 | 如果内克外、以及世克应、子孙旺相，还行。 | If the inner controls the outer, or the Self controls the Other, and Output is strong, it is still fine. |

| 父与兄弟同发，子赖兄生，其药有效。 | 父爻与兄弟同时发动，子孙靠兄弟生，那药有效。 | If the Resource and Peer lines move together, Output depends on Peer to generate it and the medicine is effective. |
| 若得月建、或日辰克制官爻，如逢岐伯。 | 如果得到月建、或日辰克制官爻，就像遇到岐伯。 | If the Month or the Day controls the Pressure line, it is as if one met Qi Bo. |
| 倘或鬼临日月、或鬼持世卦，虽有子药亦无功。 | 倘或鬼临日月、或鬼持世卦，虽然有子孙药也无功。 | If the ghost sits on the Day or Month, or the ghost holds the Self, then even with Output as medicine there is no effect. |
| 又如子财并发，官伏财扶，岂能治病？ | 又如子孙、财爻并发，官鬼伏着、财爻扶它，怎么能治病？ | Again, if Output and Wealth both move, the Pressure line hidden and supported by Wealth, how can the illness be treated? |
| 财子纵发，卦无鬼或鬼空，仍复有效。 | 财爻、子孙纵然发动，卦里没有鬼或鬼空，仍然有效。 | If Wealth and Output move but the hexagram has no ghost or the ghost is Void, it is still effective. |
| 凡论医药，若得子孙上卦，官父两安，应不空亡，方其效也。 | 凡是论医药，若得子孙上卦，官鬼父母两方都安，应爻不空亡，才有效。 | In all discussion of medicine, it works only if Output is in the hexagram, the Pressure line and the Resource line are both at peace, and the Other is not Void. |

> **按**:「卢医」是扁鹊（卢地的医），泛指高明的医生；「岐伯」是传说中黄帝的医官，也是良医的代称。原文这一句「月将伤官铲有余」指子孙休囚时，月将来伤官鬼，把官鬼的多余之势铲掉——这一句前后的连接不很清楚，这里按字面译。这一章和黄金策医药章（LY.ILL.HJC.58 到 HJC.92）是同一路读法：子孙是药，应爻是医，官鬼是病；子旺官衰药灵，官旺子衰不愈；父爻动克子孙则药力空；财爻生官鬼则药误。与易冒医药章（LY.ILL.YM.34 到 YM.36）、增删卜易（LY.ILL.ZS.26 到 ZS.34）也一致。
>
> **Note:** 'A Lu physician' is Bian Que (the physician of Lu), used for any skilled doctor, and 'Qi Bo' is the legendary physician-official of the Yellow Emperor, also a name for an excellent doctor. The line 'the Month general wounding the official and planing off what is in excess' means that when Output is resting or trapped, the Month general comes to wound the Pressure line and plane off its excess strength; how it connects with what surrounds it is not very clear, and it is translated literally. This chapter reads in the same way as the Huangjin Ce's chapter on medicine (LY.ILL.HJC.58 to HJC.92): Output is the medicine, the Other is the doctor, and the Pressure line is the illness; Output strong and Pressure weak means the medicine works, Pressure strong and Output weak means no cure; the Resource line moving and controlling Output empties the power of the medicine; Wealth generating the Pressure line means the medicine goes wrong. It also agrees with the Yi Mao's chapter on medicine (LY.ILL.YM.34 to YM.36) and the Zengshan Buyi (LY.ILL.ZS.26 to ZS.34).

相关:LY.ILL.EL.25、LY.ILL.EL.27、LY.ILL.YM.34、LY.ILL.YM.35、LY.ILL.HJC.58、LY.ILL.HJC.59、LY.ILL.HJC.61、LY.ILL.HJC.65、LY.ILL.ZS.27、LY.ILL.ZS.30、LY.ILL.YY.21

## LY.ILL.EL.27 医家治病（医生自占）：应为病者世为医，子为药效鬼为灾；鬼强子弱殃难解、福旺官柔病渐衰；应上坐官真疾病、身中带福妙医才；世克应灾当瘥、应生身宾主谐；子动兄安财静旺得多财；应空他不迎吾救、世陷吾非治彼灾

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:以子孙为主，世应为凭。应是病人，世是医生；子孙是药效，鬼是灾；鬼强、子孙弱，殃难解；福旺、官柔，病渐衰；应上坐着官鬼，是真疾病；身上带福，是妙医才。世如果克应，灾当痊愈；应如果生世，确实是宾主相合；子动、兄安、财静旺，仙丹妙剂多得财；应空，他不迎我去救；世陷落，我治不了他的灾。注说：医生自己来占，反而把应当作病人，世是自己，子孙是药效，鬼是灾殃：官旺子衰、或官动子静，病一定难医；子动官静、或子旺官柔，药没有不灵验的；应如果临官，病真的无法治；世如果值福，治病能痊愈。世克应爻、或内克外卦，能治好他的灾；应如果生世以及外卦生内爻、或卦逢六合，是有为而来、宾主相得；妻财旺相，子孙交重发动，鬼静兄弟安，药灵而利益加倍；应值空亡，他必定无心就我；世居空地，自己心里萎靡懒散，医恐怕做不成。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Output is the main line, and the Self and the Other are the ground. The Other is the patient and the Self is the doctor; Output is the effect of the medicine and the ghost is the calamity; the ghost strong and Output weak, the misfortune is hard to resolve; the fortune spirit strong and the Pressure line soft, the illness gradually wanes; a Pressure line sitting on the Other means a real illness; the fortune spirit on the body means a marvellous physician. If the Self controls the Other, the calamity should heal; if the Other generates the Self, host and guest truly accord; Output moving, Peer at peace and Wealth still and strong, elixir and wonderful remedy earn much wealth; the Other Void, he does not welcome my rescue; the Self fallen, I cannot cure his calamity. The commentary says: when a doctor himself divines, on the contrary the Other is taken as the patient, the Self as himself, Output as the effect of the medicine and the ghost as the calamity: with Pressure strong and Output weak, or Pressure moving and Output still, the illness is surely hard to treat; with Output moving and Pressure still, or Output strong and Pressure soft, the medicine is never without effect; if the Other sits on Pressure, the illness is real and cannot be treated; if the Self holds the fortune spirit, the treatment can heal. The Self controlling the Other line, or the inner trigram controlling the outer, can cure his calamity; if the Other generates the Self, or the outer generates the inner line, or the hexagram meets six-combine, one comes with purpose and host and guest suit each other; Wealth strong, Output moving, the ghost still and Peer at peace, the medicine is effective and the profit doubled; if the Other is Void, he surely has no heart to come to me; if the Self sits in Void, my own heart is dispirited and lazy, and the doctor may not succeed.

**《易林补遗·医家治病章第七十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1355 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 73: The doctor treating illness", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1355*

| 应如生世及外生内爻、或卦逢六合，有为而来主宾相得。 | 应如果生世以及外卦生内爻、或卦逢六合，是有为而来、宾主相得。 | If the Other generates the Self, or the outer generates the inner line, or the hexagram meets six-combine, one comes with purpose and host and guest suit each other. |
| 妻财旺相，子值交重，鬼静兄安，药且灵而利倍得。 | 妻财旺相，子孙交重发动，鬼静兄弟安，药灵而利益加倍。 | Wealth strong, Output moving, the ghost still and Peer at peace, the medicine is effective and the profit doubled. |
| 应值空亡，彼必无心就我。 | 应值空亡，他必定无心就我。 | If the Other is Void, he surely has no heart to come to me. |
| 世居空地己心悚懒，医恐不成。 | 世居空地，自己心里萎靡懒散，医恐怕做不成。 | If the Self sits in Void, my own heart is dispirited and lazy, and the doctor may not succeed. |

> **按**:这一章的立场和上一章（LY.ILL.EL.26）相反：上一章是病家占，应是医生、世是病人；这一章是医生自己占，应是病人、世是医生。其余不变：子孙是药，官鬼是病。这和易冒医药章里医生问的四问（LY.ILL.YM.38 到 YM.41）是同一件事：易冒说「医问疾可疗乎，以应为先务，盖应为病人」，与这里「应为病者世为医」相同。这一章讲医生自己占两件事：病能不能治（子孙对官鬼），和这趟医有没有酬谢（财爻、应世关系）。「六合」按这个写法译作 six-combine。
>
> **Note:** This chapter stands opposite to the last (LY.ILL.EL.26): there the patient's family divined, and the Other was the doctor and the Self the patient; here the doctor himself divines, and the Other is the patient and the Self the doctor. The rest is unchanged: Output is the medicine and the Pressure line the illness. It is the same matter as the four questions the doctor asks in the Yi Mao's chapter on medicine (LY.ILL.YM.38 to YM.41): the Yi Mao's 'when a doctor asks whether an illness can be treated, the Other comes first, for to him the Other is the patient' agrees with this chapter's 'the Other is the patient and the Self the doctor'. The chapter treats two matters for a doctor's own divination: whether the illness can be treated (Output against the Pressure line), and whether this visit will bring a fee (the Wealth line, and the relation of Other and Self).

相关:LY.ILL.EL.26、LY.ILL.EL.28、LY.ILL.YM.38、LY.ILL.YM.39、LY.ILL.YM.40、LY.ILL.ZS.36、LY.ILL.HJC.62、LY.ILL.HJC.90

## LY.ILL.EL.28 医家占酬谢与阻碍：兄动倘遭同辈阻、财空休望谢金来、六冲岂得终其事、官化官忧病复乖、应克世身兄雀动反遭非讼莫开怀

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:兄弟发动，倘若遭遇同辈阻挠；财空，不要指望谢金来；六冲，怎么能把事做完；官化官，担心病又变糟；应克世身、兄弟和朱雀发动，反而遭到是非和官司，不要开怀。注说：兄弟发动或克世爻，一定被同行霸占；木如果遇空，药金不要指望；六冲的卦，医治不能有始有终；鬼变出鬼爻、或卦里有两个官鬼都动，病重又变病，怎么能治？应如果克世，兄动、财空，又加上朱雀吵嚷，不光求不到谢礼，反而惹来闲是非。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Peer moving, he may meet obstruction from his fellows; Wealth Void, do not look for the fee; a Six-Clash hexagram, how can the matter be finished; Pressure changing into Pressure, one worries the illness turns bad again; the Other controlling the Self, Peer and the Vermilion Bird moving, one meets quarrels and lawsuits instead, and should not be light-hearted. The commentary says: Peer moving or controlling the Self line, one is surely muscled out by fellow physicians; if Wood is Void, do not look for medicine money; in a Six-Clash hexagram the treatment cannot have a beginning and an end; if the ghost changes into a Pressure line, or the hexagram has two Pressure lines both moving, the illness changes again into another, and how can it be treated? If the Other controls the Self, and Peer moves, Wealth is Void and the Vermilion Bird adds its noise, one not only fails to get the fee but brings on idle quarrels.

**《易林补遗·医家治病章第七十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1360 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 73: The doctor treating illness", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1360*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄动倘遭同辈阻，财空休望谢金来， | 兄弟发动，倘若遭遇同辈阻挠；财空，不要指望谢金来； | Peer moving, he may meet obstruction from his fellows; Wealth Void, do not look for the fee; |
| 六冲岂得终其事，官化官忧病复乖， | 六冲，怎么能把事做完；官化官，担心病又变糟； | a Six-Clash hexagram, how can the matter be finished; Pressure changing into Pressure, one worries the illness turns bad again; |
| 应克世身兄雀动，反遭非讼莫开怀。 | 应克世身、兄弟和朱雀发动，反而遭到是非和官司，不要开怀。 | the Other controlling the Self, Peer and the Vermilion Bird moving, one meets quarrels and lawsuits instead, and should not be light-hearted. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1361 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1361*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄弟发动或克世爻，必被同袍霸占。 | 兄弟发动或克世爻，一定被同行霸占。 | Peer moving or controlling the Self line, one is surely muscled out by fellow physicians. |
| 木若逢空，药金莫望。 | 木如果遇空，药金不要指望。 | If Wood is Void, do not look for medicine money. |
| 六冲之卦，医不始终。 | 六冲的卦，医治不能有始有终。 | In a Six-Clash hexagram the treatment cannot have a beginning and an end. |
| 鬼化鬼爻、或卦有二官皆动，病复变病，岂能治之。 | 鬼变出鬼爻、或卦里有两个官鬼都动，病重又变病，怎么能治？ | If the ghost changes into a Pressure line, or the hexagram has two Pressure lines both moving, the illness changes again into another, and how can it be treated? |
| 应如克世、兄动财空又加雀噪，非惟求谢，反惹闲非。 | 应如果克世，兄动、财空，又加上朱雀吵嚷，不光求不到谢礼，反而惹来闲是非。 | If the Other controls the Self, and Peer moves, Wealth is Void and the Vermilion Bird adds its noise, one not only fails to get the fee but brings on idle quarrels. |

> **按**:「木若逢空，药金莫望」的「木」是财爻（金在药上，财属木，木是金所克）——这里是说医生的酬谢（药金）靠财爻，财爻落空就没有酬金；原文没有明说「木」指财，这里按上下文理解。「非讼」是是非和词讼。这一条与易冒的往医（LY.ILL.YM.40：应克世者寡情、爻无财者薄赠）是同一件事，只是这里多讲了兄弟发动（同行争夺）和官化官（病又变）。
>
> **Note:** In 'if Wood is Void, do not look for medicine money' the 'Wood' is the Wealth line (Metal governs the medicine, Wealth belongs to Wood, and Wood is what Metal controls) — it means the doctor's fee (medicine money) depends on the Wealth line, and a Void Wealth line brings no fee; the original does not say outright that 'Wood' means Wealth, and it is taken so from the context. 'Quarrels and lawsuits' is disputes and legal action. It is the same matter as the Yi Mao's on going to treat (LY.ILL.YM.40: the Other controlling the Self means little feeling, no Wealth line means a meagre gift), except that here it adds Peer moving (fellows competing) and Pressure changing into Pressure (the illness changing again).

相关:LY.ILL.EL.27、LY.ILL.YM.40、LY.ILL.ZS.36、LY.ILL.HJC.62、LY.ILL.HJC.64

## LY.ILL.HJC.32 先看子孙：能克制官鬼，古人叫解神、又名福德，占病里就是医药；卦中无它则鬼无制伏、服药不效祷神不灵；要生旺有气，日辰动爻生扶则吉、冲坏则凶；占父母丈夫病不宜它发动

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:断病先看子孙爻。子孙能克制官鬼这个凶煞，古人叫它解神，又叫福德，占病时它又是医药。卦里没有子孙，鬼就没有东西制伏，吃药没有效果，祷告求神也不灵。子孙要得令有力，不要衰弱、被困、死、绝；日辰、动爻来生它、扶它就吉，来冲它、破坏它就凶。替父母或丈夫占病，子孙不宜发动：动了会伤克夫星，又克伤父母爻的原神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In judging illness look first at Output. Output can control and restrain the Pressure killer; the ancients called it the relieving spirit and also Output-virtue, and in divining illness it is also the medicine. If there is no Output in the hexagram, Pressure has nothing to restrain it: medicine has no effect and prayer to the spirits is not answered. Output should be in season and have force, not resting, trapped, dead or extinct; if the Day or a moving line generates and supports it, it is lucky, and if they clash and spoil it, unlucky. In divining the illness of a parent or a husband, Output should not move: moving, it harms and controls the husband star and also harms the Support of the Resource line.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6878 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6878*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 既明症候，当决安危，再把爻神搜索； | 已经弄明白了症候，就要判断安危，再去逐个搜查爻神； | Having understood the symptoms, one must decide danger or safety, and then search out the line-spirits one by one; |
| 个中玄妙，重加参考，方穷就里之精微。 | 其中的玄妙，要再三参考，才能穷尽里头的精微。 | the subtlety in it must be weighed again and again before its inner refinements are exhausted. |
| 先看子孙，最喜生扶拱合； | 先看子孙爻，最喜欢有生、有扶、有拱、有合； | First look at Output: it most wants generating, supporting, embracing and combining; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6879–6880 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6879–6880*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙能克制官杀，古人谓解神，又名福德，占病又为医药。 | 子孙能克制官鬼这个凶煞，古人叫它解神，又叫福德，占病时它又是医药。 | Output can control and restrain the Pressure killer; the ancients called it the relieving spirit and also Output-virtue, and in divining illness it is also the medicine. |
| 卦中无此，则鬼无制伏。 | 卦里没有它，鬼就没有东西制伏。 | If it is not in the hexagram, Pressure has nothing to restrain it. |
| 主服药不效，祷神不灵。 | 主吃药没有效果，祷告求神也不灵。 | It means medicine has no effect and prayer to the spirits is not answered. |
| 所以先宜看此。 | 所以要先看子孙。 | So one should look at this first. |
| 要生旺有气，不要休囚死绝。 | 它要得令有力，不要衰弱、被困、死、绝。 | It should be in season and have force, and not be resting, trapped, dead or extinct. |
| 日辰动爻生扶则吉，冲坏则凶。 | 日辰、动爻来生它、扶它就吉，来冲它、破坏它就凶。 | If the Day or a moving line generates and supports it, it is lucky; if they clash and spoil it, unlucky. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2362 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2362*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙能克制鬼煞，古人谓解神，又名福德。 | 子孙能克制鬼煞，古人叫它解神，又叫福德。 | Output can control and restrain the Pressure killer; the ancients called it the relieving spirit and also Output-virtue. |
| 占病又为医药，卦中无此财鬼无制，服药无效验，祷神不灵，所以先宜看此。 | 占病时它又是医药，卦里没有它，财爻和鬼爻就没有东西制约，吃药没有效验，祷告求神也不灵，所以要先看它。 | In divining illness it is also the medicine; if it is not in the hexagram, Wealth and Pressure are unrestrained, medicine has no effect and prayer to the spirits is not answered, and so one should look at it first. |
| 推占父母丈夫病不宜子孙发动，动则伤克夫星，又克伤父母之原神也。 | 替父母或丈夫占病，不宜子孙发动：动了会伤克夫星，又会克伤父母爻的原神。 | In divining the illness of a parent (the Resource line) or of a husband, Output should not move: moving, it harms and controls the husband star, and also harms and controls the Support of the Resource line. |

> **按**:「解神」是能解除灾厄的神，子孙克官鬼，所以叫解神；「福德」是子孙的另一个名字。「生扶拱合」是帮用神的四种办法：生是五行相生，扶和拱是同一五行的两个地支互相帮衬（《卜筮正宗》在别处举的例子：亥扶子、子拱亥），合是二合、三合、六合。旧注的「官杀」「鬼杀」，王洪绪写作「鬼煞」，是同一个意思。王洪绪比旧注多说了一条：替父母或丈夫占病，子孙不宜发动。丈夫用官鬼，父母的原神也是官鬼（官鬼生父母），所以子孙一动就克到病人依靠的那一爻；旧注没有这个限定。「财鬼无制」，旧注只说「鬼无制伏」，王洪绪多了一个财。
>
> **Note:** The 'relieving spirit' is the spirit that can relieve disaster; Output controls Pressure, hence the name. 'Output-virtue' (fude) is another name for Output. 'Generating, supporting, embracing and combining' are four ways of helping the Subject: generating is the generation of the five phases; supporting and embracing are two branches of the same phase helping each other (the Bushi Zhengzong gives elsewhere the examples Hai supports Zi and Zi embraces Hai); combining is the two-combine, the three-combine and the six-combine. The old commentary's 'guan sha' and 'gui sha' are written 'gui sha' by Wang Hongxu, and mean the same. Wang Hongxu adds one thing to the old commentary: in divining the illness of a parent or a husband, Output should not move. A husband takes Pressure, and the Support of the Resource line is also Pressure (Pressure generates Resource), so a moving Output controls the very line the patient depends on; the old commentary has no such limit. Of 'Wealth and Pressure unrestrained' the old commentary says only 'Pressure has nothing to restrain it', and Wang Hongxu adds Wealth.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2362 行:既明症候，当决安危，再把爻神搜索;异文(相似 0.93) 第 2362 行:个中之玄妙，重加参考，方穷就里之精微;同文 第 2362 行:先看子孙，最喜生扶拱合
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1197 行:既明症候当决安危，再把爻神搜索;异文(相似 0.93) 第 1197 行:个中之玄妙，重加参考，方穷就里之精微;同文 第 1197 行:先看子孙，最喜生扶拱合

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.40、LY.ILL.HJC.42、LY.ILL.HJC.52、LY.ILL.HJC.57

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.35 身临福德势虽险而堪医：「身」是一卦之体（旧注说是用爻，王洪绪说是月卦身），子孙临之决然无虞，纵然凶险用药必愈

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:一卦的「身」是整个卦的主体：身上坐着子孙（福德），一定没有忧患，病势再凶险也有救，用药医治一定痊愈。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The 'body' is the substance of the whole hexagram: if Output (Output-virtue) sits on it, there is surely nothing to fear; even if the illness looks dangerous it can be saved, and treated with medicine it will surely be cured.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6889 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6889*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身临福德，势虽险而堪医。 | 身上坐着福德，病势虽然凶险还是可以医治。 | The body sitting on Output-virtue: though the danger looks great, it can be treated. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6890 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6890*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用爻为身，乃一卦之体。 | 用爻就是身，是整个卦的主体。 | The Subject line is the body, the substance of the whole hexagram. |
| 若得子孙临之，决然无虞。 | 如果有子孙坐在它上面，一定没有忧患。 | If Output sits on it, there is surely nothing to fear. |
| 纵然病势凶险，自可有救，以药治之，必定痊愈。 | 就算病势凶险，自然有救，用药医治，一定痊愈。 | Even if the illness looks dangerous, it can be saved, and treated with medicine it will surely be cured. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2368 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2368*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 月卦身乃一卦之体，子孙临之，决然无虞，纵然病势凶险，用药可以痊愈。 | 月卦身是整个卦的主体，子孙坐在它上面，一定没有忧患，就算病势凶险，用药也可以痊愈。 | The month's hexagram Body is the substance of the whole hexagram; if Output sits on it there is surely nothing to fear, and even if the illness looks dangerous it can be cured with medicine. |

> **打架**:「身」指哪一爻：旧注这里说用爻就是身；王洪绪说是月卦身（按月起的卦身）。这一章的旧注在 LY.ILL.HJC.26 里另外讲「卦身持鬼」，把卦身当成另一样东西，所以旧注自己在这个字上也不一致。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which line the 'body' is: the old commentary here says the Subject line is the body; Wang Hongxu says it is the month's hexagram Body (the Body set by the month). In LY.ILL.HJC.26 the old commentary of this chapter speaks separately of 'a Pressure line on the hexagram Body' and treats the hexagram Body as something else, so the old commentary is itself not consistent about this word.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2368 行:身临福德，势虽险而堪医
- 《增删卜易》:同文 第 12686 行:身临福德，势虽险而堪医
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1206 行:身临福德，势虽险而堪医

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.26、LY.ILL.HJC.42

## LY.ILL.HJC.40 子孙空绝必乏调理之肥甘：子孙是药又是酒肉，落空、临死绝或不上卦则病中无肥甘调理；日辰或应爻带子孙生合世爻用爻则有人馈送食物资养

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙爻既是医药，又是酒肉。子孙落空亡、临在死绝上，或者不上卦，病中一定没有肥甘的东西调养，病也缠缠绵绵难好。如果日辰或者应爻带着子孙，来生、合世爻和用爻，一定有人送礼物、送吃的来补养。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Output line is both medicine and wine and meat. If Output is Void, sits on death or extinction, or is not in the hexagram, the patient surely lacks rich food to build him up during the illness, and the illness also lingers and is hard to cure. If the Day or the Other line carries Output and generates or combines with the Self and Subject lines, someone will surely send gifts or food to nourish him.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6907 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6907*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙空绝，必乏调理之肥甘。 | 子孙落空、临死绝，病中一定缺少调养的肥甘。 | Output Void or on extinction: the patient surely lacks the rich food that builds him up. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6908 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6908*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙固为医，又为酒肉。 | 子孙本来是医生，又是酒肉。 | Output is the doctor, and also wine and meat. |
| 若在空亡，或临死绝，或不上卦，其病必乏肥甘调理，亦缠绵难愈。 | 如果落在空亡，或者临在死绝，或者不上卦，这病一定缺少肥甘的调养，也缠缠绵绵难好。 | If it falls in Void, or sits on death or extinction, or is not in the hexagram, the illness surely lacks the building-up of rich food, and lingers and is hard to cure. |
| 若日辰应爻上带子孙，生合世爻用爻者，必有馈送礼物资养。 | 如果日辰或者应爻上带着子孙，来生、合世爻和用爻，一定有人送礼物来资助调养。 | If the Day or the Other line carries Output and generates or combines with the Self and Subject lines, there will surely be gifts sent to help nourish him. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2378 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2378*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙固为药，又为酒肉，若临死绝，或在空亡，或不上卦，病中必无肥甘调理。 | 子孙本来是药，又是酒肉，如果临在死绝，或者落在空亡，或者不上卦，病中一定没有肥甘的东西调养。 | Output is medicine, and also wine and meat; if it sits on death or extinction, or is Void, or is not in the hexagram, the patient surely has no rich food to build him up during the illness. |
| 或日辰或应爻带子孙生合用爻者，必有人馈送食物资养。 | 或者日辰、或者应爻带着子孙来生、合用爻的，一定有人送吃的来资助调养。 | If the Day or the Other line carries Output and generates or combines with the Subject line, someone will surely send food to help nourish him. |

> **按**:旧注说子孙是「医」（医生），王洪绪说是「药」。「肥甘」是油腻甘美的好东西，病中拿来补养。旧注多说了一句「亦缠绵难愈」，王洪绪没有。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary says Output is 'the doctor' and Wang Hongxu says it is 'the medicine'. 'Rich food' (feigan) is fat and sweet good things, used to build a patient up. The old commentary adds 'and it lingers and is hard to cure', which Wang Hongxu does not.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2378 行:子孙空绝，必乏调理之肥甘
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1221 行:子孙空绝，必乏调理之肥甘

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.HJC.55

## LY.ILL.HJC.42 身临福德岂宜父动来伤：占病以子孙为解神，身上临着是大吉，父母动来克伤仍为不美（王洪绪：父母有制则无妨）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占病把子孙当解神，身上临着它是大吉的兆头；如果父母爻发动来克伤它，仍然不好（王洪绪补一句：父母爻自己被制住就没关系）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining illness Output is the relieving spirit, and its sitting on the body is a very lucky sign; if the Resource line moves to control and harm it, it is still not good (Wang Hongxu adds: if the Resource line is itself restrained, there is no harm).

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6913 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6913*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身临福德，岂宜父动来伤。 | 身上临着福德，怎么能容父母发动来克伤它。 | The body sitting on Output-virtue: how could one allow a moving Resource line to come and harm it? |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6914 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6914*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病，以子孙为解神。 | 占病，把子孙当作解神。 | In divining illness, Output is taken as the relieving spirit. |
| 身若临之，大吉之兆。 | 身爻如果临着它，是大吉的兆头。 | If the body sits on it, it is a very lucky sign. |
| 如父母动来克伤，仍为不美。 | 如果父母发动来克伤它，仍然不好。 | If the Resource line moves to control and harm it, it is still not good. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2382 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2382*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病以子孙为解神，身临之大吉之兆。 | 占病把子孙当解神，身爻临着它是大吉的兆头。 | In divining illness Output is taken as the relieving spirit, and its sitting on the body is a very lucky sign. |
| 如父母动来克伤，仍为不美，如父母有制无妨。 | 如果父母发动来克伤它，仍然不好，如果父母爻自己被制住就没关系。 | If the Resource line moves to control and harm it, it is still not good; if the Resource line is itself restrained, there is no harm. |

> **按**:「身」指哪一爻，见 LY.ILL.HJC.35。这一条和它是一对：那里讲身临子孙是有救，这里讲父母动来克子孙就把这个好处破掉。王洪绪多了一句「父母有制无妨」。
>
> **Note:** For which line the 'body' is, see LY.ILL.HJC.35. This entry is a pair with it: there the body sitting on Output means rescue, and here a moving Resource line that controls Output spoils that advantage. Wang Hongxu adds 'if the Resource line is restrained there is no harm'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2382 行:身临福德，岂宜父动来伤
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1227 行:身临福德，岂宜父动来伤

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.35、LY.ILL.HJC.38

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.52 日加福去生扶药医则愈：日辰临子孙、生扶（拱合）用爻，必得药力而愈

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:日辰坐在子孙上，来生、扶用爻（王洪绪：或拱、合），病一定靠药力好起来。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Day sits on Output and generates and supports the Subject line (Wang Hongxu: or embraces or combines with it), the patient will surely recover through the power of medicine.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6948 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6948*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 日加福去生扶，药医则愈。 | 日辰加在子孙上，去生扶用爻，用药医治就会痊愈。 | The Day placed on Output and going to generate and support the Subject: treated with medicine, it heals. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6949 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6949*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 日辰临子孙，生扶用爻，必得药力而安也。 | 日辰坐在子孙上，来生、扶用爻，一定靠药力平安。 | The Day sitting on Output and generating and supporting the Subject line: he surely recovers through the power of medicine. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2402 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2402*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 日辰临子孙生扶拱合用爻，必得药力而愈。 | 日辰坐在子孙上，来生、扶、拱、合用爻，一定靠药力痊愈。 | The Day sitting on Output and generating, supporting, embracing or combining with the Subject line: he surely heals through the power of medicine. |

> **按**:「拱」的意思见 LY.ILL.HJC.32 的注。旧注写「生扶」，王洪绪写成「生扶拱合」。
>
> **Note:** For what 'embracing' means see the note of LY.ILL.HJC.32. The old commentary writes 'generating and supporting', Wang Hongxu 'generating, supporting, embracing and combining'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2402 行:日加福去生扶，药医则愈
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1256 行:日加福去生扶，药医则愈

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.HJC.40

## LY.ILL.HJC.58 占医药：子孙是药饵、应爻是医人；应空医不来，应动生合世则医从命，动空化空必他出，克世冲世皆不来；无子孙则药无效、空亦无功

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:病了不去求医，能保全性命的很少；药不对症，白白死掉的很多。占医药时，应爻是医生，子孙是药。请医生：应爻落空，医生一定不来；应爻发动来生合世爻，医生一定听请；动了又落空、变出来也是空，一定去了别处；克世、冲世，都是不来。卦里没有子孙，吃药没有效果，子孙落空也没有功效。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One who falls ill and does not seek a doctor seldom keeps his life whole, and one who takes medicine that does not fit the illness dies in vain more often than not. In divining medicine the Other line is the doctor and Output the medicine. In calling a doctor: if the Other line is Void, the doctor surely will not come; if it moves and generates or combines with the Self line, he surely obeys the call; if it moves, is Void and changes into Void, he has surely gone elsewhere; controlling or clashing the Self line, he will not come. With no Output in the hexagram medicine has no effect, and if Output is Void it does no good either.

**《黄金策·医药》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6980 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Doctors and medicine", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6980*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 病不求医，全生者寡； | 病了不去求医，能保全性命的很少； | One who falls ill and does not seek a doctor rarely keeps his life whole; |
| 药不对症，枉死者多。 | 药不对症，白白死掉的很多。 | medicine that does not fit the illness brings many to a death in vain. |
| 欲择善者而从之，须就蓍人而问也。 | 想要挑选好的来听从，就要去找懂蓍草占卜的人来问。 | To choose the good and follow it, one must go to a diviner of the yarrow and ask. |
| 应作医人，空则闲亡而不遇； | 应爻当作医生，落空就是闲散在外、遇不上； | The Other line is taken as the doctor; if Void, he is idle and away and cannot be met; |
| 子为药饵，伏则扞格以无功。 | 子孙当作药，伏藏起来就互相抵触而没有功效。 | Output is taken as the medicine; if hidden, it clashes and does no good. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6981–6983 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6981–6983*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡占病，以子孙为医药。 | 凡是占病，拿子孙当医药。 | In divining illness, Output is medicine. |
| 占医药，以应爻为医人，子孙为药饵。 | 占医药时，拿应爻当医生，子孙当药。 | In divining medicine, the Other line is the doctor and Output the medicine. |
| 如卜请医，应空，医人必不来。 | 比如卜请医生，应爻落空，医生一定不来。 | For example, in divining the calling of a doctor, if the Other line is Void, the doctor surely will not come. |
| 发动生合世，医必从命。 | 应爻发动来生、合世爻，医生一定听请。 | If it moves and generates or combines with the Self line, the doctor surely obeys the call. |
| 动空化空，必然他出。 | 动了又落空、变出来也是空，一定是出去了。 | If it moves, is Void and changes into Void, he has surely gone out elsewhere. |
| 克世冲世，皆主不来。 | 克世爻、冲世爻，都是不来。 | Controlling or clashing the Self line, in each case means he will not come. |
| 无子孙，服药无效，空亦无功。 | 没有子孙，吃药没有效果，子孙落空也没有功效。 | With no Output, medicine has no effect, and if Output is Void it does no good either. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2416–2418 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, lines 2416–2418*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡卜医药，以子孙爻为药饵，以应爻为医生。 | 凡是卜医药，拿子孙爻当药，拿应爻当医生。 | In all divining of medicine, the Output line is the medicine and the Other line is the doctor. |
| 如子孙受伤或墓绝，或官爻生旺，是药不对症，必不能去病。 | 如果子孙受伤或者入墓、入绝，或者官爻生旺，就是药不对症，一定不能去病。 | If Output is harmed or in a tomb or extinction, or the Pressure line is born and strong, the medicine does not fit the illness and surely cannot drive it out. |
| 如应爻旬空，医生非它出不来，定用药无效。 | 如果应爻落了旬空，医生要么出门了，要么不会来，用他的药一定无效。 | If the Other line falls into Void, the doctor either has gone elsewhere or will not come, and his medicine surely has no effect. |

> **按**:「蓍人」是用蓍草占卜的人，也就是占卜家。「药饵」是药和药食。「闲亡」是闲散在外、找不到人；王洪绪引经文时写作「瞷亡」，是不同的写法，书里没有解释。王洪绪比旧注多讲了一条：子孙受伤、入墓入绝，或官爻生旺，是药不对症。
>
> **Note:** A 'yarrow man' is one who divines with yarrow stalks, that is, a diviner. 'Medicine' (yao'er) is medicine and medicinal food. 'Idle and away' (xianwang) means loitering away and not to be found; where Wang Hongxu quotes the verse he writes it with a different character (jianwang), which the book does not explain. Wang Hongxu adds one thing to the old commentary: Output harmed, or in a tomb or extinction, or the Pressure line born and strong, means the medicine does not fit the illness.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2416 行:病不求医全生者寡;同文 第 2416 行:药不对症枉死者多;同文 第 2416 行:欲择善者而从之，须就蓍人而问也;异文(相似 0.91) 第 2416 行:应作医人，空则瞷亡而不遇;同文 第 2416 行:子为药饵，伏则扞格以无功
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1274 行:病不求医，全生者寡;同文 第 1274 行:药不对症，枉死者多;同文 第 1274 行:欲择善者而从之，须就蓍人而问也;同文 第 1274 行:应作医人，空则闲亡而不遇;异文(相似 0.91) 第 1274 行:子为药饵，伏则扦格以无功

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.HJC.59、LY.ILL.HJC.90
