## LY.ILL.HJC.89 五兴化福可用路遇医人：子孙出现逢空而第五爻变出不空的子孙，不必选求名医，路遇的草医反能治；子孙不出现、六爻不化出而日辰带子孙生合，意外自有医来治

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙出现在卦里却落空，而第五爻动、变出不落空的子孙，不用选求名医服药，路上遇到的乡野医生反而能治好。如果子孙不出现、六爻也没有变出子孙，而日辰带着子孙来生合，想不到的时候自然有医生来治。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Output appears in the hexagram but is Void, and the fifth line moves and changes out an Output that is not Void, there is no need to seek a famous doctor and take medicine: a rustic doctor met on the road can cure him instead. If Output does not appear and none of the six lines changes it out, but the Day carries Output and generates or combines with it, a doctor will come to treat him unexpectedly.

**《黄金策·医药》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7085 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Doctors and medicine", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7085*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 五兴化福，可用路遇医人。 | 第五爻发动、变出子孙，可以用路上遇到的医生。 | The fifth line rising and changing into Output: a doctor met on the road may be used. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7086 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7086*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙出现逢空，而五爻变出子孙，却不空者，不须选求名医服药，不如过路草医，反能治之。 | 子孙出现却落空，而第五爻变出子孙、不落空的，不用选求名医服药，不如路过的乡野医生，反而能治好。 | If Output appears but is Void, while the fifth line changes out Output that is not Void, there is no need to seek a famous doctor and take medicine: a rustic doctor who passes by can cure him instead. |
| 若子孙不出现，六爻不化出，而日辰带子孙生合者，不意中自有医来治也。 | 如果子孙不出现，六爻也没有变出子孙，而日辰带着子孙来生合，想不到的时候自然有医生来治。 | If Output does not appear and none of the six lines changes it out, but the Day carries Output and generates or combines with it, a doctor will come to treat him unexpectedly. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2480 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2480*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如卦中第五爻变出子孙，不须选医服药，不如路遇草医能治。 | 如果卦里第五爻变出子孙，不用选医生服药，不如路上遇到的乡野医生能治好。 | If the fifth line of the hexagram changes out Output, there is no need to choose a doctor and take medicine; a rustic doctor met on the road can cure him. |
| 若子孙不现，而日辰临子孙生合者，意外自有医生可治也。 | 如果子孙不出现，而日辰临着子孙来生合，想不到的时候自然有医生可以治。 | If Output does not appear but the Day sits on Output and generates or combines with it, a doctor who can treat him will come unexpectedly. |

> **按**:旧注的条件比王洪绪细：子孙出现而空，第五爻变出的子孙不空。王洪绪只说第五爻变出子孙。「草医」是乡间没有名气的医生。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary's condition is finer than Wang Hongxu's: Output appears and is Void, and the Output changed out at the fifth line is not Void. Wang Hongxu says only that the fifth line changes out Output. A 'rustic doctor' is a country doctor of no repute.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2480 行:五兴化福，可用路遇医人
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1366 行:五兴化福，可用路遇医人

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.58、LY.ILL.HJC.77

## LY.ILL.HJC.90 世应比和无福德须用更医：世应比和而卦无福德，此药无损无益服与不服一样，须更换医人别求治疗才可痊

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻和应爻比和，卦里又没有子孙，这药没有损害也没有益处，吃不吃都一样，必须换一个医生，另外求治，才能痊愈。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Self and Other lines are at peace with each other and there is no Output in the hexagram, this medicine neither harms nor helps, and to take it or not is the same; he must change the doctor and seek treatment elsewhere before he can be cured.

**《黄金策·医药》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7088 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Doctors and medicine", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7088*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世应比和无福德，须用更医； | 世爻和应爻比和、又没有福德，必须换医生； | The Self and Other lines at peace and no Output: a change of doctor is needed; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7089 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7089*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世应比和，卦无福德，此药无损无益，服与不服，只是一般。 | 世爻和应爻比和，卦里没有福德，这药没有损害也没有益处，吃不吃都是一样。 | The Self and Other lines at peace with each other and no Output in the hexagram: this medicine neither harms nor helps, and to take it or not is the same. |
| 须是更换医人，别求治疗，方可得痊。 | 必须换一个医生，另外求治，才可以痊愈。 | He must change the doctor and seek treatment elsewhere before he can be cured. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2482 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2482*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世应比和，卦无福德，此药无损无益，须更换医人方可得痊。 | 世爻和应爻比和，卦里没有福德，这药没有损害也没有益处，要换医生才可以痊愈。 | The Self and Other lines at peace with each other and no Output in the hexagram: this medicine neither harms nor helps, and he must change the doctor to be cured. |

> **按**:王洪绪这一句和下一条（LY.ILL.HJC.91）在同一段里，他把两条经文接着注。
>
> **Note:** Wang Hongxu's sentence and the next entry (LY.ILL.HJC.91) are in the same paragraph, where he comments on the two verses one after the other.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2482 行:世应比和无福德，须用更医
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1369 行:世应比和无福德，须用更医

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.58、LY.ILL.HJC.91

## LY.ILL.HJC.91 财官发动子孙空徒劳服药：财爻官鬼都动其势已凶，子孙又空则服之无益，纵有良医也无功效，不如不服渐愈

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:财爻、官鬼都发动，势头已经凶险，子孙又落空，吃药没有益处，就算有好医生也一定没有功效，不如不吃，病反而渐渐好起来。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When Wealth and Pressure both move, the situation is already bad, and if Output is also Void, taking medicine does no good; even with a good doctor there will surely be no effect, and it is better not to take it, and he will gradually recover.

**《黄金策·医药》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7091 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Doctors and medicine", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7091*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财官发动子孙空，徒劳服药。 | 财爻、官鬼发动，子孙落空，白白费力吃药。 | Wealth and Pressure moving and Output Void: taking medicine is labour in vain. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7093 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7093*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财官俱动，其势已凶。 | 财爻、官鬼都动，势头已经凶险。 | Wealth and Pressure both moving, the situation is already bad. |
| 子孙又空，服之无益； | 子孙又落空，服药没有益处； | Output is also Void, and taking it does no good; |
| 纵有良医，必无功效。 | 就算有好医生，也一定没有功效。 | even with a good doctor there will surely be no effect. |
| 不如不服之渐愈。 | 不如不吃，病还会渐渐好。 | Better not to take it, and he will gradually recover. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2482 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2482*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财官俱动，其势已凶，子孙又空，服之无益。 | 财爻、官鬼都动，势头已经凶险，子孙又落空，吃药没有益处。 | Wealth and Pressure both moving, the situation is already bad, and Output is also Void: taking medicine does no good. |

> **按**:旧注末尾多一句「不如不服之渐愈」，王洪绪没有。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary has one more sentence at the end, 'better not to take it, and he will gradually recover', which Wang Hongxu lacks.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2482 行:财官发动子孙空，徒劳服药
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1372 行:财官发动子孙空，徒劳服药

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.59、LY.ILL.HJC.90

## LY.ILL.HJC.92 占医药须诚心默祷，问用何人的药有效无效，不必说明姓氏；卜家据此章而断，自无荐医之弊（王洪绪）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:凡是占医药，要诚心默默祷告，问用哪个人的药有效还是无效，不必说出医生的姓氏。卜家依照这一章来断，自然没有替医生推荐的毛病，心诚没有不感通的，卦没有不灵验的，这不是彼此都安心吗？

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Whoever divines about medicine must pray silently and sincerely, asking whether the medicine of a certain person will or will not be effective, and need not state the doctor's surname. A diviner who judges by this chapter will have no abuse of touting doctors; sincerity never fails to reach, and the hexagram never fails to prove true; is that not peace of mind for both sides?

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2483 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2483*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡占医药者，须诚心默祷，用何人药有效无效，不必说明姓氏。 | 凡是占医药的，要诚心默默祷告，问用哪个人的药有效还是无效，不必说出他的姓氏。 | Whoever divines about medicine must pray silently and sincerely, asking whether the medicine of a certain person will or will not be effective, and need not state his surname. |
| 卜家据此章而断，自无荐医之弊，则诚无不格，卦无不验矣，岂非彼此安心乎？ | 卜家依照这一章来断，自然没有替医生推荐的毛病，那么诚心没有不感通的，卦没有不灵验的，这不是彼此都安心吗？ | A diviner who judges by this chapter will have no abuse of touting doctors; sincerity then never fails to reach, and the hexagram never fails to prove true; is that not peace of mind for both sides? |

> **按**:这一段是王洪绪自己加在医药章末尾的话，卜筮大全没有对应的文字。「荐医之弊」是占卜的人借断卦替某位医生说好话、推荐他的毛病。
>
> **Note:** This passage is Wang Hongxu's own addition at the end of the chapter on medicine, and the Bushi Daquan has no matching text. The 'abuse of touting doctors' is the fault of a diviner who uses his judgment of the hexagram to speak well of a certain doctor and recommend him.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.58

---

## LY.ILL.HZL.06 占医药·以应为医以子为药、鬼爻旺相大忌独发；卦之疾病以用为主以鬼为病；金鬼肺腑喘嗽、木鬼四肢不遂肝胆、水鬼沉寒腰痛肾气吐泻、火鬼头痛发热加朱雀狂言、土鬼脾胃发胀黄肿瘟疫

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:以应为医生，以子孙为药；鬼爻旺相，最忌独发。卦里的疾病，以用爻为主，以鬼为病。金鬼：肺腑病、喘嗽、气急、虚怯、瘦瘠，或疮疥、血光，或筋骨病；木鬼：四肢不遂，肝胆主病，右痪左瘫，口眼歪斜；水鬼：沉塞、痼冷、腰痛、肾气淋沥、遗精、白浊、吐泻；火鬼：头痛发热、心胸焦渴，加朱雀则狂言谵语，阳症、伤寒、嗝逆；土鬼：脾胃发胀，黄肿虚浮，瘟疫时气。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Take the Other as the doctor and Output as the medicine; the Pressure line strong is most feared moving alone. The illness of the hexagram takes the Subject line as the main and the ghost as the illness. A Metal ghost: illness of the lungs and viscera, panting and coughing, shortness of breath, deficiency and timidity, emaciation, or sores and scabies, bleeding, or an illness of the sinews and bones; a Wood ghost: the four limbs do not obey, the liver and gallbladder govern the illness, paralysis right and left, mouth and eyes awry; a Water ghost: stoppage, obstinate cold, pain in the waist, dribbling of the kidney qi, seminal emission, turbid urine, vomiting and diarrhoea; a Fire ghost: headache and fever, parched heart and chest, with the Vermilion Bird raving and delirium, a yang pattern, cold damage, hiccups; an Earth ghost: distension of the spleen and stomach, yellow swelling and puffiness, epidemics and seasonal qi.

**《火珠林·占医药》原文** — 《火珠林》第 462 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining doctors and medicine", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 462*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以应为医， | 以应为医生， | Take the Other as the doctor |
| 以子为药； | 以子孙为药； | and Output as the medicine; |
| 鬼爻旺相， | 鬼爻旺相， | the Pressure line strong |
| 大忌独发。 | 最忌独发。 | is most feared moving alone. |

**《火珠林·占医药》原文** — 《火珠林》第 463 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining doctors and medicine", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 463*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫卦之疾病， | 卦里的疾病， | The illness of the hexagram |
| 以用为主， | 以用爻为主， | takes the Subject line as the main |
| 以鬼为病。 | 以鬼为病。 | and the ghost as the illness. |

**《火珠林·占医药》原文** — 《火珠林》第 464 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining doctors and medicine", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 464*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金鬼，肺腑疾、喘嗽、气急、虚怯、瘦瘠、或疮疥、血光，或筋骨病。 | 金鬼：肺腑病、喘嗽、气急、虚怯、瘦瘠，或者疮疥、血光，或者筋骨病。 | A Metal ghost: illness of the lungs and viscera, panting and coughing, shortness of breath, deficiency and timidity, emaciation, or sores and scabies, bleeding, or an illness of the sinews and bones. |
| 木鬼，四肢不遂。肝胆主病、右痪左瘫、口眼歪斜。 | 木鬼：四肢不听使唤；肝胆主病，右侧偏瘫、左侧瘫痪，口眼歪斜。 | A Wood ghost: the four limbs do not obey; the liver and gallbladder govern the illness, paralysis right and left, mouth and eyes awry. |

**《火珠林·占医药》原文** — 《火珠林》第 465 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining doctors and medicine", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 465*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水鬼，沉塞、痼冷、腰痛、肾气淋沥、遗精、白浊、吐泻。 | 水鬼：阻塞不通、顽固的寒冷、腰痛、肾气不固而小便淋沥、遗精、小便浑浊、呕吐腹泻。 | A Water ghost: stoppage, obstinate cold, pain in the waist, dribbling of the kidney qi, seminal emission, turbid urine, vomiting and diarrhoea. |

**《火珠林·占医药》原文** — 《火珠林》第 466 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining doctors and medicine", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 466*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火鬼，头痛发热、心胸焦渴、加朱雀狂言谵语、阳症、伤寒、嗝逆。 | 火鬼：头痛发热、心胸焦渴，加上朱雀则狂言谵语，还有阳症、伤寒、打嗝气逆。 | A Fire ghost: headache and fever, parched heart and chest, with the Vermilion Bird raving and delirium, a yang pattern, cold damage, hiccups. |
| 土鬼，脾胃发胀；黄肿虚浮、瘟疫时气。 | 土鬼：脾胃胀满；发黄、浮肿、虚浮，还有瘟疫和时令的病气。 | An Earth ghost: distension of the spleen and stomach; yellow swelling and puffiness, epidemics and seasonal qi. |

> **按**:「以应为医，以子为药」的分工和天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.12）、阐奥歌章医药章（LY.ILL.CA.09：应爻及外为医者、内卦身爻是病人）是同一套，这里说医是应、药是子孙。五行鬼的症候是天玄赋疾病章那一段（LY.ILL.TXF.03）的另一份，字句略有出入：天玄赋是「金鬼：肺腑病，咳嗽气，或疮疖、血光，或筋骨疼痛」，这里是「金鬼，肺腑疾、喘嗽、气急、虚怯、瘦瘠、或疮疥、血光，或筋骨病」，多了气急、虚怯、瘦瘠几项；水鬼这里有「沉塞、痼冷、腰痛、肾气淋沥、遗精、白浊、吐泻」，天玄赋是「沉寒痼冷，遗精白浊，小便淋沥，吐泻呕逆」。「嗝逆」按「打嗝、气逆」译。「阳症」是中医的阳证。这是中医术语，按字面译。
>
> **Note:** The division of 'the Other as the doctor, Output as the medicine' is the same set as the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.12) and the Chan'ao Gezhang's chapter on medicine (LY.ILL.CA.09: the Other line and the outer trigram are the doctor, the inner trigram and the Body line are the sick person), except that here the doctor is the Other and the medicine is Output. The symptoms of the ghost of each phase are another copy of that passage of the Tianxuan Fu's chapter on illness (LY.ILL.TXF.03), with small differences in wording: the Tianxuan Fu has 'a Metal ghost: illness of the lungs and viscera, coughing and qi, or sores and boils, bleeding, or aching of the sinews and bones', and this has 'a Metal ghost, illness of the lungs and viscera, panting and coughing, shortness of breath, deficiency and timidity, emaciation, or sores and scabies, bleeding, or an illness of the sinews and bones', with shortness of breath, deficiency and timidity and emaciation added; for the Water ghost this has 'stoppage, obstinate cold, pain in the waist, dribbling of the kidney qi, seminal emission, turbid urine, vomiting and diarrhoea', where the Tianxuan Fu has 'deep cold and obstinate chill, seminal emission and turbid urine, dribbling urine, vomiting, diarrhoea and retching'. 'Ge ni' is rendered 'hiccups'. A 'yang pattern' is the traditional medical yang pattern. These are terms of Chinese medicine and are rendered literally.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:异文(相似 0.79) 第 4967 行:土鬼：乃脾胃发瘴，水肿虚浮，瘟疫时气
- 《断易天机》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1610 行:土鬼：乃脾胃发胀，水肿，虚浮，瘟疫时气

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.03、LY.ILL.TXF.12、LY.ILL.CA.09、LY.ILL.HZL.07

## LY.ILL.HZL.09 占医药·金鬼不宜针、木鬼不宜草木、水鬼不宜汤饮汤洗、火鬼不宜灸熨、土鬼不宜服丸药；金鬼可炙木鬼药方水鬼宜热剂土鬼宜木药；金鬼利南方木鬼利西方水鬼利土值火鬼利北方土鬼利东方求请医者；丑鬼不可食牛肉未子孙当食羊

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:金鬼不宜针，木鬼不宜草木，水鬼不宜汤饮汤洗之类，火鬼不宜灸熨，土鬼不宜服丸药；金鬼可以炙，木鬼用药方，火鬼带服鬼剂，水鬼宜服热剂，土鬼宜服木药。金鬼利南方，木鬼利西方，水鬼利土值，火鬼利北方，土鬼利东方，去求请医生。又：丑鬼不可食牛肉，未是子孙就该吃羊肉。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Metal ghost must not be needled, a Wood ghost must not have grasses and woods, a Water ghost must not have hot drinks or hot washing and the like, a Fire ghost must not have moxibustion or hot compresses, an Earth ghost must not take pills; a Metal ghost can be burned with moxa, a Wood ghost takes a prescription, a Fire ghost takes ghost medicine along, a Water ghost should take a hot remedy, an Earth ghost should take Wood medicine. A Metal ghost is favoured by the south, a Wood ghost by the west, a Water ghost by the earth quarter, a Fire ghost by the north and an Earth ghost by the east, in seeking and inviting a doctor. Again: a Chou ghost must not eat beef, and where Wei is Output one should eat mutton.

**《火珠林·占医药》原文** — 《火珠林》第 470 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining doctors and medicine", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 470*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金鬼不宜针， | 金鬼不宜针， | A Metal ghost must not be needled, |
| 木鬼不宜草木， | 木鬼不宜草木， | a Wood ghost must not have grasses and woods, |
| 水鬼不宜汤饮汤洗之类， | 水鬼不宜用热汤饮用、热汤洗浴之类， | a Water ghost must not have hot drinks or hot washing and the like, |
| 火鬼不宜灸熨， | 火鬼不宜灸熨， | a Fire ghost must not have moxibustion or hot compresses, |
| 土鬼不宜服丸药。 | 土鬼不宜服丸药。 | an Earth ghost must not take pills. |
| 金鬼可炙， | 金鬼可以炙， | A Metal ghost can be burned with moxa, |
| 木鬼药方， | 木鬼用药方， | a Wood ghost takes a prescription, |
| 火鬼带服鬼剂， | 火鬼带服鬼剂， | a Fire ghost takes ghost medicine along, |
| 水鬼宜服热剂， | 水鬼宜服热剂， | a Water ghost should take a hot remedy, |
| 土鬼宜服木药。 | 土鬼宜服木药。 | an Earth ghost should take Wood medicine. |

**《火珠林·占医药》原文** — 《火珠林》第 471 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining doctors and medicine", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 471*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金鬼利南方， | 金鬼利南方， | A Metal ghost is favoured by the south, |
| 木鬼利西方， | 木鬼利西方， | a Wood ghost by the west, |
| 水鬼利土值， | 水鬼利土值， | a Water ghost by the earth quarter, |
| 火鬼利北方， | 火鬼利北方， | a Fire ghost by the north, |
| 土鬼利东方求请医者。 | 土鬼利东方，去求请医生。 | and an Earth ghost by the east, in seeking and inviting a doctor. |
| 又， | 另外： | Again: |
| 丑鬼不可(食)牛肉， | 丑鬼不可（食）牛肉， | a Chou ghost must not eat beef, |
| 未子孙当食羊。 | 未子孙当食羊。 | and where Wei is Output one should eat mutton. |

> **打架**:治法和请医的方位，火珠林和天玄赋大体一致，有两处不同。一、土鬼：天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.13）是「土鬼宜锉散」，火珠林这里是「土鬼宜服木药」「土鬼不宜服丸药」；二、水鬼请医的方位：天玄赋是「水鬼利四散医人」，火珠林这里是「水鬼利土值」。增删卜易（LY.ILL.ZS.34）另有一说，请医的方位不看鬼的五行，看子孙和应爻的方位（见 TXF.13 的「打架」）。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The treatment and the direction of the doctor are broadly agreed between the Huozhu Lin and the Tianxuan Fu, with two differences. One, the Earth ghost: the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.13) has 'an Earth ghost is fit for cut powders', and the Huozhu Lin here has 'an Earth ghost should take Wood medicine' and 'an Earth ghost must not take pills'; two, the direction for a doctor for a Water ghost: the Tianxuan Fu has 'a Water ghost is favoured by a doctor from all quarters' and the Huozhu Lin here has 'a Water ghost by the earth quarter'. The Zengshan Buyi (LY.ILL.ZS.34) has yet another view, that the direction of the doctor is looked at not by the phase of the ghost but by the quarter of Output and the Other line (see the dispute in TXF.13). The books do not settle it.

> **按**:这一段和天玄赋的医药一节（LY.ILL.TXF.13）相近：天玄赋是「火鬼宜凉剂，水鬼宜温剂或丸药，木鬼宜针，金鬼宜灸，土鬼宜锉散……金鬼利南方医人，木鬼利西方医人，水鬼利四散医人，火鬼利北方医人，土鬼利东方医人」。两处大部分一致（金宜灸不宜针、水宜热剂、请医的方位是克这一行的方位），不一致的是土鬼：天玄赋说宜锉散，这里说宜木药、不宜丸药；水鬼的方位，天玄赋是「四散」，这里是「土值」（字面译作 the earth quarter，「值」的意思原文没有解释，可能是「值土」）。「金鬼可炙，木鬼药方，火鬼带服鬼剂」中「带服鬼剂」的「鬼剂」不清楚，字面译作 ghost medicine。「丑鬼不可（食）牛肉」的「（食）」是原文里的圆括号。忌食鬼爻所属生肖的肉、宜食子孙所属生肖的肉，和洞林秘诀（LY.ILL.DYTJ.16）是同一个说法。
>
> **Note:** This passage is close to the Tianxuan Fu's section on medicine (LY.ILL.TXF.13): the Tianxuan Fu has 'a Fire ghost is fit for a cooling remedy, a Water ghost for a warm remedy or pills, a Wood ghost for the needle, a Metal ghost for moxa, an Earth ghost for cut powders ... a Metal ghost is favoured by a doctor from the south, a Wood ghost by one from the west, a Water ghost by one from all quarters, a Fire ghost by one from the north, an Earth ghost by one from the east'. They mostly agree (moxa for Metal and no needle, a hot remedy for Water, and the direction of the doctor being the direction that controls the phase); where they differ is the Earth ghost: the Tianxuan Fu says cut powders, this says Wood medicine and not pills; and for the Water ghost's direction the Tianxuan Fu has 'all quarters' and this has 'the earth quarter' (rendered literally, the original does not explain 'zhi' (rule, hold), which may be 'ruled by Earth'). The 'ghost medicine' of 'a Fire ghost takes ghost medicine along' is not clear and is rendered literally. The '(shi)' of 'a Chou ghost must not (eat) beef' is a round bracket in the original. Avoiding the meat of the animal of the ghost line and eating the meat of the animal of Output is the same saying as the Dongling Mijue (LY.ILL.DYTJ.16).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:异文(相似 0.83) 第 1713 行:火鬼不宜灸;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1713 行:土鬼不宜服药

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.13、LY.ILL.ZS.34、LY.ILL.DYTJ.16、LY.ILL.HZL.08、LY.ILL.HZL.10

## LY.ILL.TXF.12 医药：伏克飞爻药奏通神之效、子临应位医逢济世之才；世为病人、应及外卦为医药，药克病则愈、病克药则药无灵，外卦及应生世药虽对症作效迟；世动克药按五行（水鬼服药不纳、金鬼久病无效、木鬼犯条贯亦无益、火鬼不能止热、土鬼不对症），应加鬼克世误药损人；子孙为医药临应动克世必遇良医、卦无子孙服药无效、世应比和无子宜换医、子孙临应被世克虽遇良医病未疗、外卦及应克世无福德难安、虎杀临子孙动防医不精

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:伏爻克飞爻，药奏神效；子孙临在应位，医生遇上济世之才。注说：世是病人，应和外卦是医药；药如果克病，病就好；病人克药，药就没有灵验；外卦和应生世，药虽然对症，见效迟。世动克药：如果是水鬼，服药不纳；金鬼无气、久病犯真（此处含混），服药无效；木鬼，病一定犯了条贯，即使有药也没有益处；火鬼旺相，症一定刚烈，药也止不住它的热；土鬼，病沉重，药不对症，不能治病；应爻加鬼克世，那就是误了药、损了人。占病以子孙为医药：临应发动克世，必遇良医，病马上痊愈；卦里没有子孙，服药无效；世应比和、卦里又没有子孙，宜换医生；子孙临应爻被世克，虽遇良医病也没有治；外卦和应虽克世，卦里没有福德也难安，可以说有药应当治，只恐怕良医没有缘；子孙虽克世，外爻和应却不克，虽有良医，药饵不中，终究没有大害；虎杀如果临着子孙发动，要防医生不精细。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The hidden line controlling the flying line: the medicine works with wondrous effect; Output sitting on the Other's place: the doctor meets one of world-saving talent. The commentary says: the Self is the sick person, the Other line and the outer trigram are the doctor and the medicine; if the medicine controls the illness, the illness is cured at once; if the sick person controls the medicine, the medicine has no effect; if the outer trigram and the Other generate the Self, though the medicine suits the symptoms it takes effect slowly. The Self moving and controlling the medicine: if it is a Water ghost, the medicine taken is not kept down; a Metal ghost without qi, in a long illness that has offended 'the true' (the words are unclear), taking medicine is ineffective; a Wood ghost: the illness surely offends the regulations, and even medicine is of no benefit; a strong Fire ghost: the symptoms are surely fierce and even medicine cannot stop the heat; an Earth ghost: the illness is heavy, the medicine does not suit the symptoms and cannot treat the illness; the Other line with a ghost added controlling the Self is the wrong medicine harming the person. In divining illness Output is the medicine and doctor: sitting on the Other and moving to control the Self, one meets a good doctor and the illness is cured at once; with no Output in the hexagram, taking medicine is ineffective; with the Self and Other harmonious and no Output in the hexagram, one should change the doctor; Output on the Other line controlled by the Self, though one meets a good doctor the illness is not treated; the outer trigram and the Other though controlling the Self, if the hexagram has no Output (the fortune spirit) it is still hard to be at peace, and one may say there is medicine that should treat it but fear the good doctor has no affinity; Output though controlling the Self, if the outer line and the Other do not control it, though there is a good doctor and the medicine does not hit, in the end there is no great harm; if a Tiger killing sits on Output and moves, beware the doctor not being careful.

**《天玄赋·天玄赋·十七、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5012 行 · *Tianxuan Fu (Rhapsody of the Heavenly Mystery), "Tianxuan Fu, chapter 17: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5012*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 伏克飞爻， | 伏爻克了飞爻， | The hidden line controlling the flying line, |
| 药奏通神之效； | 药起到通神的效果； | the medicine works with wondrous effect; |
| 子临应位， | 子孙临在应的位置上， | Output sitting on the Other's place, |
| 医逢济世之才。 | 医生遇上济世的人才。 | the doctor meets one of world-saving talent. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5013 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5013*

| 世动克药，若是水鬼，服药不纳； | 世动克药：如果是水鬼，服药不纳； | The Self moving and controlling the medicine: if it is a Water ghost, the medicine taken is not kept down; |

| 土鬼，其病沉重，药不对症，不能疗病。 | 土鬼，病沉重，药不对症，不能治病。 | an Earth ghost: the illness is heavy, the medicine does not suit the symptoms and cannot treat the illness. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5014 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5014*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病以子孙为医药，临应动克世，必遇良医，其病即痊。 | 占病以子孙为医药：临应发动克世，必遇良医，病马上痊愈； | In divining illness Output is the medicine and doctor: sitting on the Other and moving to control the Self, one meets a good doctor and the illness is cured at once; |
| 卦无子孙，服药无效。 | 卦里没有子孙，服药无效； | with no Output in the hexagram, taking medicine is ineffective; |
| 世应比和，卦无子孙，宜换医人。 | 世应比和、卦里又没有子孙，宜换医生； | with the Self and Other harmonious and no Output in the hexagram, one should change the doctor; |
| 子孙临应被世克，虽遇良医病未疗。 | 子孙临应爻被世克，虽遇良医病也没有治； | Output on the Other line controlled by the Self, though one meets a good doctor the illness is not treated; |
| 外卦及应虽克世，卦无福德亦难安；可言有药须当疗，但恐良医未有缘。 | 外卦和应虽然克世，卦里没有福德也难安，可以说有药应当治，只恐怕良医没有缘； | the outer trigram and the Other though controlling the Self, if the hexagram has no Output (the fortune spirit) it is still hard to be at peace, and one may say there is medicine that should treat it but fear the good doctor has no affinity; |
| 子孙虽克世，外爻及应却不克，虽有良医，药饵不中，终无大害。 | 子孙虽克世，外爻和应却不克，虽有良医，药饵不中，终究没有大害； | Output though controlling the Self, if the outer line and the Other do not control it, though there is a good doctor and the medicine does not hit, in the end there is no great harm; |
| 虎杀若临子孙动，须防医者不精详。 | 虎杀如果临着子孙发动，要防医生不精细。 | if a Tiger killing sits on Output and moves, beware the doctor not being careful. |

> **按**:这一节是医药：「世为病人，应及外卦为医药」——世是病人、应和外卦（应爻和外卦）是医和药；药克病则愈、病克药则无灵、外卦及应生世则药对症但效迟。这个「应为医、世为病人、子孙为药」的三角和易隐「医药·药效」（LY.ILL.YY.21）、易林补遗「求医疗病」（LY.ILL.EL.26）、增删卜易（LY.ILL.ZS.29）一致；「子孙为医药」和易冒「占医药以子孙为用」（LY.ILL.YM.34）相同。五行鬼的一段（水鬼服药不纳、金鬼无效、木鬼犯条贯、火鬼刚症、土鬼沉重）和黄金策的医药（LY.ILL.HJC.7x 一带）对得上。「久病犯真」文字含混，按字面译并标出；「条贯」是条规，「病必犯条贯」按字面译作「the illness surely offends the regulations」（可能指病是犯了什么规矩得的，书里没有解释）；「虎杀」是白虎之类的凶煞，这里写「虎杀」而不写「白虎」，译作 Tiger killing。「误药损人」是药用错了，伤了人。 卜筮大全的这一章后面还有「虎易评注」（今人加的），本库不引。
>
> **Note:** This section is about medicine: 'the Self is the sick person, the Other and the outer trigram are the doctor and the medicine'; the medicine controlling the illness cures, the sick person controlling the medicine leaves it without effect, and the outer trigram and the Other generating the Self make the medicine suit the symptoms but act slowly. This triangle 'the Other is the doctor, the Self the sick person, Output the medicine' agrees with the Yi Yin's 'medicine: the effect of the medicine' (LY.ILL.YY.21), the Yilin Buyi's 'seeking a doctor to treat illness' (LY.ILL.EL.26) and the Zengshan Buyi (LY.ILL.ZS.29); and 'Output is the medicine and doctor' is the same as the Yi Mao's 'divining medicine takes Output as the Subject' (LY.ILL.YM.34). The passage on the ghosts of the five phases (Water ghost the medicine not kept down, Metal ghost without effect, Wood ghost offending the regulations, Fire ghost a fierce condition, Earth ghost heavy) matches the Huangjin Ce's medicine passages (LY.ILL.HJC.7x and around). 'A long illness that has offended the true' is unclear, and is translated literally and marked; 'tiao guan' is regulation, and 'the illness surely offends the regulations' is rendered literally (it may mean that the illness was got by breaking some rule, which the book does not explain); 'Tiger killing' is a malign killing such as the White Tiger, and the text writes 'Tiger killing' and not 'White Tiger', so it is translated as Tiger killing. 'The wrong medicine harming the person' is medicine wrongly used and injuring the person. After this chapter the Buyi Daquan also has 'Huyi's commentary' (added by a modern hand), which this library does not quote.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1633 行:药奏通神之效;同文 第 1633 行:医逢济世之才
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1868 行:药奏通神之效;同文 第 1868 行:医逢济世之才

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.11、LY.ILL.TXF.13、LY.ILL.YY.21、LY.ILL.EL.26、LY.ILL.ZS.29、LY.ILL.YM.34、LY.ILL.HJC.76

## LY.ILL.TXF.13 必须参究五行方可攻医万病：火鬼宜凉剂、水鬼宜温剂或丸药、木鬼宜针、金鬼宜灸、土鬼宜剉散（火鬼月建旺宜大寒之剂、无气不过清凉）；金鬼利南方医人、木鬼利西方、水鬼利四散、火鬼利北方、土鬼利东方；前卦水鬼后卦火鬼先须四散医治、终于北方医人收功

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:必须钻研五行，才可以攻医各种病。注说：火鬼宜凉剂；水鬼宜温剂，或者丸药；木鬼宜针；金鬼宜灸；土鬼宜剉散。火鬼在月建旺的时候，宜大寒之剂；无气，不过用清凉的药。其余照这样推。金鬼利于请南方的医生，木鬼利西方的医生，水鬼利四散的医生，火鬼利北方的医生，土鬼利东方的医生。如果前一卦是水鬼、后一卦是火鬼，先要四散地医治，最后由北方的医生收功。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One must study the five phases before one can attack and treat the myriad illnesses. The commentary says: a Fire ghost suits cooling remedies; a Water ghost suits warming remedies, or pills; a Wood ghost suits the needle; a Metal ghost suits moxa; an Earth ghost suits cut-and-ground powders. When a Fire ghost is strong on the Month, it suits very cold remedies; without qi, only cooling and clearing medicines; the rest follow this. A Metal ghost favours a doctor from the south, a Wood ghost a doctor from the west, a Water ghost doctors from all quarters, a Fire ghost a doctor from the north, an Earth ghost a doctor from the east. If the earlier hexagram is a Water ghost and the later a Fire ghost, one must first treat with doctors from all quarters, and the work is completed at the end by a doctor from the north.

**《天玄赋·天玄赋·十七、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5016 行 · *Tianxuan Fu (Rhapsody of the Heavenly Mystery), "Tianxuan Fu, chapter 17: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5016*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 必须参究五行， | 必须参究五行， | One must study the five phases, |
| 方可攻医万病。 | 才可以攻医各种病。 | and only then can one attack and treat the myriad illnesses. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5017 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5017*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火鬼宜凉剂； | 火鬼宜用凉剂； | A Fire ghost suits cooling remedies; |
| 水鬼宜温剂，或丸药； | 水鬼宜用温剂，或者丸药； | a Water ghost suits warming remedies, or pills; |
| 木鬼宜针； | 木鬼宜用针； | a Wood ghost suits the needle; |
| 金鬼宜灸； | 金鬼宜用灸； | a Metal ghost suits moxa; |
| 土鬼宜剉散。 | 土鬼宜用剉散。 | an Earth ghost suits cut-and-ground powders. |
| 火鬼月建旺，宜大寒之剂； | 火鬼在月建旺的时候，宜用大寒的药剂； | When a Fire ghost is strong on the Month, it suits very cold remedies; |
| 无气，不过清凉之药。 | 无气的，不过用清凉的药。 | without qi, only cooling and clearing medicines. |
| 余仿此推。 | 其余照这样推。 | The rest follow this. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5018 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5018*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金鬼利南方医人， | 金鬼利于请南方的医生， | A Metal ghost favours a doctor from the south, |
| 木鬼利西方医人， | 木鬼利于请西方的医生， | a Wood ghost a doctor from the west, |
| 水鬼利四散医人， | 水鬼利于请四散的医生， | a Water ghost doctors from all quarters, |
| 火鬼利北方医人， | 火鬼利于请北方的医生， | a Fire ghost a doctor from the north, |
| 土鬼利东方医人。 | 土鬼利于请东方的医生。 | an Earth ghost a doctor from the east. |
| 若前卦水鬼，后火鬼， | 如果前一卦是水鬼、后一卦是火鬼， | If the earlier hexagram is a Water ghost and the later a Fire ghost, |
| 先须四散医治， | 先要四散地医治， | one must first treat with doctors from all quarters, |
| 终于北方医人收功。 | 最后由北方的医生收功。 | and the work is completed at the end by a doctor from the north. |

> **打架**:请医生要往哪个方向请，几家不同。天玄赋注（LY.ILL.TXF.13）按鬼的五行取克它的方位：金鬼利南方（火克金）、木鬼利西方（金克木）、火鬼利北方（水克火）、土鬼利东方（木克土），水鬼利四散（土克水，土没有定方）；火珠林（见 LY.ILL.HZL.09）大体同此说，只有水鬼一项作「利土值」；增删卜易（LY.ILL.ZS.34）则是往「子孙」所在的方位请（午火子孙请南方的医生），子孙休囚空破时看应爻所在的方位，再有独发生用神的爻取那个爻的方位——一个按官鬼的克星取方位，一个按子孙或应爻取方位，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** In which direction to call the doctor, the books differ. The commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.13) takes the direction that controls the phase of the ghost: a Metal ghost favours the south (Fire controls Metal), a Wood ghost the west (Metal controls Wood), a Fire ghost the north (Water controls Fire), an Earth ghost the east (Wood controls Earth), and a Water ghost favours all quarters (Earth controls Water, and Earth has no fixed direction); the Huozhu Lin (see LY.ILL.HZL.09) says much the same, only for the Water ghost it has 'favoured by the earth quarter'; the Zengshan Buyi (LY.ILL.ZS.34) calls the doctor from the direction of Output instead (Wu Fire Output, a doctor from the south), and when Output is resting, trapped, Void or broken looks at the direction of the Other line, and further takes the direction of a line that moves alone and generates the Subject — one takes the direction from what controls the ghost, the other from Output or the Other line, and the books do not settle it.

> **按**:这一节是「五行用药」和「五行请医的方位」。用药：火鬼凉剂、水鬼温剂丸药、木鬼针、金鬼灸、土鬼剉散——和易隐「医药·治法与用药」（LY.ILL.YY.25：官鬼属金宜灸、属木宜针、属水宜温药丸药、属火宜凉药汤药、属土宜咬咀）一致，「剉散」和「咬咀」都是把药切碎捣散的剂型。「火鬼月建旺宜大寒之剂」的「月建旺」是火鬼旺在当月；这条和黄金策（LY.ILL.HJC.76）「火鬼生旺遇生扶用大寒药、休囚死绝用凉药」是同一句。请医方位：金鬼利南方、木鬼利西方、火鬼利北方、土鬼利东方，正是各自的克星方位；水鬼「利四散」是水的克星土没有固定方位（中央）。「前卦水鬼、后卦火鬼，先须四散医治，终于北方医人收功」是说两次占卜先水鬼（先请四面的医生），后火鬼（最后北方的医生才收功）。 卜筮大全的这一章后面还有「虎易评注」（今人加的），本库不引。
>
> **Note:** This section is 'medicine by the five phases' and 'the direction to call a doctor by the five phases'. Medicine: a Fire ghost cooling remedies, a Water ghost warming remedies and pills, a Wood ghost the needle, a Metal ghost moxa, an Earth ghost cut-and-ground powders — the same as the Yi Yin's 'medicine: treatment and the use of medicine' (LY.ILL.YY.25: a Metal Pressure line suits moxa, Wood the needle, Water warming medicine and pills, Fire cooling medicine and decoctions, Earth crushing and grinding); 'cut-and-ground powders' and 'crushing and grinding' are both forms of medicine cut fine and ground to a powder. The 'strong on the Month' of 'a Fire ghost strong on the Month suits very cold remedies' is the Fire ghost strong in the current month; this is the same sentence as the Huangjin Ce's (LY.ILL.HJC.76, 'a Fire ghost in a strong place and helped is treated with very cold medicines, resting, trapped, dead or in Extinction with cooling medicines'). The direction for calling a doctor: a Metal ghost favours the south, Wood the west, Fire the north, Earth the east, which is exactly the direction of what controls each; a Water ghost 'favours all quarters' because Earth, which controls Water, has no fixed direction (it is the centre). 'The earlier hexagram a Water ghost and the later a Fire ghost: first treat with doctors from all quarters, and the work is completed by a doctor from the north' says that of the two divinations the Water ghost comes first (call doctors from all sides first) and the Fire ghost after (a doctor from the north finally completes it). After this chapter the Buyi Daquan also has 'Huyi's commentary' (added by a modern hand), which this library does not quote.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1636 行:必须参究五行;同文 第 1636 行:方可攻医万病
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1870 行:必须参究五行;同文 第 1870 行:方可攻医万病

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.12、LY.ILL.TXF.14、LY.ILL.YY.25、LY.ILL.HJC.76、LY.ILL.ZS.34、LY.ILL.YM.37

## LY.ILL.YM.34 医药章总纲：占医药以子孙为用，临日月为上、旺相次之、休囚为下、空破散绝为庸医；章内六问——占药、占医、治病、用药、往医、行道

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占医药的总纲：拿子孙爻当用。子孙临日、月是上等，旺相是次等，休囚是下等，空、破、散、绝就是庸医。这一章开头先说了几句话：得志的时候愿意做良相，不得志的时候愿意做良医；君子借医来施展志向，做儿子的要懂得医理，吉凶取舍能不慎重吗？章里分六问细讲：占药、占医、治病、用药、往医、行道；头两问是病家问，后四问（治病、用药、往医、行道）都是医生自己问。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The outline for divining medicine and doctors: take Output as the one to use. Output sitting on the Day or Month is best, thriving and strong is next, resting and trapped is lowest, and Void, broken, scattered or in Extinction is a mediocre doctor. The chapter opens with a few remarks: when successful one wishes to be a good minister, when not, a good doctor; the gentleman carries out his will through medicine, and a son must know medicine, so how can one not be careful in choosing between fortune and misfortune? The chapter then treats six questions in detail: divining the medicine, divining the doctor, treating the illness, using the medicine, going to treat, and the doctor's practice; the first two are asked by the patient's family and the last four (treating, using, going, practising) are all asked by the doctor himself.

**《易冒·医药章第五十六》原文** — 《易冒》第 1247 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 56: Medicine", base text; Yi Mao, line 1247*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 闻之称贤曰：达则愿为良相，不达则愿为良医。 | 听到被称为贤人的人说过：得志的时候愿意做良相，不得志的时候愿意做良医。 | I have heard one called a worthy say: if I succeed, I wish to be a good minister; if I do not, a good doctor. |
| 君子以之行志，人子教其须知，吉凶从违，可不慎与？ | 君子借医来施展自己的志向，做儿子的要学会该知道的医理，吉凶的取舍，能不慎重吗？ | The gentleman puts his will into practice through it, and a son is taught that he must know it; in following or avoiding fortune and misfortune, can one not be careful? |

**《易冒·医药章第五十六》原文** — 《易冒》第 1248 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 56: Medicine", base text; Yi Mao, line 1248*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡占医药，以子孙为用，临日月为上，旺相次之，休囚为下，空破散绝为庸医也。 | 凡是占医药，以子孙爻当用，子孙临日、月是上等，旺相是次等，休囚是下等，空、破、散、绝就是庸医。 | In all divining of medicine and doctors, Output is the line to use: sitting on the Day or Month is best, thriving and strong is next, resting and trapped is lowest, and Void, broken, scattered or in Extinction is a mediocre doctor. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1246 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1246*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内详占药、占医、治病、用药、往医、行道六问 | 里面详细讲六个问题：占药、占医、治病、用药、往医、行道。 | The chapter treats in detail six questions: divining the medicine, divining the doctor, treating the illness, using the medicine, going to treat, and the doctor's practice. |

> **按**:「用」在这里指取用，和「用神」是一回事：占医药不取病人的用神，取子孙爻。黄金策的医药章同样以子孙为药饵、应爻为医人（见 LY.ILL.HJC.58），下面第二条（LY.ILL.YM.36）也说应爻指医人。目录注里的「往医」「行道」是医生的问法（见后面 LY.ILL.YM.40、YM.41）：「往医」是医生上门去治，「行道」是医生行医的前程。这一条把原文里的目录注放在了最后。
>
> **Note:** 'Use' here means the one to take, the same thing as the Subject: in divining medicine one does not take the patient's Subject but the Output line. The Huangjin Ce's chapter on medicine likewise takes Output as the medicine and the Other as the doctor (see LY.ILL.HJC.58), and the second entry below (LY.ILL.YM.36) also says the Other stands for the doctor. 'Going to treat' and 'practising' in the contents note are the doctor's own questions (see LY.ILL.YM.40 and YM.41 below): 'going to treat' is the doctor visiting a patient, and 'practising' is the course of the doctor's practice. In this entry the contents note from the original is placed last.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.33、LY.ILL.YM.35、LY.ILL.HJC.58、LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.ZS.27、LY.ILL.ZS.29、LY.ILL.YY.21

## LY.ILL.YM.35 子官强弱定药效：无子药不良、强官病不治；子强官弱如烈日销冰、子弱官强如铅刀攻玉；财动鬼静子动无功；伏鬼根不除、化鬼病传变、空鬼药老；世鬼病曲折、应鬼药跷蹊

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里没有子孙，药就不好；官鬼强，病就治不好。子孙强、官鬼弱，像烈日融冰；子孙弱、官鬼强，像用铅刀去攻玉。财爻动、鬼爻静，子孙动，都没有功效。鬼伏着，病根拔不掉；变出鬼，病会传变；鬼落空，药就是「老」药。鬼临世爻而兴旺，我的病就曲折反复；鬼临应爻而旺相，那副药就古怪蹊跷。注说：要审察子孙和官鬼的强弱，鬼当值日、月时，即使子孙得到生扶也不算完全好，要比较子孙能不能制住鬼，才是吉占；鬼落空是躲开了子孙，药打不中病，所以叫「老」；鬼在世，病难很快好，鬼在应，医生诡诈难信。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Without Output the medicine is not good, and with a strong Pressure line the illness cannot be cured. Output strong and Pressure weak is a fierce sun melting ice; Output weak and Pressure strong is a lead knife attacking jade. With Wealth moving and the ghost still, or Output moving, there is no effect. A hidden ghost means the root is not removed; a change into a ghost means the illness passes on and changes; a Void ghost makes the medicine 'aged'. A ghost thriving on the Self makes my illness twist and turn, and a ghost strong on the Other makes that medicine dubious. The note says: one must examine the strength of Output and Pressure; when the ghost is on duty in the Day or Month, even if Output meets generation and support it is not wholly good, and one must compare whether Output can subdue the ghost before it is an auspicious divination; a Void ghost evades Output and so the medicine does not hit the illness, hence 'aged'; a ghost on the Self means the illness is hard to cure quickly, and a ghost on the Other means the doctor is deceitful and hard to trust.

**《易冒·医药章第五十六》原文** — 《易冒》第 1249 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 56: Medicine", base text; Yi Mao, line 1249*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 无子而药不良，强官而病不治； | 卦里没有子孙，药就不好；官鬼强，病就治不好； | Without Output the medicine is not good, and with a strong Pressure line the illness cannot be cured; |
| 子强官弱，烈日销冰，子弱官强，铅刀攻玉，财动鬼静，子动无功也。 | 子孙强、官鬼弱，像烈日融冰；子孙弱、官鬼强，像用铅刀去攻玉；财爻动、鬼爻静，子孙动，都没有功效。 | Output strong and Pressure weak is a fierce sun melting ice; Output weak and Pressure strong is a lead knife attacking jade; with Wealth moving and the ghost still, or Output moving, there is no effect. |
| 伏鬼根不除，化鬼病传（转？）变，空鬼老医药。 | 鬼伏着，病根拔不掉；变出鬼，病会传变（「传」字后面转录者标了「转？」）；鬼落空，药就是「老」药。 | A hidden ghost means the root is not removed; a change into a ghost means the illness passes on and changes (the transcription marks 'transforms?' after 'passes on'); a Void ghost makes the medicine 'aged'. |
| 世鬼兴隆，则吾病曲折，应鬼旺相，则彼药跷蹊。 | 鬼临世爻而兴旺，我的病就曲折反复；鬼临应爻而旺相，那副药就古怪蹊跷。 | A ghost thriving on the Self makes my illness twist and turn; a ghost strong on the Other makes that medicine queer and dubious. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1251 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1251*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 当审子官强弱，如鬼值日月，虽子遇生扶，亦未尽善，必较子可服鬼，方是吉占。 | 应当审察子孙和官鬼的强弱：如果鬼当值日、月，即使子孙得到生扶，也不算完全好，一定要比较子孙能不能制住鬼，才是吉占。 | One must examine the strength of Output and Pressure: if the ghost is on duty in the Day or Month, even though Output meets generation and support it is not wholly good; one must compare whether Output can subdue the ghost, and only then is it an auspicious divination. |
| 鬼空避子，药不中病，故云老也。 | 鬼落空是躲开了子孙，药打不中病，所以说「老」。 | A Void ghost evades Output, so the medicine does not hit the illness, hence it is called 'aged'. |
| 世鬼病难速愈，应鬼医诈难信。 | 鬼在世爻，病很难快好；鬼在应爻，医生诡诈，难以相信。 | A ghost on the Self, the illness is hard to cure quickly; a ghost on the Other, the doctor is deceitful and hard to trust. |

> **按**:经文里的「老医药」，注说是「鬼空避子，药不中病，故云老也」：「老」是指药不对症，像一味放老了的药。「财动鬼静，子动无功」这一句原文不带说明，注也没解；字面是财爻动、鬼爻静、子孙动的时候没有功效，和上一句「子强官弱」并不矛盾——财动是在生鬼（或冲克父母），子孙即使动，制鬼的力气也被财爻的生助抵掉了，这是推的，书里没有写。「传（转？）变」的「转？」是转录者自己标的疑字，这里照录，没有改。这一条与黄金策医药章「官旺福衰药饵轻而病重」（LY.ILL.HJC.61）、「福化忌爻误服杀身之恶剂」（HJC.66）是同一路读法。
>
> **Note:** For the text's 'aged medicine', the note says a Void ghost evades Output and the medicine does not hit the illness, hence it is called aged: 'aged' means the medicine does not fit the illness, like a herb left to go stale. The sentence 'Wealth moving, the ghost still, Output moving: no effect' comes without comment in the text and the note does not explain it; literally it says there is no effect when Wealth moves, the ghost is still and Output moves, and this does not conflict with 'Output strong and Pressure weak' just before, since Wealth moving feeds the ghost (or clashes the Resource line) and even a moving Output has its strength against the ghost cancelled by the Wealth's support, but this is inferred and not written in the book. The 'transforms?' after 'passes on' is the transcriber's own mark of doubt and is copied as it stands. This entry reads in the same way as the Huangjin Ce's medicine chapter, 'Pressure strong and Output weak, the medicine light and the illness heavy' (LY.ILL.HJC.61) and 'Output changing into an Adversary line, a bad draught that kills' (HJC.66).

相关:LY.ILL.YM.34、LY.ILL.YM.36、LY.ILL.HJC.61、LY.ILL.HJC.64、LY.ILL.HJC.65、LY.ILL.HJC.66、LY.ILL.HJC.70、LY.ILL.HJC.73、LY.ILL.ZS.30、LY.ILL.BSZZ.12、LY.ILL.YY.21

## LY.ILL.YM.36 应指医人、非占克效：应空不得遇、应破不得专、应散识见反惑、应克世好利多诈、世应冲绝续不终

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:应爻指的是医生，不是拿来占能不能克住病的：所以应落空，遇不上这个医生；应被冲破，医生不专心；应散，医生的见识反倒糊涂；应克世，医生爱财、多诈；世应相冲，治疗断断续续，做不到底。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Other stands for the doctor, and is not for divining whether the illness will be overcome: so with the Other Void, one cannot meet this doctor; broken, the doctor is not devoted; scattered, his insight is confused instead; the Other controlling the Self, the doctor loves profit and is full of deceit; Self and Other clashing, the treatment breaks off and is not carried to the end.

**《易冒·医药章第五十六》原文** — 《易冒》第 1252 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 56: Medicine", base text; Yi Mao, line 1252*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫应指医人，非占克效，是故应空不得遇，应破不得专，应散则识见反惑，应克世则好利多诈，世应冲则绝续不终也。 | 应爻指的是医生，不是拿来占克住病的效验的，所以应落空，遇不上；应被冲破，医生不专心；应散，识见反倒糊涂；应克世，爱财、多诈；世应相冲，治疗断断续续，做不到底。 | The Other stands for the doctor, and is not for divining the efficacy of overcoming the illness; therefore, with the Other Void, one cannot meet him; broken, he cannot be devoted; scattered, his insight is instead confused; the Other controlling the Self, he loves profit and is full of deceit; Self and Other clashing, the treatment breaks off and does not last to the end. |

> **按**:这一条把医生和药分开：药看子孙（LY.ILL.YM.34、YM.35），医生看应爻；问药灵不灵不取应，问医生可不可信不取子孙。黄金策同样以应爻为医人（LY.ILL.HJC.58、HJC.62），增删卜易也以应爻为医人，应爻不宜空破墓绝（LY.ILL.ZS.29）。「绝续不终」的「绝续」指治疗断了又续，始终不能了结。
>
> **Note:** This entry separates the doctor from the medicine: the medicine is read from Output (LY.ILL.YM.34, YM.35) and the doctor from the Other line; to ask whether a medicine works one does not take the Other, and to ask whether the doctor can be trusted one does not take Output. The Huangjin Ce likewise takes the Other as the doctor (LY.ILL.HJC.58, HJC.62), and the Zengshan Buyi also takes the Other as the doctor, who should not be Void, broken, in a tomb or in Extinction (LY.ILL.ZS.29). 'Breaks off and does not last to the end' means the treatment stops and resumes but is never concluded.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.35、LY.ILL.YM.37、LY.ILL.HJC.58、LY.ILL.HJC.62、LY.ILL.HJC.69、LY.ILL.ZS.29、LY.ILL.YY.21
