## LY.ILL.YM.37 占药占医取象：水子旺宜汤、土子旺宜丸、火灸金针木末；火鬼喜凉药、木鬼喜风药、土脾金肺水肾；占医考子孙之属，木东金西乾僧坤妇——注：子为药鬼为病，象只用来问何药何人，问吉凶仍看子孙衰旺

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占药：水属的子孙旺，宜用汤剂；土属的子孙旺，宜用丸药；火像艾灸，金像针，木像药末，以这些作象。火鬼喜欢凉药，木鬼喜欢祛风的药；土对应脾，金对应肺，水对应肾，也以这些作象。占医生：要考察子孙属于哪一类，木属东、金属西，乾是僧人、坤是妇人，也以这些作象。注说：子孙是药，鬼是病，占该用什么药、是什么病，就用这个办法；如果占的是这味药吉不吉，就看子孙的衰旺，不要死板地认为子孙属土就该用丸药。占该请哪个方位、哪种人当医生，也是这个办法；单独占医生好不好，还是用子孙的强弱，比如问请僧人当医生，得乾卦而子孙落空，怎么能说吉？

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Divining medicine: a strong Water Output suits a decoction, a strong Earth Output suits pills; Fire is moxibustion, Metal needles, Wood powders, taken as the images. A Fire ghost likes cooling medicine and a Wood ghost likes wind medicine; Earth is the spleen, Metal the lung and Water the kidney, also taken as images. Divining the doctor: examine what kind the Output belongs to; Wood is east and Metal west, Qian a monk and Kun a woman, again taken as images. The note says: Output is the medicine and the ghost is the illness, and to divine which medicine to use or what the illness is, use this method; but if what is divined is whether a medicine is auspicious, use the strength of Output, and do not rigidly hold that an Earth Output must mean pills. To divine which direction and which kind of person to use as doctor the method is the same; but to divine on its own whether the doctor is good, still use the strength of Output: for instance, if one asks about a monk-doctor and obtains Qian with Output Void, how could one call it auspicious?

**《易冒·医药章第五十六》原文** — 《易冒》第 1253 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 56: Medicine", base text; Yi Mao, line 1253*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占药水子旺宜汤，土子旺宜丸，火灸、金针、木末、以为像。 | 占药：水属的子孙旺，宜用汤剂；土属的子孙旺，宜用丸药；火像艾灸，金像针，木像药末，以这些作象。 | In divining medicine: a strong Water Output suits a decoction, a strong Earth Output suits pills; Fire is moxibustion, Metal needles, Wood powders: these are the images. |
| 火鬼喜凉药，木鬼喜风药，土脾、金肺、水肾，以为像。 | 火鬼喜欢凉药，木鬼喜欢祛风的药；土对应脾，金对应肺，水对应肾，也以这些作象。 | A Fire ghost likes cooling medicine and a Wood ghost likes wind medicine; Earth is the spleen, Metal the lung and Water the kidney: these are the images. |
| 占医也，考子孙之属，木东金西，乾僧坤妇，以为像。 | 占医生，要考察子孙属于哪一类：木属东、金属西，乾是僧人、坤是妇人，以此作象。 | In divining the doctor, examine what the Output belongs to: Wood is east and Metal west, Qian a monk and Kun a woman: these are the images. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1254 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1254*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子为药而鬼为病，卜用何药，卜是何病，则用此法，若卜是药吉凶，用子孙衰旺，无执子孙属土，宜用丸药。 | 子孙是药，鬼是病。占该用什么药、占是什么病，就用这个办法；如果占的是这味药吉不吉，就看子孙的衰旺，不要死板地认为子孙属土就该用丸药。 | Output is the medicine and the ghost is the illness. To divine which medicine to use, or what illness it is, use this method; but if what is divined is whether a medicine is auspicious, use the strength of Output, and do not rigidly hold that an Earth Output must mean pills. |
| 如卯建辛酉日，占医得离之贲，未土子爻，真空四败，焉能丸药有功？ | 比如卯月辛酉日，占医生，得离卦变贲卦，未土子爻，是真空，又逢四败，怎么能说丸药有功效？ | For example, in a Mao month on a Xinyou day, a doctor was divined and Li changing into Bi (Adornment) was obtained; the Wei Earth Output was truly Void and met the four Defeats; how could pills be effective? |
| 卜筮宜用何方何人，则用此法，若独占则用子孙，设问僧医，得乾而子空，岂可言吉？ | 占该请哪个方位、哪种人当医生，就用这个办法；如果单独占医生好不好，就用子孙，假如问请僧人当医生，得乾卦而子孙落空，怎么能说吉？ | To divine which direction or which kind of person to use, apply this method; but if it is divined on its own, use Output: suppose one asks about a monk-doctor and obtains Qian with Output Void, how could one call it auspicious? |

> **打架**:用药要不要按五行来配：易冒把「水子旺宜汤、土子旺宜丸、火灸金针木末」「火鬼喜凉药、木鬼喜风药」当作象，但注马上限定，象只用来问「用何药、是何病」，问吉凶还是看子孙衰旺，不要认死「子孙属土就用丸药」；黄金策旧注、王洪绪和易隐把五行当作用药的依据（火鬼旺用大寒药、水鬼旺用大热药、金鬼宜灸、木鬼宜针，见 LY.ILL.HJC.76、HJC.81、HJC.84、YY.25）；增删卜易的李我平则批评这类说法拘泥，说子孙不是针灸之药、火鬼旺不一定该用大寒药（LY.ILL.ZS.40）。易冒站在中间：象可以用，但不作凭据。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether medicine should be matched by the phases: the Yi Mao takes 'a strong Water Output suits a decoction, an Earth Output pills, Fire moxibustion, Metal needles, Wood powders' and 'a Fire ghost likes cooling medicine, a Wood ghost wind medicine' as images, but the note at once limits them: images are used only for 'which medicine to use and what the illness is', while for whether it is auspicious one still reads the strength of Output and should not hold rigidly that 'an Earth Output means pills'; the old commentary and Wang Hongxu on the Huangjin Ce and the Yi Yin take the phases as the ground for the choice of medicine (a strong Fire ghost takes a very cold medicine, a strong Water ghost a very hot one, a Metal ghost moxibustion, a Wood ghost needles; see LY.ILL.HJC.76, HJC.81, HJC.84, YY.25); Li Woping of the Zengshan Buyi criticises such sayings as rigid, saying Output is not a medicine for needles and moxibustion and that a strong Fire ghost does not necessarily call for a very cold medicine (LY.ILL.ZS.40). The Yi Mao stands in the middle: the images may be used, but not as the ground.

> **按**:「四败」是十二长生里的沐浴：金败在午、木败在子、火败在卯、水土败在酉（见卜筮大全，沐浴即四败）；例中辛酉日，土的败正在酉，所以说逢四败。「真空」在易冒旬空章里指被月建克住的那种空（春天的土、夏天的金、秋天的木、冬天的火，以及辰戌丑未月里的水），卯月里的未土正是春土，所以叫真空。「以为像」的「像」就是象。
>
> **Note:** 'The four Defeats' is Bath in the twelve life stages: Metal's Defeat is at Wu, Wood's at Zi, Fire's at Mao, and Water's and Earth's at You (see the Bushi Daquan, where Bath is the four Defeats); in the case the day is Xinyou, and Earth's Defeat is exactly at You, hence 'meets the four Defeats'. 'True Void' in the Yi Mao's chapter on Void means the Void of a line controlled by the Month (Earth in spring, Metal in summer, Wood in autumn, Fire in winter, and Water in the Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei months), and the Wei Earth in a Mao month is spring Earth, hence 'truly Void'. The 'xiang' in 'taken as images' is the image.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.36、LY.ILL.YM.38、LY.ILL.HJC.76、LY.ILL.HJC.77、LY.ILL.HJC.81、LY.ILL.HJC.82、LY.ILL.HJC.84、LY.ILL.YY.24、LY.ILL.YY.25、LY.ILL.ZS.34、LY.ILL.ZS.40

---

## LY.ILL.YM.38 医问疾可疗乎：以应为先务（应为病人），应坏、随墓则其病不治；若占六亲仍以用神为断

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:医生问这个病能不能治，先看应爻，因为对医生来说应爻就是病人；应爻坏了、或随墓，这个病就治不好。如果占的是病人的某个亲属，仍然以用神来断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a doctor asks whether an illness can be treated, the Other line comes first, for to him the Other is the patient; if the Other is spoiled or follows into the tomb, the illness cannot be cured. If what is divined is one of the patient's relations, judgment still goes by the Subject.

**《易冒·医药章第五十六》原文** — 《易冒》第 1255 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 56: Medicine", base text; Yi Mao, line 1255*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 医问疾可疗乎，以应为先务，盖应为病人，应坏随墓，其病不治； | 医生问这个病能不能治，先看应爻，因为对医生来说应爻就是病人，应爻坏了、随墓，这个病就治不好； | When a doctor asks whether an illness can be treated, the Other comes first, for to him the Other is the patient; if the Other is spoiled or follows into the tomb, the illness cannot be cured; |
| 若占六亲，仍以用神为断。 | 如果占的是病人的某个亲属，仍然以用神来断。 | if what is divined is one of the patient's relations, judgment still goes by the Subject. |

> **按**:医生自己占的时候，对方（应）就是病人，所以头一个要看的是应爻；这和病家占病取世（自己）或取用神不同。「若占六亲」是说医生受托问的其实是病人的某个亲属，这时不再取应，回到用神。这是治病问的。
>
> **Note:** When the doctor himself divines, the other party (the Other) is the patient, so the first thing to read is the Other line; this differs from the patient's family, who take the Self (oneself) or the Subject. 'If what is divined is a relation' means the doctor is in fact asked about one of the patient's relations, and then the Other is no longer taken and judgment returns to the Subject. This entry answers the question of treating the illness.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.37、LY.ILL.YM.39、LY.ILL.YM.20、LY.ILL.ZS.36、LY.ILL.HJC.62

---

## LY.ILL.YM.39 医问是药奏功乎：亦以子孙为用，福强鬼弱而奏效、子虚鬼实而无功（与占药同）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:医生问这味药能不能奏效，也是拿子孙爻当用：子孙强、鬼弱，药就见效；子孙虚、鬼实，药就没有功效。注说：和占药一样。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a doctor asks whether a medicine will take effect, Output is again the line to use: with Output strong and the ghost weak it takes effect, and with Output empty and the ghost full it has no effect. The note says: it is the same as divining medicine.

**《易冒·医药章第五十六》原文** — 《易冒》第 1255 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 56: Medicine", base text; Yi Mao, line 1255*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 医问是药奏功乎，亦以子孙为用，福强鬼弱而奏效也，子虚鬼实而无功。 | 医生问这味药能不能奏效，也是以子孙为用：子孙强、鬼弱，药就见效；子孙虚、鬼实，药就没有功效。 | When a doctor asks whether a medicine will take effect, Output is again the line to use: with Output strong and the ghost weak it takes effect, and with Output empty and the ghost full it has no effect. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1256 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1256*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 亦如占药。 | 也和占药一样。 | It is the same as divining medicine. |

> **按**:这一条是用药问：医生已经开了某味药，问它灵不灵，取子孙的强弱，和病家占药一样（见 LY.ILL.YM.35、YM.37）。「福强鬼弱」的「福」是子孙，「子虚鬼实」的「虚」「实」用的是上面 LY.ILL.YM.31 里的意思：空破散绝叫虚，旺相有生扶叫实。
>
> **Note:** This entry is the question of using the medicine: the doctor has settled on a certain medicine and asks whether it will work, and takes the strength of Output, as the patient's family does in divining medicine (see LY.ILL.YM.35, YM.37). The 'fortune' in 'fortune strong, ghost weak' is Output, and 'empty' and 'full' in 'Output empty, ghost full' are used in the sense given in LY.ILL.YM.31 above: Void, broken, scattered or in Extinction is empty, and thriving and strong with generation and support is full.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.38、LY.ILL.YM.40、LY.ILL.YM.35、LY.ILL.YM.37、LY.ILL.YM.31、LY.ILL.ZS.37、LY.ILL.HJC.61

---

## LY.ILL.YM.40 医问往医利乎：以世应推——应克世者寡情、爻无财者薄赠、空有欺志、破无专心、散冲无终

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:医生问上门去治病有没有利，就从世、应来推：应克世的，病家没有情义；卦里没有财爻的，酬谢微薄；落空的，有欺骗的心思；冲破的，没有专心；散了、冲了的，不会有个结果。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a doctor asks whether going to treat will be profitable, judge by the Self and the Other: the Other controlling the Self means little feeling; a hexagram with no Wealth line means a meagre gift; Void means an intent to deceive; broken, no wholehearted devotion; scattered or clashed, no end.

**《易冒·医药章第五十六》原文** — 《易冒》第 1257 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 56: Medicine", base text; Yi Mao, line 1257*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 医问往医利乎，则以世应而推，应克世者寡情，爻无财者薄赠，空有欺志，破无专心，散、冲无终。 | 医生问上门去治病有没有利，就从世、应来推：应克世的，病家没有情义；卦里没有财爻的，酬谢微薄；落空的，有欺骗的心思；冲破的，没有专心；散了、冲了的，不会有个结果。 | When a doctor asks whether going to treat will be profitable, judge by the Self and the Other: the Other controlling the Self means little feeling; a hexagram with no Wealth line means a meagre gift; Void means an intent to deceive; broken, no wholehearted devotion; scattered or clashed, no end. |

> **按**:原文里「空有欺志，破无专心，散、冲无终」没有说是谁的空、破、散、冲；按上文「以世应而推」，这里译作世、应落空、冲破、散、冲时的情形，没有替它加主语。这是医生问的第三种（往医）。
>
> **Note:** The original does not say whose Void, breaking, scattering or clash it is in 'Void has intent to deceive, broken has no devotion, scattered or clashed has no end'; following 'judge by the Self and the Other' just before, it is rendered as the case when the Self and Other are Void, broken, scattered or clashed, and no subject has been added. This is the third of the doctor's questions (going to treat).

相关:LY.ILL.YM.39、LY.ILL.YM.41、LY.ILL.YM.36、LY.ILL.ZS.36、LY.ILL.HJC.62

---

## LY.ILL.YM.41 医问道行：财官强而利名遂；医药不精不足以利物、求医不慎不足以疗生，己弗误人亦弗自误

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:医生问自己行医的前程：财爻、官爻强，名和利就能遂愿。所以医术不精，不足以造福万物；求医不谨慎，不足以救人性命；自己不耽误别人，也不耽误自己。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a doctor asks about the course of his practice, if the Wealth and Pressure lines are strong, profit and fame are achieved. So medicine that is not refined cannot benefit things, and seeking a doctor without care cannot heal life; if one does not mislead others, one does not mislead oneself.

**《易冒·医药章第五十六》原文** — 《易冒》第 1258 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 56: Medicine", base text; Yi Mao, line 1258*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 医问道行，财官强而利名遂，故医药不精，不足以利物，求医不慎，不足以疗生，己弗误人，亦弗自误也。 | 医生问行医的前程，财爻、官爻强，名和利就能遂愿；所以医术不精，不足以造福万物，求医不谨慎，不足以救人性命，自己不耽误别人，也不耽误自己。 | When a doctor asks about the course of his practice, if the Wealth and Pressure lines are strong, profit and fame are achieved; therefore, medicine that is not refined cannot benefit things, and seeking a doctor without care cannot heal life; if one does not mislead others, one does not mislead oneself. |

> **按**:「财官强而利名遂」是本条唯一的断法：财主利，官主名。后半句「故医药不精……」是章末的劝诫，对医生也对病家说：医生的技术不精，不能救人；病家选医生不小心，也救不了命。「财官」这里是财爻和官爻，原文没有写「妻财」「官鬼」，但意思相同。
>
> **Note:** 'Wealth and Pressure strong, profit and fame are achieved' is the only judgment in this entry: Wealth governs profit and Pressure governs fame. The second half, 'so medicine that is not refined...', is the chapter's closing admonition, addressed to the doctor and to the patient's family alike: a doctor whose skill is poor cannot save a life, and a family that chooses a doctor carelessly cannot save it either. 'Wealth and Pressure' here are the Wealth line and the Pressure line, written without 'wife-wealth' or 'official-ghost' but meaning the same.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.40、LY.ILL.YM.34、LY.ILL.ZS.36

---

## LY.ILL.YY.21 医药·药效：内卦世爻用爻是病人、外卦应爻子孙是医药；外克内、应与子孙克世克用则药效；内克外则药不效；外生合内则药对症效迟；无子孙不遇良医；子孙两动换医而愈；鬼子俱空不治自愈；鬼子俱动医祷兼用；子财两动鬼得助病难治

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:内卦的世爻、用爻是病人，外卦的应爻、子孙是医生和药。外卦克内卦，应爻和子孙克世、克用的，药有效；内卦克外卦，世爻、用爻克应、克子孙的，药没有效；外卦生合内卦，应爻和子孙生合世的，药对症但见效慢；外卦应爻克内卦世爻而卦里没有子孙的，遇不到好医生。应爻带子孙发动来克世的，药有效；世克应和子孙的，是名医但药没有效；子孙克世而外卦应爻不克内卦世爻的，药没效也没害。应爻带鬼发动、刑害克冲世爻的，是用错药伤了人；子孙落在辰午酉亥自刑之地的，用药有差错；日辰、动爻冲伤子孙的，药没有效；子孙变出父母或鬼的，药收不到功；子孙变出子孙的，药杂不精；子孙带白虎煞发动的，是庸医误人。世下伏官、遇子孙发动的，药虽好而病根除不掉；世持衰鬼的，病轻而药难治。世应比和而卦无子孙、或子孙空绝的，宜换药换医；子孙有两个都动的，换医就好；鬼和子孙都落空的，不治自愈；鬼和子孙都发动的，医药和祈祷一起用；子孙和财爻都动，鬼得到帮助，病难治。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Self line and Subject line in the inner trigram are the patient, and the Other line and Output in the outer trigram are the doctor and medicine. If the outer controls the inner, and the Other and Output control the Self and the Subject, the medicine is effective; if the inner controls the outer, and the Self and Subject control the Other and Output, it is not effective; if the outer generates and combines with the inner, and the Other and Output generate and combine with the Self, the medicine fits the illness but works slowly; if the Other in the outer trigram controls the Self in the inner and there is no Output in the hexagram, one does not meet a good doctor. If the Other line carries Output and moves to control the Self, the medicine is effective; if the Self controls the Other and Output, the doctor is famous but the medicine does not work; if Output controls the Self but the Other in the outer trigram does not control the Self, the medicine has no effect and does no harm. If the Other line carries the ghost and moves to punish, harm, control or clash the Self, wrong medicine harms the person; if Output stands on Chen, Wu, You or Hai, which punish themselves, the medicine is mistaken; if the Day or a moving line clashes with and wounds Output, it does not work; if Output changes into Resource or a ghost, it does not bring completion; if Output changes into Output, the medicines are mixed and not refined; if Output carries the Tiger and a killing star and moves, a mediocre doctor harms people. If Pressure is hidden beneath the Self and Output moves, the medicine is good but the root of the illness is not removed; if the Self holds a weak ghost, the illness is light but the medicine has difficulty curing it. If the Self and Other are in harmony and there is no Output, or Output is Void or in Extinction, change the medicine and doctor; if two Output lines both move, changing the doctor leads to recovery; if the ghost and Output are both Void, he recovers without treatment; if the ghost and Output both move, use doctors and prayers together; if Output and Wealth both move, the ghost is helped and the illness is hard to treat.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3013 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3013*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内卦世爻用爻为病人，外卦应爻子孙为医药。 | 内卦的世爻、用爻是病人，外卦的应爻、子孙是医生和药。 | The Self line and Subject line in the inner trigram are the patient, and the Other line and Output in the outer trigram are the doctor and medicine. |
| 外克内应与子孙克世克用者，药效也。 | 外卦克内卦，应和子孙克世、克用的，药有效。 | If the outer controls the inner, and the Other and Output control the Self and the Subject, the medicine is effective. |
| 内克外，世爻用爻克应克子孙者，药不效也。 | 内卦克外卦，世爻、用爻克应、克子孙的，药没有效。 | If the inner controls the outer, and the Self and Subject lines control the Other and Output, the medicine is not effective. |
| 外生合内，应与子孙生合世者，药对症而效迟也。 | 外卦生合内卦，应和子孙生合世的，药对症，但见效慢。 | If the outer generates and combines with the inner, and the Other and Output generate and combine with the Self, the medicine fits the illness but works slowly. |
| 外应克内世，卦无子孙者，不遇良医也。 | 外卦的应爻克内卦的世爻，而卦里没有子孙的，遇不到好医生。 | If the Other in the outer trigram controls the Self in the inner, and there is no Output in the hexagram, one does not meet a good doctor. |
| 父兄俱动者，子叨生而医善也。 | 父母爻、兄爻都发动的，子孙沾光得到生，医生就好。 | If Resource and Peer both move, Output gains from being generated and the doctor is good. |
| 父官双静，而子临应动者，良医到也。 | 父母爻、官爻都安静，而子孙临在应爻上发动的，好医生来了。 | If Resource and Pressure are both still, and Output sits on the Other line and moves, a good doctor arrives. |
| 应加子动克世者，药有效也。 | 应爻带子孙发动来克世爻的，药有效。 | If the Other line carries Output and moves to control the Self, the medicine is effective. |
| 世克应子者，名医而药不效也。 | 世爻克应爻、克子孙的，是名医，但药没有效。 | If the Self controls the Other and Output, the doctor is famous but the medicine is not effective. |
| 子克世，而外应不克内世者，药不效而无害也。 | 子孙克世爻，而外卦的应爻不克内卦的世爻的，药没有效，但也没有害。 | If Output controls the Self but the Other in the outer trigram does not control the Self in the inner, the medicine has no effect but does no harm. |
| 应加鬼动刑害克冲世者，误药伤人也。 | 应爻带鬼发动、刑害克冲世爻的，是用错了药，伤了人。 | If the Other line carries the ghost and it moves to punish, harm, control or clash the Self, wrong medicine harms the person. |
| 子孙值辰午酉亥自刑者，用药差误也。 | 子孙落在辰、午、酉、亥这四个自刑的地支上，用药有差错。 | If Output stands on Chen, Wu, You or Hai, which punish themselves, the medicine is mistaken. |
| 日辰动爻冲伤子孙者，药不效也。 | 日辰、动爻冲伤子孙的，药没有效。 | If the Day or a moving line clashes with and wounds Output, the medicine is not effective. |
| 子化父鬼者，药不收功也。 | 子孙变出父母、鬼的，药收不到功效。 | If Output changes into Resource or a ghost, the medicine does not bring completion. |
| 子化子者，杂药不精也。 | 子孙变出子孙的，药杂而不精。 | If Output changes into Output, the medicines are mixed and not refined. |
| 子加虎杀动者，庸医误人也。 | 子孙带白虎、煞发动的，是庸医误人。 | If Output carries the Tiger and a killing star and moves, a mediocre doctor harms people. |
| 世下伏官，逢子动者，药虽好而病根不除也。 | 世爻下面伏着官，遇到子孙发动的，药虽好，病根却除不掉。 | If Pressure is hidden beneath the Self and Output moves, the medicine is good but the root of the illness is not removed. |
| 世持衰鬼者，病虽轻而药难疗也。 | 世爻持着衰弱的鬼的，病虽轻，药却难以治疗。 | If the Self holds a weak ghost, the illness is light but the medicine has difficulty curing it. |
| 世应比而卦无子，或子空绝者，宜换药医。 | 世爻、应爻比和，而卦里没有子孙，或者子孙空、绝的，应当换药换医生。 | If the Self and Other are in harmony and there is no Output in the hexagram, or Output is Void or in Extinction, the medicine and doctor should be changed. |
| 子孙两动者，换医而愈也。 | 子孙有两个都发动的，换个医生就会好。 | If two Output lines both move, changing the doctor leads to recovery. |
| 鬼子俱空者，不治自愈也。 | 鬼和子孙都落空的，不治疗也会自己好。 | If the ghost and Output are both Void, he recovers without treatment. |
| 鬼子俱动者，医祷兼用也。 | 鬼和子孙都发动的，医药和祈祷一起用。 | If the ghost and Output both move, doctors and prayers should be used together. |
| 子财两动，则鬼得助而病难治也。 | 子孙和财爻都动，鬼就得到帮助，病难治。 | If Output and Wealth both move, the ghost is helped and the illness is hard to treat. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 3033 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, line 3033*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 庚寅年戊寅月己巳日，代占人病，得坎之蹇。 | 庚寅年戊寅月己巳日，替别人占病，得到坎卦变蹇卦。 | In the Gengyin year, the Wuyin month, on the Jisi day, someone divined for another's illness and obtained Kan changing into Jian. |
| 应爻相气，岁月生之，日辰比之，但嫌化出申金，冲刑寅木子孙，服药不效。 | 应爻旺相，岁、月生它，日辰扶它，只嫌变出申金，冲刑寅木子孙，所以服药没有效。 | The Other line is in season and is generated by the Year and Month and supported by the Day, but it is unfortunate that it changes into Shen Metal, which clashes with and punishes Yin Wood Output, so taking medicine had no effect. |
| 辰宫化午财，刑应上午，是以多进食而受伤也。 | 辰（土）变出午财，刑到应爻上的午，所以吃得多而受了伤。 | The Chen line changes into Wu Wealth, which punishes the Wu on the Other line, and so he was harmed by eating too much. |
| 官属土，脾胃病。 | 官爻属土，是脾胃的病。 | The Pressure line is Earth: an ailment of the spleen and stomach. |
| 子旺官衰，卦虽六冲，新病不死，只是难痊耳。 | 子孙旺、官爻衰，卦虽然是六冲，新病不会死，只是难以痊愈。 | Output is strong and Pressure weak; although the hexagram is Six-Clash, a recent illness does not kill, it is only hard to cure. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 3041 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, line 3041*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 庚寅年甲申月乙丑日，夫占妻病，得震之豫。 | 庚寅年甲申月乙丑日，丈夫占妻子的病，得到震卦变豫卦。 | In the Gengyin year, the Jiashen month, on the Yichou day, a husband divined about his wife's illness and obtained Zhen changing into Yu. |
| 世下戌财，正值旬空，日辰丑又刑戌，应爻辰财又自刑，初下子水动，化未土，与世下戌土又相刑，用爻受制，病必重也，子孙为药，午为自刑，用药差误，所以病剧也。 | 世爻下面的戌财，正好值旬空，日辰丑又刑戌；应爻的辰财又自刑；初爻下的子水发动，变出未土，和世爻下的戌土又相刑；用爻受制，病一定重；子孙是药，午是自刑，用药有差错，所以病加剧。 | The Xu Wealth beneath the Self is exactly in Void, the Chou Day further punishes Xu, and the Chen Wealth on the Other line punishes itself; the Zi Water on the first line moves and changes into Wei Earth, which again punishes the Xu Earth beneath the Self; the Subject line is restrained, so the illness is surely heavy; Output is the medicine and Wu punishes itself, so the medicine was mistaken, and that is why the illness worsened. |
| 逢巳午日，子旺生财，始病退。 | 逢到巳日、午日，子孙旺相来生财，病才开始退。 | On a Si or Wu day, Output is strong and generates Wealth, and only then does the illness retreat. |
| 若至日病加重，必难疗也。 | 如果到了（那一）日病加重，就一定难治。 | If on reaching that day the illness grows heavier, it will surely be hard to cure. |

> **按**:两个断例挂在这里：坎之蹇（应爻旺相本可服药，却变出申金冲刑寅木子孙，服药不效；子旺官衰，新病不死只是难痊）、震之豫（子孙是药而午自刑，用药差误，巳午日子旺生财病才退）。书里最后一句「若至日病加重」缺了是哪一日，译文按上下文译作「那一日」，不猜是哪天。「自刑」是辰午酉亥四个地支遇到自己（如午见午）。「六冲」的新病与久病的不同，见 LY.ILL.YY.07。「良医」「名医而药不效」这一组和黄金策讲医药（LY.ILL.HJC.58 起）、增删卜易讲延医（LY.ILL.ZS.26 起）是三家各说各的一套：黄金策以应爻为医、子孙为药，易隐同；增删卜易另有以子孙为忧神解药、应爻为医人的说法。
>
> **Note:** Two cases hang here: Kan into Jian (the Other line strong and so medicine should work, but it changes into Shen Metal which clashes with and punishes Yin Wood Output and the medicine fails; Output strong and Pressure weak, so a recent illness does not kill and is only hard to cure) and Zhen into Yu (Output is the medicine but Wu punishes itself, the medicine was mistaken, and the illness retreated only on a Si or Wu day when Output strong generated Wealth). The last sentence of the second case, 'if on reaching the day the illness grows heavier', lacks which day, and the translation renders it 'that day' without guessing. 'Self-punishment' (zixing) is the four branches Chen, Wu, You and Hai meeting themselves (Wu meeting Wu). For the difference between a recent illness and a long one under Six-Clash, see LY.ILL.YY.07. This group on the good doctor and 'famous doctor, ineffective medicine' is a set of its own beside the Huangjin Ce's treatment of medicine (from LY.ILL.HJC.58) and the Zengshan Buyi's on calling a doctor (from LY.ILL.ZS.26): the Huangjin Ce takes the Other line as the doctor and Output as the medicine, as the Yi Yin does; the Zengshan Buyi also has its own account of Output as the spirit that relieves worry and the Other line as the doctor.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.20、LY.ILL.YY.22、LY.ILL.YY.26、LY.ILL.HJC.58、LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.ZS.26、LY.ILL.ZS.29、LY.ILL.ZS.30

---

## LY.ILL.YY.22 医药·天医（《法法赋》）：天医生世是良医、克世是庸医；日辰克医医学不精、医克日辰用药不当；世爻用爻属金天医在巳、木在亥、火在寅、水土在申则效

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《法法赋》说：天医生世爻的，是好医生；天医克世爻的，是庸医；日辰克医生的，医术不精；医生克日辰的，用药不恰当。世爻、用爻属金的，天医在巳有效；属木的，天医在亥有效；属火的，天医在寅有效；属水、土的，天医在申有效。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Fafa Fu says: if the Heavenly Doctor generates the Self, he is a good doctor; if it controls the Self, a mediocre one; if the Day controls the doctor, his medical learning is not refined; if the doctor controls the Day, the medicine used is not fitting. If the Self line and Subject line are Metal, the Heavenly Doctor at Si is effective; if Wood, at Hai; if Fire, at Yin; if Water or Earth, at Shen.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3013 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3013*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 法法赋曰，天医生世者，良医。 | 《法法赋》说：天医生世的，是好医生。 | The Fafa Fu says: if the Heavenly Doctor generates the Self, he is a good doctor. |
| 天医克世者，庸医。 | 天医克世的，是庸医。 | If the Heavenly Doctor controls the Self, a mediocre doctor. |
| 日辰克医者，医学不精。 | 日辰克医生的，医术不精。 | If the Day controls the doctor, his medical learning is not refined. |
| 医克日辰者，用药不当也。 | 医生克日辰的，用药不恰当。 | If the doctor controls the Day, the medicine used is not fitting. |
| 世爻用爻属金，天医在巳者效也。 | 世爻、用爻属金的，天医在巳有效。 | If the Self line and the Subject line are Metal, the Heavenly Doctor at Si is effective. |
| 属木，天医在亥者效也。 | 属木的，天医在亥有效。 | If Wood, the Heavenly Doctor at Hai is effective. |
| 属火，天医在寅者效。 | 属火的，天医在寅有效。 | If Fire, the Heavenly Doctor at Yin is effective. |
| 属水土，天医在申者效也。 | 属水、土的，天医在申有效。 | If Water or Earth, the Heavenly Doctor at Shen is effective. |

> **按**:「天医」是一颗神煞。这里的天医按世爻用爻的五行取，取的是那一行长生的地支（金长生在巳、木在亥、火在寅、水土在申）；易冒讲的天医是按月取（丁神），不是这一个（见易冒的天医，书里另有说明）。「法法赋」是易隐引的一部书，书里没有再介绍。
>
> **Note:** The 'Heavenly Doctor' is a spirit-star. Here it is taken by the element of the Self and Subject lines, using the branch where that element is born (Metal is born at Si, Wood at Hai, Fire at Yin, Water and Earth at Shen); the Heavenly Doctor the Yi Mao speaks of is taken by the month (the Ding spirit) and is not this one (the Yi Mao explains its own). The 'Fafa Fu' is a work the Yi Yin quotes and the book does not introduce.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.21、LY.ILL.YY.23

---

## LY.ILL.YY.23 医药·脉（《董和筮秘》）：脉宜细察，身爻为主；月卦临金脉弦紧代革、临木洪长虚动、临水芤滑濡动、值火数大促短、值土沉实滞涩

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《董和筮秘》说：脉要仔细察，以身爻为主。月卦临在金上，脉一定是弦、紧、代、革；临在木上，脉一定是洪、长、虚、动；临在水上，脉一定是芤、滑、濡、动；值火，脉一定是数、大、促、短；值土，脉一定是沉、实、滞、涩。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Dong He Shimi says: the pulse should be examined carefully, with the Body line as the master. If the month-hexagram falls on Metal, the pulse is surely wiry, tight, intermittent and leathery; on Wood, surging, long, vacuous and stirring; on Water, hollow, slippery, soft and stirring; on Fire, rapid, large, hurried and short; on Earth, deep, full, sluggish and rough.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3013 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3013*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 董和筮秘曰，脉宜细察，身爻为主。 | 《董和筮秘》说：脉要仔细察，以身爻为主。 | The Dong He Shimi says: the pulse should be examined carefully, with the Body line as the master. |
| 月卦临金，脉必弦紧代革也。 | 月卦临在金上，脉一定是弦、紧、代、革。 | If the month-hexagram falls on Metal, the pulse is surely wiry, tight, intermittent and leathery. |
| 临木，脉必洪长虚动也。 | 临在木上，脉一定是洪、长、虚、动。 | On Wood, the pulse is surely surging, long, vacuous and stirring. |
| 临水，脉必芤滑濡动也。 | 临在水上，脉一定是芤、滑、濡、动。 | On Water, the pulse is surely hollow, slippery, soft and stirring. |
| 值火，脉必数大促短也。 | 值火，脉一定是数、大、促、短。 | On Fire, the pulse is surely rapid, large, hurried and short. |
| 值土，脉必沉实滞涩也。 | 值土，脉一定是沉、实、滞、涩。 | On Earth, the pulse is surely deep, full, sluggish and rough. |

> **按**:弦、紧、代、革、洪、长、虚、动、芤、滑、濡、数、大、促、短、沉、实、滞、涩都是中医脉名，译文用通行的英文脉名的字面。「身爻」「月卦」书里没有在这里解释（月卦身法，见 LY.ILL.HJC.35）。这一段是易隐引的《董和筮秘》，不是易隐自己的话。
>
> **Note:** Xian, jin, dai, ge, hong, chang, xu, dong, kou, hua, ru, shu, da, cu, duan, chen, shi, zhi and se are all names of pulses in Chinese medicine, rendered by the usual literal English pulse names. The 'Body line' and 'month-hexagram' are not explained here (for the month-body method see LY.ILL.HJC.35). This passage is the Dong He Shimi as quoted in the Yi Yin, not the Yi Yin's own words.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.22、LY.ILL.YY.24、LY.ILL.HJC.35

---

## LY.ILL.YY.24 医药·推医是何人：子孙为用，五行定方位姓氏——金宜西方金旁姓氏、木宜东方草木旁、水宜北方点水旁、火宜南方日火旁、土宜土旁及丹散医人；子孙带贵人宜有官职人、带华盖宜僧道

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:推断请来的医生是什么样的人，拿子孙做用：子孙属金，适合西方的、姓氏带金字旁的人；属木，适合东方的、姓氏带草头或木字旁的人；属水，适合北方的、姓氏带三点水的人；属火，适合南方的、姓氏带日字旁或火字旁的人；属土，适合姓氏带土字旁的，以及用丹药散剂的医生。子孙带贵人，适合有官职的人；子孙带华盖，适合僧人道士。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To work out what kind of person the doctor is, Output is the Subject: if Output is Metal, it suits the west, a person whose surname has the metal radical; if Wood, the east, a surname with the grass or wood radical; if Water, the north, a surname with the water radical; if Fire, the south, a surname with the sun or fire radical; if Earth, a surname with the earth radical, and a doctor who works with cinnabar and powders. If Output carries the Noble, a person holding an official post; if Output carries the Canopy, a monk or Daoist.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3013 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3013*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又推医是何人，子孙为用。 | 又，推断请来的医生是什么样的人，拿子孙做用。 | Again, to work out what kind of person the doctor is, Output is the Subject. |
| 子孙属金，宜西方，金傍姓氏人也。 | 子孙属金，适合西方的、姓氏带金字旁的人。 | If Output is Metal, it suits the west, a person whose surname has the metal radical. |
| 属木，宜东方，草木傍姓氏也。 | 属木，适合东方的、姓氏带草头或木字旁的人。 | If Wood, the east, a person whose surname has the grass or wood radical. |
| 属水，宜北方，点水傍姓氏人也。 | 属水，适合北方的、姓氏带三点水的人。 | If Water, the north, a person whose surname has the water radical. |
| 属火，宜南方，日火傍姓氏人也。 | 属火，适合南方的、姓氏带日字旁或火字旁的人。 | If Fire, the south, a person whose surname has the sun or fire radical. |
| 属土，宜土傍姓氏，及丹散医人也。 | 属土，适合姓氏带土字旁的，以及用丹药散剂的医生。 | If Earth, a person whose surname has the earth radical, and a doctor who works with cinnabar and powders. |
| 子孙带贵人，宜有官职人。 | 子孙带贵人，适合有官职的人。 | If Output carries the Noble, a person holding an official post. |
| 子孙带华盖，宜僧道也。 | 子孙带华盖，适合僧人道士。 | If Output carries the Canopy, a monk or Daoist. |

> **按**:这一条和增删卜易讲的「延医于子孙之方，姓名按五行：水点水、火象火、木草头木旁、土有土」（LY.ILL.ZS.34）几乎一样，是同一个说法两本书都有。「华盖」是一颗神煞，主僧道孤高；「贵人」也是神煞，主有人扶助。
>
> **Note:** This entry is almost the same as the Zengshan Buyi's 'calling the doctor in the direction of Output, the surname by the element: Water with the water radical, Fire with the fire image, Wood with the grass or wood radical, Earth with earth' (LY.ILL.ZS.34); the two books have the same saying. The 'Canopy' (Huagai) is a spirit-star for monks and Daoists and the solitary and aloof; the 'Noble' is also a spirit-star, for help from others.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.23、LY.ILL.YY.25、LY.ILL.ZS.34

---

## LY.ILL.YY.25 医药·治法与用药：官鬼属金宜灸不宜丸药、属木宜针不宜汤药、属水宜温药丸药不宜针、属火宜凉药汤药不宜草头药饮片、属土宜咬咀不宜灸（取克鬼者、忌生鬼者）；鬼伤二间宜宽胸膈；财外动合用宜吐、内动宜泻；鬼在外卦宜外表、伏内卦宜内表

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用药不要轻易乱投。官鬼属金的，适合灸，不适合丸药（火能克金，土生金）；鬼属木的，适合针，不适合汤药（金能克木，水生木）；鬼属水的，适合温药、丸药，不适合针（土克水，金生水）；鬼属火的，适合凉药、汤药，不适合草头药和饮片（水克火，木生火）；鬼属土的，适合咬咀，不适合灸（木克土，火生土）。此外，鬼伤到两个间爻的，适合宽胸膈；世爻旺又有扶助的，不妨再进补；财爻在外卦发动合用爻的，适合吐法，在内卦发动合用爻的，适合泻法；用爻、鬼爻出现或在外卦的，适合治外表，伏藏在内卦的，适合治内。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Medicine is not to be given lightly. If the Pressure line is Metal, moxibustion suits and pills do not (Fire can control Metal, while Earth generates Metal); if the ghost is Wood, acupuncture suits and decoctions do not (Metal can control Wood, while Water generates Wood); if Water, warm medicines and pills suit and acupuncture does not (Earth controls Water, while Metal generates Water); if Fire, cool medicines and decoctions suit and herbal medicines and sliced drugs do not (Water controls Fire, while Wood generates Fire); if Earth, crushed herbs suit and moxibustion does not (Wood controls Earth, while Fire generates Earth). Besides: if the ghost harms the two between lines, ease the chest and diaphragm; if the Self is strong and supported, there is no harm in tonics again; if Wealth moves in the outer trigram and combines with the Subject line, an emetic suits, and in the inner trigram, a purgative; if the Subject line and the Pressure line appear openly or are in the outer trigram, treat the exterior, and if hidden in the inner trigram, treat the interior.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3013 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3013*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 至于君臣佐使药莫轻投。 | 至于君臣佐使这些配药的讲究，药不要轻易乱投。 | As for the 'sovereign, minister, assistant and envoy' of compounding, medicine should not be given lightly. |
| 官鬼属金，宜灸，不宜丸药也(火可克金，土则生金)。 | 官鬼属金的，适合灸，不适合丸药（火能克金，土生金）。 | If the Pressure line is Metal, moxibustion suits and pills do not (Fire can control Metal, while Earth generates Metal). |
| 鬼属木，宜针，不宜汤药也(金能克木，水则生木)。 | 鬼属木的，适合针，不适合汤药（金能克木，水生木）。 | If the ghost is Wood, acupuncture suits and decoctions do not (Metal can control Wood, while Water generates Wood). |
| 鬼属水，宜温药，丸药，不宜针也(土克水，金生水)。 | 鬼属水的，适合温药、丸药，不适合针（土克水，金生水）。 | If the ghost is Water, warm medicines and pills suit and acupuncture does not (Earth controls Water, while Metal generates Water). |
| 鬼属火，宜凉药，汤药，不宜草头药，与饮片也(以水克火，而木生火)。 | 鬼属火的，适合凉药、汤药，不适合草头药和饮片（以水克火，而木生火）。 | If the ghost is Fire, cool medicines and decoctions suit and herbal medicines and sliced drugs do not (Water controls Fire, while Wood generates Fire). |
| 鬼属土，宜咬咀，不宜灸也(以木克土，而火生土)。 | 鬼属土的，适合咬咀，不适合灸（以木克土，而火生土）。 | If the ghost is Earth, crushed herbs suit and moxibustion does not (Wood controls Earth, while Fire generates Earth). |
| 他若鬼伤二间者，宜宽胸膈也。 | 另外，鬼伤到两个间爻的，适合宽胸膈。 | Again, if the ghost harms the two between lines, ease the chest and diaphragm. |
| 世旺有扶者，不妨再补也。 | 世爻旺又有扶助的，不妨再进补。 | If the Self is strong and supported, there is no harm in tonics again. |
| 财外动合用者，宜吐。 | 财爻在外卦发动合用爻的，适合吐法。 | If Wealth moves in the outer trigram and combines with the Subject line, an emetic suits. |
| 财内动合用者，宜泻。 | 财爻在内卦发动合用爻的，适合泻法。 | If Wealth moves in the inner trigram and combines with the Subject line, a purgative suits. |
| 用爻鬼爻出现，与在外卦者，宜外表。 | 用爻和鬼爻出现，或者在外卦的，适合治外表。 | If the Subject line and the Pressure line appear openly, or are in the outer trigram, treat the exterior. |
| 伏藏在内卦者，宜内表也。 | 伏藏在内卦的，适合治内。 | If they are hidden in the inner trigram, treat the interior. |

> **打架**:治法与药性要不要按鬼的五行来选：易隐说得很具体——鬼属金宜灸、属木宜针、属水宜温药丸药、属火宜凉药汤药、属土宜咬咀，原则是用克鬼的、避开生鬼的；黄金策也有火鬼旺宜大寒药、水鬼旺宜大热药的说法（LY.ILL.HJC.82、LY.ILL.HJC.84），方向一致。增删卜易的李我平驳这些说法拘泥，说子孙不是针灸之药，胶柱鼓瑟会坑陷人命（LY.ILL.ZS.40）。三家意见不同，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether the way of treatment and the nature of the drug is chosen by the element of the ghost: the Yi Yin is very specific — Metal, moxibustion; Wood, acupuncture; Water, warm medicines and pills; Fire, cool medicines and decoctions; Earth, crushed herbs — the principle being to use what controls the ghost and avoid what generates it; the Huangjin Ce also has the sayings that a strong Fire ghost suits very cold medicine and a strong Water ghost very hot medicine (LY.ILL.HJC.82 and LY.ILL.HJC.84), in the same direction. Li Woping of the Zengshan Buyi rejects these as rigid, saying Output is not the medicine of acupuncture and moxibustion and that sticking to the letter pits and drowns lives (LY.ILL.ZS.40). The three differ and the books do not decide.

> **按**:「君臣佐使」是配药时药的四种分工。「咬咀」（也写作㕮咀）是把药切碎或捣成粗末，用来煎汤。「表」是身体的表层，「外表」「内表」书里没有再解释，译文按字面译作治外表、治内。「二间」是世应之间的两爻（间爻）。括号里的话是书里自己的理由。
>
> **Note:** 'Sovereign, minister, assistant and envoy' are the four divisions of labour among the drugs in compounding. 'Yaozu' (also written fu zu) is cutting medicine up or pounding it into coarse powder for decoction. 'Biao' is the surface layer of the body, and 'outer biao' and 'inner biao' are not explained further and are rendered literally as treating the exterior and the interior. The 'two between' are the two lines between the Self and Other (the between lines). The words in brackets are the book's own reasons.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.24、LY.ILL.YY.26、LY.ILL.HJC.82、LY.ILL.HJC.84、LY.ILL.ZS.40

---

## LY.ILL.YY.26 医药·受病在此所治在彼（周杰）：金虎鬼冲伤用是肝受病然治肺不治肝，木虎鬼脾受病治肝，水虎鬼心受病治肾，火虎鬼肺受病治心，土虎鬼肾受病治脾；颗师：子孙动者误服药

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:周杰说：有的病是在这里受的，该治的却在那里。比如金的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肝经受了病（金来克木），但应当治肺，不能治肝（这就是所说的去掉害我的那一个，其余照这个办法推）；木的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是脾经受病（木来伤土），该治肝，不能治脾；水的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是心经受病（水来伤火），该治肾，不能治心；火的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肺经受病（火来伤金），该治心，不能治肺；土的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肾经受病（土来伤水），该治脾，不能治肾（出自《松径玄谈》）。颗师说：子孙发动的，是吃错了药。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Zhou Jie says: in some cases the illness is received in one place while the treatment lies in another. For example, if a Metal Tiger ghost clashes with and wounds the Subject line, the liver channel has received the illness (Metal comes to control Wood), yet one may treat the lungs and must not treat the liver (this is what is meant by removing what harms me; the rest follow this); a Wood Tiger ghost wounding the Subject line: the spleen channel has received the illness (Wood comes to wound Earth), treat the liver, not the spleen; a Water Tiger ghost: the heart channel (Water wounds Fire), treat the kidneys, not the heart; a Fire Tiger ghost: the lung channel (Fire wounds Metal), treat the heart, not the lungs; an Earth Tiger ghost: the kidney channel (Earth wounds Water), treat the spleen, not the kidneys (from the Songjing Xuantan). Master Ke says: when Output moves, the wrong medicine has been taken.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3013–3015 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, lines 3013–3015*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 周杰曰，有受病在此，所治在彼者。 | 周杰说：有的病是在这里受的，该治的却在那里。 | Zhou Jie says: in some cases the illness is received in one place while the treatment lies in another. |
| 如金虎鬼冲伤用，乃肝经受病(金来克木)。 | 比如金的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肝经受了病（金来克木）。 | For example, if a Metal Tiger ghost clashes with and wounds the Subject line, the liver channel has received the illness (Metal comes to control Wood). |
| 然可治肺，不可治肝也(所云去其害我者，余仿此)。 | 但是应当治肺，不能治肝（所说的去掉害我的那一个，其余照这个办法推）。 | But one may treat the lungs and must not treat the liver (this is what is meant by removing what harms me; the rest follow this). |
| 木虎鬼冲伤用，乃脾经受病(木来伤土)。 | 木的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是脾经受病（木来伤土）。 | If a Wood Tiger ghost clashes with and wounds the Subject line, the spleen channel has received the illness (Wood comes to wound Earth). |
| 然可治肝，不可治脾也。 | 但是应当治肝，不能治脾。 | But one may treat the liver and must not treat the spleen. |
| 水虎鬼冲伤用，乃心经受病(水来伤火)。 | 水的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是心经受病（水来伤火）。 | If a Water Tiger ghost clashes with and wounds the Subject line, the heart channel has received the illness (Water comes to wound Fire). |
| 然可治肾，不可治心也。 | 但是应当治肾，不能治心。 | But one may treat the kidneys and must not treat the heart. |
| 火虎鬼冲伤用，乃肺经受病(火来伤金)。 | 火的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肺经受病（火来伤金）。 | If a Fire Tiger ghost clashes with and wounds the Subject line, the lung channel has received the illness (Fire comes to wound Metal). |
| 然可治心，不可治肺也。 | 但是应当治心，不能治肺。 | But one may treat the heart and must not treat the lungs. |
| 土虎鬼冲伤用，乃肾经受病(土来伤水)。 | 土的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肾经受病（土来伤水）。 | If an Earth Tiger ghost clashes with and wounds the Subject line, the kidney channel has received the illness (Earth comes to wound Water). |
| 然可治脾，不可治肾也(出松径玄谈)。 | 但是应当治脾，不能治肾（出自《松径玄谈》）。 | But one may treat the spleen and must not treat the kidneys (from the Songjing Xuantan). |
| 颗师曰，子孙动者误服药。 | 颗师说：子孙发动的，是吃错了药。 | Master Ke says: when Output moves, the wrong medicine has been taken. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 3038 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, line 3038*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 庚寅年戊寅月戊寅日，妻占夫病，得剥之观。 | 庚寅年戊寅月戊寅日，妻子占丈夫的病，得到剥卦变观卦。 | In the Gengyin year, the Wuyin month, on the Wuyin day, a wife divined about her husband's illness and obtained Bo changing into Guan. |
| 官爻受三传生合，人皆云吉。 | 官爻受到三传（年、月、日）的生、合，大家都说吉。 | The Pressure line was generated and combined by the three rulers (Year, Month and Day), and everyone said it was auspicious. |
| 不知本宫午官自刑，伏墓戌土下，傍爻子动冲克官，极凶。 | 不知道本宫的午官自己刑自己，伏在墓上的戌土下面，旁边的子孙爻发动来冲克官爻，极凶。 | They did not know that the Wu Pressure of the home palace punishes itself and lies hidden beneath Xu Earth, its tomb, while the Output line beside it moves and clashes with and controls Pressure — extremely ominous. |
| 子动，误服药。 | 子孙动，是吃错了药。 | Output moves: wrong medicine was taken. |
| 子又化绝于巳，必始因药不投而后不延医也。 | 子孙又变出巳而绝，一定是起初因为药不对症，后来就不请医生了。 | Output further changes into Si, where it is in Extinction; surely at first the medicine did not fit, and afterwards no doctor was called. |
| 只有三传生合官鬼，不过暂时少甦，至甲申日，元神绝而忌神生，果死也。 | 只有三传生合官鬼，不过让他暂时稍微苏醒一下；到甲申日，原神绝、忌神生，果然死了。 | Only the three rulers generating and combining with Pressure gave him a brief revival; on the Jiashen day the Support was extinct and the Adversary born, and he indeed died. |

> **打架**:子孙发动是好是坏：颗师说「子孙动者误服药」，子孙一动就是吃错了药，断例（剥之观）也照这个断，子孙发动冲克官爻、又变出巳绝，药不对症；黄金策、卜筮正宗、增删卜易都是另一个方向——子孙发动制鬼，是药能见效、甚至不药自愈（LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.BSZZ.12、LY.ILL.ZS.30）。同一个爻象，两个相反的说法，书里没有裁决。周杰讲的「受病在此所治在彼」则是易隐这一组里独有的说法，别处没有。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether a moving Output line is good or bad: Master Ke says 'when Output moves, the wrong medicine has been taken', and the case (Bo into Guan) is judged the same way — Output moving to clash with and control Pressure and then changing into Si in Extinction, the medicine did not fit; the Huangjin Ce, the Bushi Zhengzong and the Zengshan Buyi go the other way — Output moving to restrain the ghost means the medicine will take effect, even that he recovers without medicine (LY.ILL.HJC.32, LY.ILL.BSZZ.12 and LY.ILL.ZS.30). The same line-image has two opposite sayings and the books do not decide. Zhou Jie's 'the illness is received here and the treatment lies there' is a saying peculiar to this group in the Yi Yin and is found nowhere else.

> **按**:「虎鬼」是白虎带鬼。周杰讲的是一种间接的治法：病是某一行的鬼伤了用爻所在的那一脏引起的，治的却不是被伤的那一脏，而是「去掉那个害我的」，也就是鬼那一行自己所属的脏——金鬼伤木（肝），治肺；木鬼伤土（脾），治肝；水鬼伤火（心），治肾；火鬼伤金（肺），治心；土鬼伤水（肾），治脾。书里只给了这张对应表和一句「去其害我者，余仿此」，没有再解释。「颗师」「松径玄谈」都是易隐引的人名书名，书里没有再介绍。
>
> **Note:** The 'Tiger ghost' is the White Tiger carrying the ghost. Zhou Jie gives an indirect way of treatment: the illness is caused by a ghost of one element wounding the viscus that the Subject line stands for, yet what is treated is not the viscus wounded but 'what harms me is removed', that is, the viscus belonging to the ghost's own element — a Metal ghost wounding Wood (the liver), treat the lungs; a Wood ghost wounding Earth (the spleen), treat the liver; a Water ghost wounding Fire (the heart), treat the kidneys; a Fire ghost wounding Metal (the lungs), treat the heart; an Earth ghost wounding Water (the kidneys), treat the spleen. The book gives only this table and the phrase 'remove what harms me; the rest follow this' and no further explanation. 'Master Ke' and the 'Songjing Xuantan' are a person and a work the Yi Yin cites and the book does not introduce.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.25、LY.ILL.YY.27、LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.HJC.42、LY.ILL.BSZZ.12、LY.ILL.ZS.30、LY.ILL.ZS.37

## LY.ILL.ZS.10 身临福德势虽险而堪医有两说：自占病子孙持世不药而痊，临空破者实空实破之日愈；代占六亲之病不必看用神衰旺即知其安，因子孙是喜悦之神（觉子：占父母病、妻占夫病子孙动反不是喜悦之神）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:身上临着福德，病势虽凶险也可以医治，有两种情形。自己占病：子孙持世，不吃药也会痊愈；临空亡、月破的，到填实空、填实破的那天就好。替别人占六亲的病：不必看用神旺衰，就知道他平安，因为子孙是喜悦的神，只要旺相，如果在卦中发动，也是吉庆。觉子补充：占父母的病，子孙持世又发动，会克掉父母的元神；妻子占丈夫的病，子孙动会伤丈夫，这些情形它都不是喜悦的神，要通变来看。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With the body sitting on Output-virtue, though the danger looks great the illness can be treated, and there are two cases. Divining one's own illness: with Output holding the Self line, one recovers without medicine; if it sits on Void or Month-break, he recovers on the day the Void or the break is filled. Divining a relation's illness for another: one need not look at whether the Subject is strong or weak to know he will be safe, because Output is the spirit of joy, and so long as it is strong, if it moves in the hexagram it is also a sign of good fortune. Juezi adds: in divining a parent's illness, an Output line that holds the Self line and moves controls away the Support of the Resource line; when a wife divines her husband's illness, a moving Output harms the husband; in these cases it is not the spirit of joy, and one must look at it flexibly.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12686 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12686*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身临福德，势虽险而堪医。 | 身上临着福德，病势虽然凶险还是可以医治。 | The body sitting on Output-virtue: though the danger looks great, it can be treated. |
| 亦有两说： | 也有两种说法： | There are also two views: |

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12687 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12687*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ①自占病：子孙持世，不药而痊； | ①自己占病：子孙持世，不吃药也会痊愈； | 1. Divining one's own illness: with Output holding the Self line, one recovers without medicine; |
| 临空破者，实空、实破之日而愈。 | 临空亡、月破的，到填实空、填实破的那天就好。 | if it sits on Void or Month-break, he recovers on the day the Void or the break is filled. |

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12688 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12688*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ②代占六亲之病：不必看用神之衰旺，即知其安，何也？ | ②替别人占六亲的病：不必看用神旺衰，就知道他平安，为什么呢？ | 2. Divining a relation's illness for another: one need not look at whether the Subject is strong or weak to know he will be safe. Why? |
| 盖子孙乃喜悦之神，只要旺相，若动于卦中者，亦为吉庆。 | 因为子孙是喜悦的神，只要旺相，如果在卦中发动，也是吉庆。 | Because Output is the spirit of joy, and so long as it is strong, if it moves in the hexagram it is also a sign of good fortune. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 12689 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 12689*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如：寅月乙卯日(旬空：子丑)，占妻病，得“屯之节” | 比如：寅月乙卯日（旬空：子、丑），占妻子的病，得屯卦变节卦。 | For example: in the Yin month, on a Yimao day (void: Zi and Chou), a divination on a wife's illness gave Zhun changing into Jie. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 12720 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 12720*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 断曰：未日即愈。 | 断语：未日就会好。 | Judgment: she will recover on the Wei day. |
| 彼问：妻财不上卦，何言即愈？ | 他问：妻财不上卦，为什么说马上就好？ | He asked: Wealth is not in the hexagram, so why do you say she will recover at once? |
| 余曰：子孙化子孙，乃喜悦之神，嫌其太旺，许未日愈者，木墓于未，未日而喜悦也，尊正若不全安，何喜之有？ | 我说：子孙变子孙，是喜悦的神，嫌它太旺，断定未日好，是因为木墓在未，到未日就喜悦了；正室夫人要是不能全然平安，哪里还有喜悦？ | I said: Output changing into Output is the spirit of joy; I object that it is too strong, and I promise recovery on the Wei day because Wood has its tomb in Wei, so on the Wei day there is joy; if your lady were not wholly safe, what joy would there be? |
| 果午日退灾，未日大愈。 | 果然午日灾退，未日大好了。 | Indeed the trouble receded on the Wu day and she was well on the Wei day. |

**觉子(李文辉,《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12722–12724 行 · *Juezi (Li Wenhui, Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, lines 12722–12724*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 觉子曰：占父母病，子孙持世而动者，克去父母之元神； | 觉子说：占父母的病，子孙持世又发动的，会克掉父母的元神； | Juezi says: in divining a parent's illness, an Output line that holds the Self line and moves controls away the Support of the Resource line; |
| 妻占夫病，子动伤夫，皆非喜悦之神也，须宜通变观之。 | 妻子占丈夫的病，子孙动会伤丈夫，这些情形它都不是喜悦的神，要通变来看。 | in a wife's divining her husband's illness, a moving Output harms the husband; in these cases it is not the spirit of joy, and one must look at it flexibly. |

> **按**:这一条和上一条（LY.ILL.ZS.09）是一对：那里讲鬼持世，这里讲子孙持世，都拆成自占和代占。黄金策的对应句是 LY.ILL.HJC.35；那里把「身」解作用爻或月卦身，野鹤这里讲的是子孙持世，看的是世爻。「填实」是落空的爻等到自己的地支值日，把空填上。
>
> **Note:** This entry is a pair with the previous one (LY.ILL.ZS.09): there Pressure holds the Self line, here Output does, and both are split into divining oneself and divining another. The matching line of the Huangjin Ce is LY.ILL.HJC.35, which explains the 'body' as the Subject line or the month's hexagram Body; what the Old Man treats here is Output holding the Self line, and so he looks at the Self line. To 'fill' is for a Void line to wait until its own branch rules the day and fills the void.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.35、LY.ILL.ZS.09、LY.ILL.ZS.13

## LY.ILL.ZS.26 问卜求神、延医服药有没有用：术不精则不效；病要分有根无根，有根者可救、无根者不能（像移花接木，上盖下浇自能渐活，无根之木浇盖无益）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:问卜求神、请医吃药是有道理的：不灵验有两个原因，一是术不精，精了没有不灵验的；二是病要分有根无根，有根的可以救，无根的救不了，就像移栽花木，有根的上面遮盖、下面浇灌，自然慢慢活过来，无根的树、无蒂的花，浇了盖了也没有用。人不懂这个，一次不灵验就不信神和药，是冤枉了它。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** There is reason in consulting the oracle and praying to the spirits, calling a doctor and taking medicine: when it fails there are two causes, one that the art is not refined, for if refined nothing fails, and the other that an illness must be told as rooted or rootless, the rooted being savable and the rootless not, as with moving a flowering plant, where one that has roots is covered above and watered below and revives gradually, while a tree without root or a flower without stem gains nothing from watering and covering. People who do not understand this lose faith in the spirits and medicine at the first failure, and wrong them.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13212 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13212*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：有人而问余曰：问卜求神，延医服药，有是理乎？ | 野鹤说：有人问我：问卜求神、请医吃药，有这个道理吗？ | The Old Man of Yehe says: someone asked me: to consult the oracle and pray to spirits, to call a doctor and take medicine, is there any reason in this? |
| 余曰：若无此理，伏羲神农，开天大圣人也，画卦尝药可为乎？ | 我说：如果没有这个道理，伏羲、神农是开天辟地的大圣人，他们画卦、尝药能这样做吗？ | I said: if there were no reason in it, Fuxi and Shennong were great sages who opened up the world, could they have drawn the trigrams and tasted the herbs? |
| 彼曰：然则每有不效者，何也？ | 他说：那么常常有不灵验的，是什么缘故？ | He said: then why does it so often fail? |
| 余曰：有二说焉，一在术之不精，精则无有不效者； | 我说：有两种说法，一是术不精，精了就没有不灵验的； | I said: there are two accounts. One is that the art is not refined, for if refined nothing fails; |
| 一人之疾，要分有根无根。 | 另一种是，一个人的病，要分有根还是无根。 | the other is that a person's illness must be told as rooted or rootless. |
| 有根者可以救之，无根者则不能也。 | 有根的可以救，无根的就不能。 | The rooted can be saved; the rootless cannot. |
| 人之星辰过宫，或是交运脱运，谓之移花接木之年，移其有根之花木者，上则遮盖，下宜浇溉，自能渐活，不浇不盖，见日亦枯，有病而求神服药者，即此意也； | 人的星辰过宫，或者是交运、脱运，叫做移花接木的年份，移栽有根的花木，上面要遮盖，下面要浇灌，自然慢慢活过来，不浇不盖，见了太阳也枯死，有病而去求神、吃药，就是这个意思； | When a person's stars pass into another palace, or he enters or leaves a fortune cycle, it is called a year of grafting flowers: for a flowering plant that has roots and is moved, one covers it above and waters it below, and it revives gradually, while without watering and covering it withers in the sun; one who is ill and prays and takes medicine is in the same case; |
| 若使无根之木，无蒂之花，虽浇之盖之，亦无益矣，故尝有求神服药而弗效者。 | 如果是无根的树、无蒂的花，虽然浇它、盖它，也没有用，所以常常有求神吃药却不灵验的。 | but a tree without root or a flower without stem gains nothing from watering and covering, and so there are those who pray and take medicine and are not cured. |
| 此天地之至理也，人不识此，一不效，而不信神药焉，岂不冤哉！ | 这是天地间最根本的道理，人不懂这个，一次不灵验，就不信神和药，难道不冤枉吗！ | This is the supreme principle of heaven and earth; people who do not understand it lose faith in the spirits and medicine at the first failure; is that not unjust? |

> **按**:「有根、无根」书里只用移花接木打了比方，没有说怎样从卦上看出病有根无根。「星辰过宫」「交运脱运」是命理里说的运势交接。
>
> **Note:** For 'rooted' and 'rootless' the book uses only the figure of grafting flowers and does not say how to see from the hexagram whether an illness is rooted or rootless. 'Stars passing into another palace' and 'entering or leaving a fortune cycle' are the handing over of fortune spoken of in fate reckoning.

相关:LY.ILL.ZS.27

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.27 鬼是忧神、子孙是喜悦解忧之神：鬼持世或动，得子孙动而制之，此医可请手到成功；子孙临应爻是明医，因为子孙克去的是忧不是邪魔（例：三十余人泪眼相对，子水兄爻旬空，半夜子时退灾）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼这一爻，不是鬼也不是病，是忧神：替儿子占病的，父母爱子的心，没病时都怕他生病担忧，何况已经病了；自己占病的，性命在一呼一吸之间，一个人起不来，全家惊慌，谁不担忧？所以官鬼就是一家人的忧神，能解这份忧的，只有子孙爻。鬼持世或在卦中发动，得到子孙发动来制服它，这个医生可以请，一下手就成功；子孙临在应爻上，是高明的医生，因为子孙克掉的是忧神，我就没有忧愁了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Pressure line is neither ghost nor illness but the spirit of worry: in divining a son's illness, the parents' love for their son is such that even before he is ill they fear illness and worry, and how much more when he is already ill; in divining one's own illness, life hangs on a breath, and when one person cannot rise the whole household is alarmed, and who would not worry? So the Pressure line is the spirit of worry of the whole household, and what can relieve that worry is only the Output line. If Pressure holds the Self line or moves in the hexagram and Output moves to restrain it, this doctor can be called and his hand will succeed; if Output sits on the Other line he is a brilliant doctor, because what Output controls away is the spirit of worry, and then I have no worry.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13214 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13214*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼作忧神休妄动，福为喜悦要生扶。 | 鬼是忧愁的神，不要乱动；子孙（福）是喜悦的神，要有生扶。 | Pressure is the spirit of worry and should not move at random; Output should be the spirit of joy, generated and supported. |
| 自占代占，鬼乃犹疑之神也，若持世，及动于卦中，得子孙动而制之，此医可请，手到成功。 | 自己占也好替别人占也好，鬼是犹疑不定的神；如果它持世、或在卦中发动，得到子孙发动来制服它，这个医生可以请，一下手就成功。 | Whether divining for oneself or for another, Pressure is the spirit of doubt; if it holds the Self line or moves in the hexagram, and Output moves and restrains it, this doctor can be called, and his hand will succeed. |
| 若得子孙临应爻者，乃明医也，其故何也？ | 如果子孙临在应爻上，那是高明的医生，为什么呢？ | If Output sits on the Other line, he is a brilliant doctor; why? |
| 子孙乃制鬼之神，非治邪魔之鬼也，乃克去忧神，我无忧也。 | 子孙是制服鬼的神，不是去治邪魔的鬼，而是克掉忧神，我就没有忧愁了。 | Output is the spirit that restrains Pressure, not one that treats evil ghosts; it controls away the spirit of worry, and I have no worry. |

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13232 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13232*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：诸书无不以官为鬼为病，应为医人，子孙为药，理固然也，但此官鬼者，非鬼非病，乃忧神耳。 | 野鹤说：各种书没有不把官当作鬼、当作病，应当作医生，子孙当作药的，道理本来如此，但这个官鬼，不是鬼也不是病，而是忧神。 | The Old Man of Yehe says: all the books take the Pressure line as the ghost and the illness, the Other line as the doctor and Output as the medicine, which is reasonable enough, but this Pressure line is neither ghost nor illness; it is the spirit of worry. |
| 占子病者，父母爱子之心，未病惟恐疾忧，况已病乎？ | 占儿子病的，父母爱儿子的心，没病时都怕他生病担忧，何况已经病了呢？ | In divining a son's illness: the parents' love for their son is such that even before he is ill they fear illness and worry, and how much more when he is already ill? |
| 自占病者，性命在呼吸之间，且一人不起，举室惊惶，谁不忧乎？ | 自己占病的，性命在一呼一吸之间，而且一个人起不来，全家人都惊慌，谁不担忧呢？ | In divining one's own illness: life hangs on a breath, and when one person cannot rise the whole household is alarmed; who would not worry? |
| 故此官鬼者，即一家之忧神也，解此忧者，惟凭子孙爻也。 | 所以这个官鬼，就是一家人的忧神，能解这份忧的，只有靠子孙爻。 | So this Pressure line is the spirit of worry of the whole household, and what can relieve that worry is only the Output line. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13233 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13233*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如：午月甲寅日(旬空：子丑)，曾有一人，病在危笃，医家不治，迎余到宅。 | 比如：午月甲寅日（旬空：子、丑），曾经有一个人，病危重，医生治不了，请我到他家。 | For example: in the Wu month, on a Jiayin day (void: Zi and Chou), there was a man gravely ill whom the doctors could not treat, and I was invited to his house. |
| 见有三十余人，至亲泪眼不干，密友愁容可掬，此非一家之忧乎？ | 看到有三十多个人，至亲的眼泪不干，密友满脸愁容，这难道不是一家人的忧愁吗？ | I saw more than thirty people there, the nearest kin with tears unceasing and close friends with faces full of sorrow; is this not the worry of a whole household? |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13234 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13234*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 午月甲寅日(旬空：子丑)，弟占兄病，得“屯之中孚” | 午月甲寅日（旬空：子、丑），弟弟占哥哥的病，得屯卦变中孚卦。 | In the Wu month, on a Jiayin day (void: Zi and Chou), a younger brother divining his elder brother's illness got Zhun changing into Zhongfu. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13281 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13281*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 余即笑而言曰：“列位放心，今日半夜退灾，明日卯日即起床矣。” | 我就笑着说：“各位放心，今天半夜灾就退了，明天卯日就能起床了。” | I said with a smile: 'Set your minds at rest, gentlemen; the trouble recedes at midnight tonight, and tomorrow, the Mao day, he will be out of bed.' |
| 举家大小一门愁容变喜。 | 全家大小一门的愁容都变成了喜色。 | The whole household, old and young, turned from sorrow to joy. |
| 此卦中之子孙爻者，是药耶？ | 这个卦里的子孙爻，是药吗？ | Is the Output line in this hexagram medicine? |
| 是解忧之神耶？ | 还是解忧的神呢？ | Or is it the spirit that relieves worry? |
| 许即愈者，何也？ | 断定马上就好，是什么缘故？ | Why did I promise recovery at once? |
| 盖此人虽是险症，其实乃近病，子水兄爻值旬空，近病逢空即愈，值半夜子时而不空也。 | 因为这个人病症虽然凶险，其实是近病，子水兄弟爻落旬空，近病遇空就好，到半夜子时就不空了。 | Because though this man's illness was dangerous, it was in fact a recent illness; the Zi Water Peer line falls into Void, a recent illness meeting Void heals, and at the Zi hour at midnight it is no longer Void. |
| 或曰：“近病逢空，何不许冲空之午日，实空之子日？” | 有人说：“近病遇空，为什么不断在冲空的午日、填实空的子日？” | Someone said: 'For a recent illness meeting Void, why did you not fix the Wu day that clashes the Void, or the Zi day that fills it?' |
| 余曰：“因子水兄爻化卯木子孙，世爻又临寅木子孙，而化卯木子孙，次日即是卯日，正一家解忧释疑之时，所以许半夜实空之时也。” | 我说：“因为子水兄弟爻变成卯木子孙，世爻又临着寅木子孙，变出卯木子孙，第二天就是卯日，正是一家人解忧释疑的时候，所以断在半夜填实空的时候。” | I said: 'Because the Zi Water Peer line changes into Mao Wood Output, and the Self line sits on Yin Wood Output and changes into Mao Wood Output, and the next day is the Mao day, exactly the time when the household is relieved of worry and doubt, I fixed the midnight hour when the Void is filled.' |
| 果于子时退灾，次日起床。 | 果然在子时灾退，第二天起了床。 | Indeed the trouble receded at the Zi hour and the next day he got out of bed. |
| 此何尝用药而制鬼耶？ | 这哪里是用药来制鬼呢？ | Was this ever a matter of controlling Pressure with medicine? |

> **打架**:子孙是什么：黄金策旧注、王洪绪都说子孙是医药（LY.ILL.HJC.32）；野鹤说官鬼不是鬼也不是病，是一家人的忧神，子孙不是药，是解忧的喜悦之神，所以「子孙旺动，不药而愈」。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What Output is: the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce and Wang Hongxu both say Output is the medicine (LY.ILL.HJC.32); the Old Man says the Pressure line is neither ghost nor illness but the spirit of worry of the whole household, and that Output is not medicine but the spirit of joy that relieves worry, so that 'with Output strong and moving, one recovers without medicine'.

> **按**:这是野鹤对「子孙为医药」的新解释，后面几条（LY.ILL.ZS.28、ZS.37、ZS.39、ZS.40）都建立在它上面。书中例里近病、子水兄弟爻落旬空，野鹤断半夜子时（空被填实）退灾，次日卯日（子孙日）起床。子孙（福）一词在译文里保留为 Output。
>
> **Note:** This is the Old Man's new explanation of 'Output is the medicine', and several later entries (LY.ILL.ZS.28, ZS.37, ZS.39 and ZS.40) rest on it. In the case the illness is recent and the Zi Water Peer line falls into Void; the Old Man fixed the Zi hour at midnight (when the Void is filled) for the trouble to recede, and the next day, the Mao day (a day of Output), for him to rise. The word for Output as 'fortune' is kept as Output in the translation.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.ZS.28、LY.ILL.ZS.37、LY.ILL.ZS.39

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.29 应作医人不宜破空墓绝：自占或代六亲求医都以应爻为医人；应爻空破墓绝休囚衰弱，或旺相被日月动爻冲克，或动而化鬼、化绝、化回头克，药不见效

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:应爻当作医生，不宜落在破、空、墓、绝上。自己求医、替六亲求医，都拿应爻当医生。应爻如果空、破、入墓、入绝、休囚衰弱，或者旺相却被日月和动爻冲克，或者动了变成鬼、变成绝、变出回头克，药就不见效。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Other line is taken as the doctor and should not be broken, Void, in a tomb or extinct. Whether one seeks a doctor for oneself or for a relation, the Other line is the doctor. If the Other line is Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct, resting, trapped or weak, or is strong but clashed or controlled by the Day, Month or a moving line, or moves and changes into Pressure, into extinction or into a returning control, the medicine has no effect.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13215 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13215*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应作医人，不宜破空墓绝。 | 应爻当作医生，不宜落在破、空、墓、绝上。 | The Other line is taken as the doctor and should not be broken, Void, in a tomb or extinct. |
| 自占求医，代六亲求医，皆以应爻为医人。 | 自己占求医，替六亲求医，都拿应爻当医生。 | Whether one seeks a doctor for oneself or for a relation, the Other line is the doctor. |
| 应爻若空破墓绝、休囚衰弱，或旺相而被日月动爻冲克，或动而化鬼、化绝、化回头克，药不见效。 | 应爻如果空、破、入墓、入绝、休囚衰弱，或者旺相却被日月、动爻冲克，或者动了变成鬼、变成绝、变出回头克，药就不见效。 | If the Other line is Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct, resting, trapped or weak, or is strong but clashed or controlled by the Day, Month or a moving line, or moves and changes into Pressure, into extinction or into a returning control, the medicine has no effect. |

> **按**:这一条是黄金策 LY.ILL.HJC.58（应作医人，空则闲亡而不遇）的野鹤版，只是把「应空」扩成好几种坏的情形。
>
> **Note:** This entry is the Old Man's version of LY.ILL.HJC.58 in the Huangjin Ce (the Other line as the doctor, Void, idle and not to be met), except that he widens 'the Other line Void' into several bad conditions.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.58、LY.ILL.ZS.30

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.30 子孙制鬼最喜旺相生扶：不受刑冲克害、不逢破墓绝空药必见效；子动化鬼化克药不精、化空实空之日有效、化生化旺化进神更灵；古以子动化子药必杂，野鹤说非也，子动化旺子孙是改换药品即比仙丹

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙制服鬼，最喜欢旺相、有生扶。子孙临在世爻、应爻上，或者在卦中发动，也要旺相，不受刑、冲、克、害，不遇破、墓、绝、空，药就一定见效；子孙动了变成鬼、变出克，那药就不精；动了变空，到填实空的那天有效；动了变出生、变旺、变进神，药更灵。古人认为子孙动变子孙，药一定杂乱，不对！子孙动变成旺相的子孙，是另外改换了药品，好比仙丹。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Output restrains Pressure and most wants to be strong and to be generated and supported. If Output sits on the Self or Other line, or moves in the hexagram, it too must be strong, suffer no punishment, clash, control or harm, and meet no break, tomb, extinction or Void, and then the medicine surely takes effect; if Output moves and changes into Pressure or into a control, the medicine is not refined; if it moves and changes into Void, it is effective on the day the Void is filled; if it moves and changes into generation, strength or an Advancing spirit, the medicine is even more effective. The ancients held that Output moving and changing into Output means a mixed medicine, but this is wrong! Output moving and changing into an Output that is strong means that the medicines have been changed, and it is as good as an immortal's elixir.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13216 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13216*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙制鬼，最喜旺相生扶。 | 子孙制服鬼，最喜欢旺相、有生扶。 | Output restrains Pressure and most wants to be strong and to be generated and supported. |
| 子孙临世应，或发动于卦中，亦要旺相，不受刑冲克害，不逢破墓绝空，药必见效； | 子孙临在世爻、应爻上，或者在卦中发动，也要旺相，不受刑、冲、克、害，不遇破、墓、绝、空，药就一定见效； | If Output sits on the Self or Other line, or moves in the hexagram, it too must be strong, suffer no punishment, clash, control or harm, and meet no break, tomb, extinction or Void, and then the medicine surely takes effect; |
| 子动化鬼化克，其药不精； | 子孙动了变成鬼、变出克，那药就不精； | if Output moves and changes into Pressure or into a control, the medicine is not refined; |
| 子动化空，实空之日有效； | 子孙动了变空，到填实空的那天有效； | if Output moves and changes into Void, it is effective on the day the Void is filled; |
| 子动化生、化旺、化进神者，其药更灵。 | 子孙动了变出生、变旺、变进神的，那药更灵。 | if Output moves and changes into generation, into strength or into an Advancing spirit, the medicine is even more effective. |
| 古以子动化子，其药必杂，非也！ | 古人认为子孙动变子孙，那药一定杂乱，不对！ | The ancients held that Output moving and changing into Output means a mixed medicine, but this is wrong! |
| 子动化子孙而旺相者，另改药品，即比仙丹。 | 子孙动变成旺相的子孙，是另外改换了药品，就好比仙丹。 | Output moving and changing into an Output that is strong means that the medicines have been changed, and it is as good as an immortal's elixir. |

> **打架**:「子化子」是好是坏：黄金策旧注（LY.ILL.HJC.65）说子孙重叠、或子孙又变子孙，是医生不精、用药杂；王洪绪读作子孙化进神药有效、化退神与伏吟卦不可服；野鹤直接说「古以子动化子，其药必杂，非也」，变成旺相的子孙是另换药品，好比仙丹。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether 'Output changing into Output' is good or bad: the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.65) says that Output doubled, or Output changing again into Output, means an unskilled doctor and mixed medicines; Wang Hongxu reads it as Output changing into an Advancing spirit making the medicine effective, and a change into a Retreating spirit or a Hidden Chant hexagram meaning it may not be taken; the Old Man says outright that 'the ancients judged Output moving and changing into Output as a surely mixed medicine, and this is wrong', and that a change into a strong Output means the medicine is changed, as good as an elixir.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.65、LY.ILL.ZS.29

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.31 医克用爻近病即愈：自占病应爻克世、应爻克用神是克制我的病；不宜久病和体弱的人，克病也伤身；应爻临兄鬼而克用神或克世爻，不拘久近必遭其害

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:医生克用爻，近病就好。自己占病，应爻克世爻，是克制我的病；应爻克用神，也这样断。但是不适宜久病和体弱的人，因为它不光克掉了病，身体也受到伤害。应爻临着兄弟、官鬼而去克用神，以及克世爻的，不管久病近病，一定受它的害。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The doctor controlling the Subject line: a recent illness heals at once. In divining one's own illness, if the Other line controls the Self line, it restrains my illness; if the Other line controls the Subject, judge the same. It is unsuitable only for one long ill or of weak body, for it controls not only the illness but harms the body too. If the Other line sits on Peer or Pressure and controls the Subject, or controls the Self line, whether the illness is long or recent, harm from it is certain.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13217 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13217*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 医克用爻，近病即愈。 | 医生克用爻，近病就好。 | The doctor controlling the Subject line: a recent illness heals at once. |
| 自占病，应爻克世者，克制我之病也； | 自己占病，应爻克世爻，是克制我的病； | In divining one's own illness, if the Other line controls the Self line, it restrains my illness; |
| 应爻克用神，亦如此断。 | 应爻克用神，也这样断。 | if the Other line controls the Subject, judge the same. |
| 惟不宜乎久病及体弱之人，非独克病，身亦受其伤矣。 | 只是不适宜久病和体弱的人，因为它不光克掉了病，身体也受到它的伤害。 | It is unsuitable only for one long ill or of weak body, for it controls not only the illness but harms the body too. |
| 应爻临兄鬼而克用神，及克世爻者，不拘久病近病，必遭其害。 | 应爻临着兄弟、官鬼而克用神，以及克世爻的，不管久病近病，一定受它的害。 | If the Other line sits on Peer or Pressure and controls the Subject, or controls the Self line, whether the illness is long or recent, harm from it is certain. |

> **按**:这一条讲的是「应爻」克世爻反而是好事：病是克我的，应（医生）去克它。和黄金策病体章里应克世的一般讲法（LY.ILL.HJC.25 的应临鬼刑克世）不同，那里是别人的病缠过来。
>
> **Note:** This entry treats the Other line controlling the Self line as, on the contrary, good: the illness is what controls me, and the Other line (the doctor) comes to control it. It differs from the usual reading of the Other line against the Self line in the chapter on the patient's condition of the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.25, Pressure on the Other line punishing the Self line), where someone else's illness comes to entangle the patient.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.25、LY.ILL.ZS.29

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.32 父爻持世妙药难调：自占病父持世药不见功宜静养；兄持世有子孙动可延医，嫌鬼旺鬼兴主误服药；财爻持世勿误食肥甘；旺相遇生扶良医有觅，休囚逢冲克无药可医

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:父母爻持世，再好的药也难调治。自己占病，父母持世，药不见功效，宜于静养，远离色欲，平息气恼就可以了；兄弟持世，有子孙在卦中发动的，可以请医生，只嫌鬼旺相、鬼兴起，主吃错药；财爻持世，千万不要误吃油腻甘美的东西。旺相又遇到生扶，能找到良医；休囚又遇到冲克，没有药可以医。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With the Resource line holding the Self line, even wonderful medicine is hard to prescribe. In divining one's own illness, with Resource holding the Self line the medicine shows no effect, and it is fitting to rest quietly, keep away from lust and calm one's anger, and that will do; with Peer holding the Self line, if Output moves in the hexagram a doctor may be called, and the only fear is that Pressure is strong and rising, which means taking the wrong medicine; with the Wealth line holding the Self line, take care not to eat rich food by mistake. If strong and generated and supported, a good doctor can be found; if resting or trapped and clashed or controlled, there is no medicine that can treat him.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13218 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13218*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父爻持世，妙药难调。 | 父母爻持世，再好的药也难调治。 | The Resource line holding the Self line: even wonderful medicine is hard to prescribe. |
| 自占病，父持世，药不见功，宜于静养，远色欲，息气恼，可也； | 自己占病，父母持世，药不见功效，宜于静养，远离色欲，平息气恼就可以了； | In divining one's own illness, with Resource holding the Self line the medicine shows no effect, and it is fitting to rest quietly, keep away from lust and calm one's anger, and that will do; |
| 兄持世，有子孙动于卦中者，医可延之，惟嫌鬼旺鬼兴，主误服药饵； | 兄弟持世，有子孙在卦中发动的，可以请医生，只嫌鬼旺相、鬼兴起，主吃错药； | with Peer holding the Self line, if Output moves in the hexagram a doctor may be called, and the only fear is that Pressure is strong and rising, which means taking the wrong medicine; |
| 财爻持世，切勿误食肥甘。 | 财爻持世，千万不要误吃油腻甘美的东西。 | with the Wealth line holding the Self line, take care not to eat rich food by mistake. |
| 旺相遇生扶，良医有觅； | 旺相又遇到生扶，能找到良医； | If strong and generated and supported, a good doctor can be found; |
| 休囚逢冲克，无药可医。 | 休囚又遇到冲克，没有药可以医。 | if resting or trapped and clashed or controlled, there is no medicine that can treat him. |

> **打架**:父母持世怎么办：黄金策旧注（LY.ILL.HJC.63）说父母持世可以许经醮保禳、祈祷之后再服药；野鹤说药不见功，宜静养、远色欲、息气恼。兄弟持世饮食减省见 LY.ILL.HJC.46。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What to do when Resource holds the Self line: the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.63) says one may vow scriptures, offerings and protective rites and take medicine after praying; the Old Man says the medicine shows no effect and one should rest quietly, keep from lust and calm anger. For Peer holding the Self line reducing food, see LY.ILL.HJC.46.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.46、LY.ILL.HJC.63、LY.ILL.ZS.29

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.33 子不代父占药、妻不代夫卜医：子占父宜父爻旺，占药占医又以子孙为用神，父旺伤子，一爻不能两用；妻占夫宜官鬼旺，占医药又以子孙为用神，子旺伤夫，所以不可代占医药

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:儿子不替父亲占药，妻子不替丈夫卜医。儿子占父亲的病，宜父爻旺相；可是占药、占医，又要拿子孙当用神，父爻旺就会伤子孙，一个爻不能同时当两样用。妻子占丈夫的病，宜官鬼旺相；占医占药，也拿子孙当用神，子孙旺又会伤丈夫，所以不可以替他占医药。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A son does not divine medicine for his father, nor a wife a doctor for her husband. A son divining his father's illness wants the Resource line strong; but in divining medicine or a doctor Output is taken as the Subject, and a strong Resource line harms Output, and one line cannot serve two uses. A wife divining her husband's illness wants the Pressure line strong; in divining a doctor or medicine Output is also taken as the Subject, and a strong Output harms the husband, so one may not divine medicine or a doctor on his behalf.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13219 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13219*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子不代父以占药，妻不代夫以卜医。 | 儿子不替父亲占药，妻子不替丈夫卜医。 | A son does not divine medicine for his father, nor a wife a doctor for her husband. |
| 子占父，宜父爻旺相，占药占医，又以子孙为用神，父旺以伤子。 | 儿子占父亲的病，宜父爻旺相；可是占药、占医，又要拿子孙当用神，父爻旺就会伤子孙。 | A son divining his father's illness wants the Resource line strong; but in divining medicine or a doctor Output is taken as the Subject, and a strong Resource line harms Output. |
| 一爻不能两用也； | 一个爻不能同时当两样用； | one line cannot serve two uses; |
| 妻占夫，宜官鬼旺相，占医占药，亦以子孙为用神，子旺又能伤夫，所以不可代占医药也。 | 妻子占丈夫的病，宜官鬼旺相；占医占药，也拿子孙当用神，子孙旺又会伤丈夫，所以不可以替他占医药。 | a wife divining her husband's illness wants the Pressure line strong; in divining a doctor or medicine Output is also taken as the Subject, and a strong Output harms the husband, so one may not divine medicine or a doctor on his behalf. |

> **按**:这一条和 LY.ILL.HJC.32 里王洪绪的限定（占父母、丈夫病不宜子孙发动）是一个道理。但野鹤在医占往治章里自己给儿子占父病的药占过两卦（LY.ILL.ZS.37 的人参、牛黄），用的是子孙，并说「卦无不灵，在人通变」；所以这条是通则，他自己的实占是按情形变通的。
>
> **Note:** This entry gives the same reason as Wang Hongxu's limit in LY.ILL.HJC.32 (in divining a parent's or a husband's illness Output should not move). But in the chapter on the doctor's divination the Old Man himself divined medicine twice for a father's illness on the son's behalf (the ginseng and the bezoar of LY.ILL.ZS.37), used Output, and said 'no hexagram fails to work, it depends on whether one adapts'; so this entry is the general rule and his own practice adapted to the case.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.ZS.37

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.34 延医于子孙之方、治病以应爻为定：子孙旺则往子孙所在方位请（午火请南方医）；子孙休囚空破则看应爻旺相生合用神，往应爻之方请；有一爻独发生用神，往此方请，姓名按五行——水点水、火象火、木草头木旁、土有土、金有金

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:请医生要往子孙所在的方位去请，治病则以应爻来定。卦里子孙爻旺相的，从子孙所在的方位请医生，比如午火子孙，旺相或发动，就请南方的医生。子孙休囚、空、破的，再看应爻旺不旺，如果应爻旺相、又生合用神，就往应爻所在的方位请，比如应爻临申金，名医一定在西南。卦里有一个爻单独发动、而且生用神的，就从这个爻的方位请，并用五行来定医生的姓名：水爻动，姓名带三点水；火爻动，姓名像火；木爻动，带草字头、木字旁；土爻动，姓名里有土；金爻动，姓名里有金。比如占妻子的病，妻爻属水，得申金发动来生它，就请姓钱的医生。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Call the doctor from the direction of Output; for treatment, the Other line decides. If the Output line in the hexagram is strong, call the doctor from the direction of Output; for example, with Wu Fire Output, strong or moving, call a doctor from the south. If Output is resting, trapped, Void or broken, look at whether the Other line is strong; if the Other line is strong and generates or combines with the Subject, call from the direction of the Other line; for example, with the Other line on Shen Metal, a famous doctor is surely in the south-west. If one line in the hexagram moves alone and generates the Subject, call from the direction of that line, and fix the doctor's name by the five phases: a Water line moving, a name with the water radical; Fire, a name resembling fire; Wood, a name with the grass or wood radical; Earth, a name with earth in it; Metal, a name with metal in it. For example, in divining a wife's illness, if the wife's line is Water and Shen Metal moves and generates it, call a doctor with the surname Qian.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13220 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13220*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 延医于子孙之方，治病以应爻为定。 | 请医生要往子孙所在的方位去请，治病则以应爻来定。 | Call the doctor from the direction of Output; for treatment, the Other line decides. |

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13221–13223 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, lines 13221–13223*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦中子孙爻旺者，延医于子孙之方，且如午火子孙，或旺或动，请南方之医是也。 | 卦里子孙爻旺相的，从子孙所在的方位请医生，比如午火子孙，或旺相或发动，就请南方的医生。 | If the Output line in the hexagram is strong, call the doctor from the direction of Output; for example, with Wu Fire Output, strong or moving, call a doctor from the south. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest follow this. |
| 卦中子孙休囚空破者，又看应爻旺否，若得应爻旺相，生合用神，即于应爻之方请之，且如应爻临申金，名医必在西南。 | 卦里子孙休囚、空、破的，再看应爻旺不旺，如果应爻旺相、又生合用神，就往应爻所在的方位去请，比如应爻临申金，名医一定在西南。 | If the Output line in the hexagram is resting, trapped, Void or broken, look at whether the Other line is strong; if the Other line is strong and generates or combines with the Subject, call from the direction of the Other line; for example, with the Other line on Shen Metal, a famous doctor is surely in the south-west. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest follow this. |
| 卦中有一爻独发而生用神者，即以此方而延医，以五行而定名姓。 | 卦里有一个爻单独发动、而且生用神的，就从这个爻的方位请医生，并用五行来定医生的姓名。 | If one line in the hexagram moves alone and generates the Subject, call the doctor from the direction of that line, and fix the doctor's name by the five phases. |
| 如水爻动，点水之姓名； | 比如水爻动，姓名带三点水； | If a Water line moves, a name with the water radical; |
| 火爻动，姓名象火； | 火爻动，姓名像火； | if a Fire line moves, a name resembling fire; |
| 木爻动，草头木旁之名姓； | 木爻动，姓名带草字头、木字旁； | if a Wood line moves, a name with the grass or wood radical; |
| 土爻动，姓名有土； | 土爻动，姓名里有土； | if an Earth line moves, a name with earth in it; |
| 金爻动，姓名有金。 | 金爻动，姓名里有金。 | if a Metal line moves, a name with metal in it. |
| 且如占妻病，妻爻属水，得申金动而相生，请钱姓之医以治之。 | 比如占妻子的病，妻爻属水，得申金发动来生它，就请姓钱的医生来治。 | For example, in divining a wife's illness, if the wife's line is Water and Shen Metal moves and generates it, call a doctor with the surname Qian to treat her. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest follow this. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13127–13129 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, lines 13127–13129*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父爻发动，虽有制服，而鬼又变子，此子须请高明手治之，彼问：何处医人为吉？ | 父母爻发动，虽然有东西制服它，可是鬼又变成子孙，这个孩子要请高明的手来治，他问：哪个方向的医生吉利？ | The Resource line moves; though something restrains it, Pressure changes into Output again, and this boy must be treated by a skilful hand; he asked: from which direction is a doctor lucky? |
| 余曰：古以子孙爻为医人，今既占子病，又不得以子孙爻为医药也。 | 我说：古人拿子孙爻当医生，现在既然占的是儿子的病，就不能再拿子孙爻当医药了。 | I said: the ancients took the Output line as the doctor, but now that the divination is on a son's illness, the Output line cannot also be taken as the medicine. |
| 须再占之，又得“临之师” | 要再占一次，又得临卦变师卦。 | It must be divined again, and he got Lin changing into Shi. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13159 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13159*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 巳火父动克子，北方医人可治，如言请之，至庚子日痘变，又占：得“解之归妹” | 巳火父母动来克子孙，北方的医生可以治，照这话去请，到庚子日痘症起了变化，又占了一次：得解卦变归妹卦。 | Si Fire Resource moves and controls Output, so a doctor from the north can treat him; he called one as told, and on the Gengzi day the pox changed, and he divined again and got Xie changing into Guimei. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13178 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13178*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子日冲动午火子孙，遇寅木生之，此卦与前卦不同，已有生机矣。 | 子日冲动午火子孙，又有寅木生它，这一卦和前一卦不同，已经有生机了。 | The Zi day clashes Wu Fire Output into motion and Yin Wood generates it; this hexagram differs from the former, and there is already a chance of life. |
| 彼曰：今日痘变，意欲延北门一杨姓之医，再占一卦何如？ | 他说：今天痘症起了变化，想请北门一位姓杨的医生，再占一卦怎么样？ | He said: the pox have changed today, and I would like to call a doctor of the surname Yang at the north gate; how about divining once more? |
| 余曰：杨字有木，卦中寅木生火，是此人也，不必再占。 | 我说：杨字有木，卦里寅木生火，就是这个人，不必再占。 | I said: the character Yang has wood in it, and Yin Wood in the hexagram generates Fire; it is this man, and there is no need to divine again. |
| 果此人治之而愈。 | 果然这个人治好了他。 | Indeed this man treated him and he recovered. |
| 此乃教人延医之法也。 | 这就是教人请医生的办法。 | This is the method of teaching how to call a doctor. |

> **按**:书中例是 LY.ILL.ZS.18 里那个孩子的后几个卦：第二卦（临之师）之后，父母爻巳火动克子孙，说北方的医生可治；痘症变化后再占（解之归妹），子日冲动午火子孙、寅木生它，姓杨的医生名字里有木，野鹤说就是他。「古以子孙爻为医人」一句，按上下文「医人」应是「医药」的意思（下一句讲医药），译文按字面。黄金策的对应条目是 LY.ILL.HJC.77（验福德以迎医）。
>
> **Note:** The case is the later hexagrams of the child of LY.ILL.ZS.18: after the second hexagram (Lin into Shi), the Si Fire Resource line moves and controls Output, and a doctor from the north can treat him; after the pox changed he divined again (Xie into Guimei), the Zi day clashed Wu Fire Output into motion and Yin Wood generated it, and the Yang doctor's name has wood in it, so the Old Man said it was he. In the phrase 'the ancients took the Output line as the doctor', 'doctor' is from the context probably meant as 'medicine' (the next words speak of medicine), and the translation follows the letter. The matching entry of the Huangjin Ce is LY.ILL.HJC.77 (examine Output to welcome a doctor).

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.77、LY.ILL.ZS.18、LY.ILL.ZS.29
