## LY.ILL.ZS.36 医生自占往治与否：世爻旺相、子孙财爻持世、世生应爻、世动化进、化回头生，日月动爻临财星子孙生世，皆主手到病除；近病应空或化空、卦逢六冲等宜速往，久病应旺相或生世宜往，此外勿往治

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:医生自己占要不要去治，和替别人占卜差不多：世爻是自己，应爻是病人。世爻旺相，子孙或财爻持世，世爻生应爻，世动变进神、变回头生，日月和动爻临着财星或子孙来生世爻，都主手到病除。近病：应爻值旬空，或动了变空、变回头生、变旺、变退，或日月、动爻生应爻，以及卦逢六冲、卦变六冲的，都该赶快去救，别让别人先到而成了功。久病：应爻旺相，或遇到日月、动爻来生，或动了变生、变旺，应爻临子孙、临财爻，动而生世爻的，虽然是久病，我一定能除根。除了这些，都不要去治，白白损了名声。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A doctor divining for himself whether to go and treat is much like divining for another: the Self line is oneself and the Other line is the patient. If the Self line is strong, Output or Wealth holds the Self line, the Self line generates the Other line, the Self line moves and changes into an Advancing spirit or into a returning generation, or the Day, Month or a moving line sits on the Wealth star or Output and generates the Self line, all mean that the hand arrives and the illness is gone. For a recent illness: if the Other line falls into Void, or moves and changes into Void, a returning generation, strength or retreat, or the Day, Month or a moving line generates the Other line, or the hexagram is Six-Clash or changes into Six-Clash, one should hurry to the rescue and not let others arrive first and succeed. For a long illness: if the Other line is strong, or is generated by the Day, Month or a moving line, or moves and changes into generation or strength, or sits on Output or Wealth, or moves and generates the Self line, then though the illness is long I shall surely remove the root. Apart from these, do not go to treat, for it only harms one's reputation.

**《增删卜易》正文(未标说话人)** — 《增删卜易》第 13231 行 · *Zengshan Buyi, main text (speaker not marked); Zengshan Buyi, line 13231*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 大抵与医卜相仿，世为己，应为病人，若得世爻旺相、子孙财爻持世、世生应爻、世动化进、化回头生、日月动爻临财星子孙而生世者，皆主手到病除。 | 大体上和医生自己占卜相仿：世爻是自己，应爻是病人；如果世爻旺相、子孙或财爻持世、世爻生应爻、世动变进神、变回头生、日月和动爻临着财星或子孙来生世爻，都主手到病除。 | In general it is like a doctor's own divination: the Self line is oneself and the Other line is the patient; if the Self line is strong, Output or Wealth holds the Self line, the Self line generates the Other line, the Self line moves and changes into an Advancing spirit or into a returning generation, or the Day, Month or a moving line sits on the Wealth star or Output and generates the Self line, all mean that the hand arrives and the illness is gone. |
| 近病者，应值旬空，或动而化空、化回头生、化旺、化退，或日月动爻生应，及卦逢六冲、卦变六冲者，皆宜速往救，莫让他人先到以成功也。 | 近病：应爻值旬空，或者动了变空、变回头生、变旺、变退，或者日月、动爻生应爻，以及卦逢六冲、卦变六冲的，都该赶快去救，别让别人先到而成了功。 | For a recent illness: if the Other line falls into Void, or moves and changes into Void, a returning generation, strength or retreat, or the Day, Month or a moving line generates the Other line, or the hexagram is Six-Clash or changes into Six-Clash, one should hurry to the rescue and not let others arrive first and succeed. |
| 久病者，应爻旺相，或逢日月动爻相生，或动而化生化旺、应临子孙、应临财爻、动而生世，虽然久病，我必除根。 | 久病：应爻旺相，或者遇到日月、动爻来生，或者动了变生、变旺，应爻临子孙、临财爻，动而生世爻，虽然是久病，我一定能除根。 | For a long illness: if the Other line is strong, or is generated by the Day, Month or a moving line, or moves and changes into generation or strength, or sits on Output or Wealth, or moves and generates the Self line, then though the illness is long I shall surely remove the root. |
| 除此之外，皆勿往治，徒损盛名。 | 除了这些，都不要去治，白白损了名声。 | Apart from these, do not go to treat, for it only harms one's reputation. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13312–13313 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, lines 13312–13313*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又如一日，时值初更，叩门入曰：“家小主有病，相迎占卜。” | 又有一天，正值初更，有人敲门进来说：“我家小主人有病，来接您去占卜。” | Another day, at the first watch, someone knocked at the door and came in saying: 'Our young master is ill; we have come to fetch you to divine.' |
| 余问得病源，自山东方回，顷刻得病。 | 我问起病因，说是刚从山东回来，顷刻之间得的病。 | I asked the cause of the illness and was told that he had just come back from Shandong and fell ill in an instant. |
| 余即自占一卦。 | 我就自己占了一卦。 | I at once divined a hexagram for myself. |
| 于未月壬子日(旬空：寅卯)，占此行有益否？ | 在未月壬子日（旬空：寅、卯），占这一趟去有没有好处？ | In the Wei month, on a Renzi day (void: Yin and Mao), I divined whether this trip would do good. |
| 得“节之比” | 得节卦变比卦。 | I got Jie changing into Bi. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13339 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13339*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 巳火财爻持世，子动生世，子孙乃喜悦之神，此行有益。 | 巳火财爻持世，子孙动来生世爻，子孙是喜悦的神，这一趟去有好处。 | Si Fire Wealth holds the Self line and Output moves to generate it; Output is the spirit of joy, so this trip will do good. |
| 及到达伊家，值医检药，其父卜之。 | 等到了他家，正碰上医生在检点药，孩子的父亲占了一卦。 | When I reached his house, the doctor was just checking the medicines, and the boy's father divined. |
| 未月壬子日(旬空：寅卯)，占子病，得“解之坎” | 未月壬子日（旬空：寅、卯），占儿子的病，得解卦变坎卦。 | In the Wei month, on a Renzi day (void: Yin and Mao), he divined his son's illness and got Xie changing into Kan. |

> **按**:这一条是医生自己占「要不要去治」的规则；野鹤的书中例是他被请去看一个刚从山东回来就得病的孩子，出门前自占「此行有益否」，得财爻持世、子孙动生世，断此行有益。这个孩子的病和用药，接着在 LY.ILL.ZS.37 里展开。
>
> **Note:** This entry is the rule for a doctor's divination on whether to go and treat; the Old Man's case is of being called to a child who fell ill on coming back from Shandong, and who, before setting out, divined 'will this trip do good' and got Wealth holding the Self line with Output moving to generate it, and judged that the trip would do good. The child's illness and medicine are carried on in LY.ILL.ZS.37.

相关:LY.ILL.ZS.37

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.37 占药能不能服以子孙为用神：医生治危症想用某药、行舟远行无处寻医得一奇方、半夜来不及请医，都要占；子孙持世或发动服之立可解忧；子孙不拘五行，旺或动即喜悦之神，不可因金临子孙用针、火属子孙用炙（例：热极用附子肉桂、人参、牛黄）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占药能不能服，拿子孙当用神。医生治垂危的病、想用某味药，可以起死回生，又怕一有闪失；泛海行船、长途旅行没有地方找医生、有人传来奇方；半夜来不及请医、手边的古方或家里的药散确实对这个病却不敢擅用，都要占。只要子孙持世或发动，服了立刻可以解忧，必定见效。子孙不管是金木水火土哪一行，旺相或发动就是喜悦的神；不可以又因为金临子孙就用针、火属子孙就用灸。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To divine whether a medicine may be taken, take Output as the Subject. It should be divined when a doctor treating a dying patient wants a certain medicine that may bring him back from death, and fears one failure; when one is crossing the sea or on a long journey with nowhere to find a doctor and someone passes on a rare prescription; when in the depth of night there is no time to call a doctor and the old prescriptions or powders kept at home truly fit the illness but one does not dare use them on one's own authority. If Output holds the Self line or moves, taking it at once relieves the worry, and it will surely work. Whatever phase Output is, metal, wood, water, fire or earth, so long as it is strong or moving it is the spirit of joy; one must not take Metal on Output to mean the needle, or Fire as Output to mean moxibustion.

**李我平(楚江李坦,《增删卜易》的鉴定者)** — 《增删卜易》第 13451–13454 行 · *Li Woping (Li Tan of Chujiang, who appraised the Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, lines 13451–13454*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 李我平曰：凡卜药，亦有当卜之道，或医家治临危之症，欲投此药，可以起死回生，惟恐一失，关系不小。 | 李我平说：凡是占药，也有该占的时候，比如医生治疗垂危的病，想用这味药，能起死回生，只怕一有闪失，关系不小。 | Li Woping says: in divining medicine there are times when one ought to divine, as when a doctor treating a dying patient wants to give a certain medicine that might bring him back from death, and fears only one failure, which would matter greatly. |
| 得子孙动者，必喜悦也，即可用之，用而必效。 | 得到子孙发动的，一定是喜悦，就可以用它，用了必定见效。 | If Output moves, there is surely joy, and it may be used, and used it will surely be effective. |
| 或泛海行舟，或长途穹旅，无处寻医，有人传一奇方，占得子孙动者，其方必效。 | 或者泛海行船，或者长途旅行，没有地方找医生，有人传来一个奇方，占得子孙发动的，这个方子一定见效。 | Or crossing the sea in a boat, or on a long journey with nowhere to find a doctor, someone passes on a rare prescription; if the divination shows Output moving, the prescription will surely work. |
| 或半夜更深，延医不及，检点古方及家藏药散，实对此症，不敢擅用，不可不占，皆以子孙为用神，但得子孙持世，子孙发动，服之立可解忧。 | 或者半夜更深，来不及请医生，翻检古方和家里藏的药散，确实对这个病，却不敢擅自用，就不能不占，都拿子孙当用神，只要子孙持世、子孙发动，吃下去马上可以解忧。 | Or in the depth of night with no time to call a doctor, one checks old prescriptions and the powders kept at home, which truly fit this illness, but does not dare use them on one's own authority; then one must divine, and in all cases Output is the Subject: if only Output holds the Self line and Output moves, taking it at once relieves the worry. |
| 此诸占之子孙者，不拘金木水火土也，或旺或动，即为喜悦之神，盖用此方，见效而喜，不效，何喜之有？ | 在这些占里，子孙不管是金木水火土哪一行，只要旺相或发动，就是喜悦的神，因为用这个方子，见效才喜，不见效，哪里有什么喜？ | In all these divinations Output, whatever its phase of metal, wood, water, fire or earth, is the spirit of joy so long as it is strong or moving, for one takes the prescription in order to be glad that it works, and if it does not work, what gladness is there? |
| 切不可又以金临子孙而用针，火属子孙而用炙，反致错误。 | 千万不可又因为金临子孙就用针，火属子孙就用灸，反而弄错。 | One must on no account take Metal on Output to mean the needle, or Fire as Output to mean moxibustion, which only leads to error. |

**觉子(李文辉,《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13451 行 · *Juezi (Li Wenhui, Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13451*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 觉子曰：此卦酉金兄动以生子孙，服此热药而病愈者，若使前贤见之，又以酉金兄爻而为药矣。 | 觉子说：这一卦酉金兄弟动来生子孙，服了这热药而病好了，要是让前辈贤人看到，又会把酉金兄弟爻当成药了。 | Juezi says: in this hexagram You Metal Peer moves to generate Output, and the patient recovered by taking this hot medicine; had the worthies of old seen it, they would again have taken the You Metal Peer line as the medicine. |

| 彼曰：“从无病根，便自山东一路而来，亦无病起。 | 他说：“从来没有病根，从山东一路回来，也没有生病。 | He said: 'He has never had a root of illness, and coming all the way from Shandong he did not fall ill either. |

| 随以家人问途中何如，家人言曰：“到家热极，移床于此有风之处，再用大冰二块，安于凉床之下，命侄女掌扇。 | 接着我问家里人路上怎么样，家人说：“到家热得厉害，把床移到这个有风的地方，又用两块大冰放在凉床下面，叫侄女摇扇。 | Then I asked the household about the journey, and they said: 'He came home extremely hot; we moved the bed to this windy place, put two large blocks of ice under the cool bed and told his niece to fan him. |
| 一觉睡熟，忽然醒来呼唤几声，即不言矣。” | 他一觉睡得很熟，忽然醒来叫了几声，就不说话了。” | He fell into a deep sleep, woke suddenly and called out a few times, and then said no more.' |

| 再命伊父占之： | 再叫他父亲占一次： | I then had his father divine again: |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13501 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13501*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |

| 余曰：“灌服此丸，可保立愈。” | 我说：“灌服这颗丸药，可以保证马上好。” | I said: 'Give him this pill by pouring it down, and he will surely recover at once.' |
| 服之果醒。 | 服了果然醒过来。 | He took it and did come to. |
| 盖喜子孙持世也。 | 是因为喜见子孙持世。 | It was because Output holding the Self line is what is wanted. |
| 或曰：同是子占父药，一卦看父爻，一卦看子孙，其故何也？ | 有人问：同样是儿子占父亲的药，一卦看父爻，一卦看子孙，是什么缘故？ | Someone asked: both are a son divining medicine for his father, yet in one hexagram you look at the Resource line and in the other at Output; why? |
| 余曰：前卦父爻发动，神兆基于动，故以父母断之； | 我说：前一卦父爻发动，神的兆示在于动，所以按父母爻来断； | I said: in the former hexagram the Resource line moves, and the spirit's omen is based on the movement, so I judged by the Resource line; |
| 后卦子孙持世值日辰，子孙乃喜悦之神，父病即安，则喜悦矣。 | 后一卦子孙持世又值日辰，子孙是喜悦的神，父亲的病马上安了，就喜悦了。 | in the latter hexagram Output holds the Self line and rules the Day, and Output is the spirit of joy; when the father's illness is at once at peace, there is joy. |
| 故用子孙也，卦无不灵，在人通变。 | 所以用子孙。卦没有不灵验的，在于人会不会变通。 | So I used Output. No hexagram fails to work; it depends on whether one adapts. |

> **打架**:占药看什么：黄金策旧注、王洪绪讲用药（LY.ILL.HJC.76 起）是看官鬼的五行、子孙的五行来定药；李我平、野鹤说占某味药能不能服，拿子孙当用神，子孙持世或发动服了就见效，不拘五行。同样是儿子占父亲的药，一卦看父爻（人参）、一卦看子孙（牛黄），野鹤说要看哪一个动、哪一个持世，在人通变。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What to look at in divining medicine: the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce and Wang Hongxu fix the medicine (from LY.ILL.HJC.76 on) by the phase of the Pressure line and of Output; Li Woping and the Old Man say that in divining whether a given medicine may be taken, Output is the Subject, and if Output holds the Self line or moves it works when taken, whatever its phase. Of two hexagrams of a son divining medicine for his father, one looks at the Resource line (ginseng) and the other at Output (bezoar), and the Old Man says one must see which one moves and which holds the Self line, and adapt.

> **按**:书中例是 LY.ILL.ZS.36 里那个孩子：在山东受暑回来，卧冰吹风后满床乱滚，医生已经下了凉药。野鹤连占几卦，子孙动、酉金兄弟动来生它，断定要用附子、肉桂这样的大热药，服后果然好了。觉子说，前辈看到酉金兄动，又会把兄爻当成药，这是不对的。「使姻掌扇」的「姻」按上下文大概是「侄女」之误，译文按字面。人参、牛黄两例讲的是同一个道理：占药要看的是喜悦之神。
>
> **Note:** The case is the child of LY.ILL.ZS.36, who had taken the heat in Shandong and after lying on ice in the wind rolled about the bed, the doctor having already given cold medicine. The Old Man divined several hexagrams in a row, found Output moving and You Metal Peer moving to generate it, judged that a very hot medicine such as aconite and cinnamon bark was needed, and he recovered after taking it. Juezi says that the worthies of old would again have taken the moving Peer line as the medicine, which is wrong. The 'in-law' of 'an in-law made to fan him' is from the context probably a slip for 'niece', and the translation follows the letter. The ginseng and bezoar cases speak of the same principle: what one looks at in divining medicine is the spirit of joy.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.76、LY.ILL.ZS.27、LY.ILL.ZS.33、LY.ILL.ZS.38、LY.ILL.ZS.40

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.38 子孙持世也要不受克：前面各篇都说子孙持世病即愈，野鹤补一条——必要子孙不受克才行（例：杨梅疮占医人，子孙持世应为医人，但初爻巳火动克申金，巳日添灾）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:前面几篇都说子孙持世，病就马上好，但一定要子孙不受克才行，后来的人不可以不知道。比如有人生了杨梅疮，占这位医生好不好：虽然是子孙持世、应爻是医生，但初爻巳火发动克申金，又有卯木生火，世爻虽旺，巳日一定添灾；他不听，还是请了这位医生，卯日、辰日服药还平安，巳日忽然病症变了，全身疼痛难以忍受。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The earlier passages all say that with Output holding the Self line the illness heals at once, but it is so only when Output is not controlled, which later people must know. For example, a man with syphilitic sores divined whether a certain doctor was a good one: though Output held the Self line and the Other line was the doctor, the first line, Si Fire, moved and controlled Shen Metal, with Mao Wood also generating Fire, so that though the Self line was strong there would be added trouble on the Si day; he did not listen and called the doctor, and on the Mao and Chen days the medicine kept him safe, but on the Si day the symptoms suddenly changed and pain all over the body was unbearable.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13510 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13510*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：前篇俱言子孙持世，其病即愈，然必要子孙不受克方可，后贤不可不知。 | 野鹤说：前面几篇都说子孙持世，病就马上好，但一定要子孙不受克才行，后来的贤人不可以不知道。 | The Old Man of Yehe says: the earlier passages all say that with Output holding the Self line the illness heals at once, but it is so only when Output is not controlled; later worthies must not fail to know this. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13502 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13502*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又如：有生杨梅疮者，于申月癸卯日(旬空：辰巳)，占医人好否？ | 又如：有人生了杨梅疮，在申月癸卯日（旬空：辰、巳），占这位医生好不好？ | Another: a man with syphilitic sores, in the Shen month on a Guimao day (void: Chen and Si), divined whether the doctor was a good one. |
| 得“履之否” | 得履卦变否卦。 | He got Lu changing into Pi. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13509 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13509*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 断曰：此医不可用之。 | 断语：这个医生不能用。 | Judgment: this doctor must not be used. |
| 虽系子孙持世，应为医人，但不宜初爻巳火动克申金，又有卯木生火，世爻虽旺，巳日一定添灾。 | 虽然是子孙持世，应爻是医生，但初爻巳火发动克申金不合适，又有卯木生火，世爻虽然旺，巳日一定添灾。 | Though Output holds the Self line and the Other line is the doctor, it is not fitting that the first line, Si Fire, moves and controls Shen Metal, with Mao Wood also generating Fire; though the Self line is strong, on the Si day there will surely be added trouble. |
| 不听，竟延此医治之。 | 他不听，还是请这位医生来治。 | He did not listen and called this doctor to treat him. |
| 卯辰日服药，还保平安，巳日忽然变症，遍身疼痛难当。 | 卯日、辰日服药，还平安，巳日忽然病症变了，全身疼痛难以忍受。 | On the Mao and Chen days he took the medicine and was still safe, but on the Si day the symptoms suddenly changed and pain all over the body was unbearable. |
| 病人着急而问余曰：可伤命否？ | 病人着急，问我说：会伤到性命吗？ | The patient was anxious and asked me: will it endanger my life? |
| 余曰：世值月建，如何伤命？ | 我说：世爻值月建，怎么会伤命？ | I said: the Self line rules the Month; how could it endanger life? |
| 速服解药，过此巳午日则止疼矣。 | 赶快服解药，过了巳日、午日就不疼了。 | Take the antidote quickly, and once the Si and Wu days are past the pain will stop. |
| 即服解药，至申日，其病如常。 | 立即服了解药，到申日，他的病像平常一样。 | He took the antidote at once, and by the Shen day his illness was as usual. |

> **按**:这一条是对 LY.ILL.ZS.10（子孙持世不药而痊）的补充限制：子孙持世还要看它有没有被克。书中例是占医生，不是占病：子孙持世、应是医人，但初爻巳火动来克申金，野鹤据此断这位医生的药会在巳日添灾。
>
> **Note:** This entry is a limiting supplement to LY.ILL.ZS.10 (Output holding the Self line heals without medicine): one must also see whether Output is controlled. The case divines a doctor and not an illness: Output holds the Self line and the Other line is the doctor, but the Si Fire at the first line moves to control Shen Metal, and from this the Old Man judged that this doctor's medicine would bring added trouble on the Si day.

相关:LY.ILL.ZS.10、LY.ILL.ZS.30、LY.ILL.ZS.37

## LY.ILL.ZS.40 子孙不是针灸之药，火鬼忌热水鬼忌寒是拘泥：子孙旺动则不药自愈，前贤以金生子孙用针、火持子孙用炙是错；诸书说火鬼旺宜大寒药、水鬼旺宜大热药，李我平举土用神、妻占夫二例驳之，说胶柱决之坑陷人命

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙旺相又发动的，不吃药就好，并不是教人拿这个子孙当治病的药；前辈贤人常常看到子孙旺动，占病病好、占医医良，就把子孙爻当成治病的药，金生子孙就断用针，火持子孙就断用灸，不知道子孙旺相病自己就好了，哪里用得着针灸，子孙落空被破，针灸又有什么益处。各种书又说火鬼忌热、水鬼忌寒，水鬼在生旺之地要用大热的药，火鬼旺相宜用大寒的药去攻；李我平不知道他们从哪里看出来的：假使用神属土、儿子占父亲的病，父爻临土，火鬼发动是生用爻的元神，怎么能用大寒的药去治元神？妻子占丈夫的病，鬼是用神，丈夫临水鬼，又怎么能用大热的药去攻用神？死板地照大寒大热去断，坑害了不知多少性命。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Where Output is strong and moving, one recovers without medicine, and this does not teach us to take that Output as the medicine that treats the illness; the worthies of old often saw Output strong and moving, found that in divining illness the illness healed and in divining a doctor the doctor was good, and so took the Output line as the medicine, judging the needle where Metal generates Output and moxibustion where Fire holds Output, without realising that if Output is strong the illness heals of itself, so what need is there of needle and moxa, and if Output is Void or broken what good would they do. The books also say that a Fire Pressure fears heat and a Water Pressure fears cold, that a Water Pressure in a place of birth or strength must be treated with very hot medicine and a strong Fire Pressure attacked with very cold; Li Woping does not know where they saw this: suppose the Subject is Earth, as when a son divines his father's illness and the Resource line sits on Earth, a moving Fire Pressure is the Support that generates the Subject line, and how could one treat the Support with very cold medicine? When a wife divines her husband's illness, Pressure is the Subject, and if the husband sits on a Water Pressure, how could one attack the Subject with very hot medicine? To judge rigidly by very cold and very hot has sunk who knows how many lives in the pit.

**李我平(楚江李坦,《增删卜易》的鉴定者)** — 《增删卜易》第 13514 行 · *Li Woping (Li Tan of Chujiang, who appraised the Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13514*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙旺而动者，不药而愈，非教人以此子孙为治病之药也，往往前贤见此子孙旺动，占病病愈，占医医良，遂以此子孙爻为治病之药。 | 子孙旺相又发动的，不吃药就好，并不是教人拿这个子孙当治病的药；往往前辈贤人看到这个子孙旺相发动，占病病好，占医医生高明，就拿这个子孙爻当成治病的药了。 | Where Output is strong and moving, one recovers without medicine, and this does not teach us to take that Output as the medicine that treats the illness; the worthies of old often saw Output strong and moving, found that in divining illness the illness healed and in divining a doctor the doctor was good, and so took that Output line as the medicine that treats the illness. |
| 金生子孙者，断用针； | 金生子孙的，就断用针； | Where Metal generates Output, they judged to use the needle; |
| 火持子孙者，断用炙。 | 火持子孙的，就断用灸。 | where Fire holds Output, to use moxibustion. |
| 殊不知子孙若旺，其病自痊，何用针灸？ | 殊不知子孙如果旺相，那病自己就好了，哪里用得着针灸？ | They did not realise that if Output is strong the illness heals of itself, so what need is there of needle and moxa? |
| 子孙若值空破，用针灸而何益？ | 子孙如果落空、被破，用针灸又有什么益处？ | and if Output is Void or broken, what good would needle and moxa do? |

**李我平(楚江李坦,《增删卜易》的鉴定者)** — 《增删卜易》第 13515 行 · *Li Woping (Li Tan of Chujiang, who appraised the Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13515*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 诸书又皆曰：“火鬼忌热，水鬼忌寒，水鬼若居生旺之地，须大热之药以治之； | 各种书又都说：“火鬼忌热，水鬼忌寒，水鬼如果在生旺的地方，要用大热的药来治； | The books all say again: 'A Fire Pressure fears heat and a Water Pressure fears cold; if a Water Pressure is in a place of birth or strength, it must be treated with very hot medicine; |
| 火鬼旺者，宜大寒之剂以攻之。” | 火鬼旺相的，宜用大寒的药去攻它。” | if a Fire Pressure is strong, attack it with very cold medicine.' |
| 余不知从何所见。 | 我不知道他们是从哪里看出来的。 | I do not know from where they saw this. |
| 假使用神属土，及子占父病，父爻临土，若得火鬼发动，乃生用爻之元神也，岂可用大寒之药而治元神耶？ | 假使用神属土，比如儿子占父亲的病，父爻临土，如果火鬼发动，那是生用爻的元神，怎么可以用大寒的药去治元神呢？ | Suppose the Subject is Earth, as when a son divines his father's illness and the Resource line sits on Earth; if a Fire Pressure moves, it is the Support that generates the Subject line, and how could one treat the Support with very cold medicine? |
| 又如妻占夫病，鬼为用神，假便夫临水鬼，又可以大热之药以攻夫爻之用神耶？ | 又如妻子占丈夫的病，鬼是用神，假使丈夫临水鬼，又可以用大热的药去攻丈夫爻的用神吗？ | Again, when a wife divines her husband's illness, Pressure is the Subject; suppose the husband sits on a Water Pressure, may one attack the husband line's Subject with very hot medicine? |
| 每见穷乡僻壤，无处觅医，只谓《周易》乃大圣大贤之书，遵之必灵，而卜者以此大寒大热之药胶柱决之，不知坑陷如许之命，言及发指。 | 常见穷乡僻壤，没有地方找医生，只当《周易》是大圣大贤的书，照着它办必定灵验，而占卜的人拿这大寒大热的药死板地去决断，不知道坑害了多少条命，说起来令人发指。 | I have often seen in poor and remote places where there is nowhere to find a doctor, people who think the Zhouyi a book of great sages and worthies that must prove true if followed, and diviners who settle the matter rigidly with these very cold and very hot medicines, not knowing how many lives they have sunk into the pit; it makes one's hair stand on end to speak of it. |
| 此书一出，而寒热之药不敢轻用，从此泉下无怨鬼矣。 | 这部书一出来，寒热的药就不敢轻易用了，从此黄泉之下没有含冤的鬼了。 | Once this book is out, no one will dare to use cold or hot medicines lightly, and from then on there will be no wronged ghosts under the springs. |

> **打架**:用药看什么：黄金策旧注、王洪绪（LY.ILL.HJC.76、HJC.81、HJC.84）按官鬼的五行定寒热（火鬼旺用大寒、水鬼旺用大热），按子孙的五行定针灸（子孙属金用刀针、属火用灸）；李我平、野鹤说这是拘泥：子孙旺动本来就不药而愈，不是针灸之药；火鬼可能是用神的元神，不能拿大寒药去攻；死板照大寒大热去断，是坑人性命。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What to look at in fixing the medicine: the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce and Wang Hongxu (LY.ILL.HJC.76, HJC.81, HJC.84) fix cold or hot by the phase of the Pressure line (a strong Fire Pressure very cold, a strong Water Pressure very hot) and needle or moxa by the phase of Output (Metal Output knife and needle, Fire Output moxa); Li Woping and the Old Man call this rigid: Output strong and moving means recovery without medicine and is not the medicine of needle and moxa, a Fire Pressure may be the Support of the Subject and cannot be attacked with very cold medicine, and to judge rigidly by very cold and very hot sinks lives.

> **按**:这是整个疾病域里两派分歧最尖锐的一条。李我平末尾说「此书一出，而寒热之药不敢轻用」，是说《增删卜易》出来以后，占卜的人不该再拿五行定寒热。「炙」「灸」在书里写法不一，都指艾灸。「元神」的用法见 LY.ILL.ZS.08。
>
> **Note:** This is the sharpest split between the two schools in the whole illness domain. Li Woping's closing 'once this book is out, no one will dare to use cold or hot medicines lightly' means that after the Zengshan Buyi diviners should no longer fix cold and hot by the five phases. 'Zhi' and 'jiu' are written differently in the book and both mean moxibustion. For the use of the 'Support' see LY.ILL.ZS.08. The 'Qian note' at the end of the chapter is a modern addition and is not quoted here.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.76、LY.ILL.HJC.81、LY.ILL.HJC.84、LY.ILL.ZS.37
